In experiments the decision-aware compressor flips the decision less often than representation-aware compression, for both the linear criterion and a non-linear proxy read-out.

</details>


### [172] [Operationalising Relative Causal Knowledge: Backbone Identifiability from Private Reports on a Shared Outcome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10664)
*Fabrizio Russo,Mark Somers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究因果知识相对性（RCK）框架下的先验识别问题：多个拥有不同结构因果模型的智能体，何时能通过共享的干预一致性抽象（backbone）来确定地交换因果知识。结果表明，在基本的双智能体共同效应场景中，局部因果边际无法唯一确定backbone，存在无穷多个联合干预核产生相同的局部报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RCK框架预设了一个共享的骨架结构来支持智能体间的因果知识传输，但此前未回答这个骨架能否由智能体各自的私有因果知识所确定。该先验识别问题是整个知识传输机制成立的前提。

Method: 以双智能体共同效应结构为基本案例（两个私有原因影响一个共享结果，每个智能体仅识别与自己视角相关的单原因因果边际），在标准兼容性、非退化性和局部重叠假设下，分析局部因果边际对联合干预核的识别能力。通过可加分离性约束以及沟通因果识别响应函数来探索条件恢复方案。

Result: （1）当仅交换局部因果边际时，无法识别唯一的backbone——存在无穷多个联合干预核产生完全相同的局部报告但在联合干预上不一致；（2）可加分离性可以消除隐藏的交互自由度，但仅靠观测残差摘要仍不足够；（3）当智能体沟通因果识别的响应函数时，识别成为可能。

Conclusion: 因果知识的相对性本质上首先是一个沟通问题，然后才是政策组合问题。以教育增值（value-added）案例说明，智能体必须超越局部因果边际的交换，主动沟通其因果识别的响应函数，才能实现可靠的联合知识传输。

Abstract: The Relativity of Causal Knowledge (RCK) explains how a network of agents with different structural causal models can exchange causal knowledge through a shared interventionally consistent abstraction, or backbone. We ask the prior identification question that this transport mechanism presupposes: when is that backbone determined by the agents' private causal knowledge? In the basic two-agent common-effect case, two private causes influence one shared outcome and each agent identifies only the single-cause causal marginal relevant to its own perspective. We show that, under standard compatibility, non-degeneracy, and local overlap assumptions, those local causal marginals do not identify a unique backbone. Infinitely many joint intervention kernels can induce exactly the same private reports while disagreeing on joint interventions. We then give a conditional recovery result. Additive separability removes the hidden interaction degree of freedom, but observational residual summaries remain insufficient. Identification becomes possible when agents communicate causally identified response functions. An education value-added example illustrates why this is first a communication problem, and only then a policy-composition problem.

</details>


### [173] [VERDICT: Training-Free Step-Wise Verification of Multimodal Reasoning via Disagreement-Aware Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10665)
*Rohit Sinha,Kunal Tilaganji,Tanuja Ganu,Nagarajan Natarajan,Amit Sharma,Vineeth Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VERDICT，一种无需训练的跨模态验证方法，通过形式化多验证器间的分歧信号来实现逐步推理验证，在六个基准上最高提升+5.95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型常生成包含细微错误的推理链，导致最终答案不正确。现有验证方法要么需要昂贵的标注监督且跨任务性能不一致，要么简单聚合多源分数，忽略了分数不一致本身蕴含的重要信息。

Method: 将验证问题形式化为多个异构冻结验证器间的耦合打分问题，可解释为具有唯一闭式均衡的协调博弈——一致信号表示有效步骤，分歧揭示不稳定。VERDICT通过闭式解计算共识分数，实现分歧感知过滤和稳定性感知的推理步骤排序。

Result: 在六个基准上，VERDICT相对于基础模型最高提升+5.95%，与需要大量监督的领域特定评判器表现相当，证明跨模态一致性可在无需任务特定适配的情况下提供稳健的验证信号。

Conclusion: 跨模态分歧的结构化利用是无需训练、无需领域适配的可靠验证信号来源，VERDICT是首个将跨模态分歧结构显式化并加以利用的无需训练验证器。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models often generate reasoning chains containing subtle errors that lead to incorrect answers. Current verification approaches have notable limitations. Existing approaches either require expensive labelled supervision with inconsistent cross-task performance or aggregate scores from multiple sources by simple aggregations, missing a key insight: when these scores disagree, that disagreement itself carries important information about whether a reasoning step is truly valid or not. We formalise this as a coupled scoring problem among disparate, frozen verifiers, interpretable as a coordination game with a unique closed-form equilibrium where agreement signals valid steps while disagreement reveals instability. Towards this end, we propose a training-free domain-agnostic step-wise verification approach we call VERDICT: VERification via Disagreement-Informed Coupled Thresholding. To our knowledge, VERDICT is the first training-free verifier that makes the structure of cross-modal disagreement explicit and actionable. It computes consensus scores through a closed-form solution, enabling both disagreement-aware filtering and stability-conscious ranking of reasoning steps. Evaluated across six benchmarks, \method consistently improves over the base model by up to +5.95%, and performs competitively with domain-specific critics that demand extensive supervision, demonstrating that cross-modal agreement provides robust verification signals without task-specific adaptation and Training-Free Verification

</details>


### [174] [FITTER: Vocabulary-Agnostic Cross-Domain Inference on Temporal Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10668)
*Jiaxin Pan,Mojtaba Nayyeri,Osama Mohammed,Daniel Hernandez,Rongchuan Zhang,Cheng Cheng,Steffen Staab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FITTER是首个全归纳（fully-inductive）结构化的时序知识图谱链接预测模型，支持跨域迁移：推理图中可包含训练时完全未见过的实体、关系名和时间戳。FITTER通过相对时间顺序编码和消息传递融合局部与全局时序上下文，生成与词汇无关的嵌入，并在六个不同领域、粒度和时间跨度的基准上优于归纳基线，无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序知识图谱补全方法假设待推理的实体、关系名和时间戳在训练时已知，这限制每个模型只能适用于单一图谱和词汇表，无法支持跨图、跨域的迁移推理。作者旨在解决这一封闭世界假设带来的局限性，提出支持跨域迁移的全归纳模型。

Method: FITTER的核心方法包括：1）每个谓词通过其与其他谓词的交互模式以及时间的交互模式来表示；2）使用相对时间顺序而非绝对时间顺序进行编码，从而保证时间编码具有时移不变性（time-shift invariant）；3）通过消息传递机制融合局部和全局的时序上下文，生成与词汇无关（vocabulary-agnostic）的嵌入表示。

Result: FITTER在六个不同领域、粒度和时间跨度的时序知识图谱基准上进行了跨域、跨图迁移评估。结果显示FITTER一致性地优于归纳基线方法，且无需重新训练即可完成迁移任务。

Conclusion: 词汇无关的结构化学习方法可以成为语义网中异构知识图谱推理的可行基础。FITTER验证了利用相对时间编码和交互模式表示可实现无需微调的跨域时序知识图谱推理，为语义网中的异构知识图谱推断提供了新方向。

Abstract: Temporal knowledge graphs are central to many uses of the Semantic Web, but existing completion methods assume the entities, relation names, and timestamps to be reasoned about are already known at training time, restricting each model to a single graph and vocabulary. We propose FITTER, the first fully-inductive structural model for temporal knowledge graph link prediction that supports cross-domain transfer: the inference graph may contain entirely unseen entities, relation names, and timestamps drawn from a different domain. FITTER represents each predicate by its interaction patterns with others and time through encodings of relative rather than absolute ordering; message-passing fuses local and global temporal context to produce vocabulary-agnostic embeddings. We prove the temporal encoding is time-shift invariant and evaluate FITTER on cross-domain, cross-graph transfer over six temporal knowledge graph benchmarks of diverse domains, granularities, and time spans. FITTER consistently outperforms inductive baselines without retraining, indicating that vocabulary-agnostic structural learning is a viable foundation for inference over the heterogeneous knowledge graphs of the Semantic Web.

</details>


### [175] [REDAgentBench: Executable Red Teaming and Faithful Measurement of LLM Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10669)
*Zixing Chen,Xingyuan Liu,Jie Zhu,Huaixia Dou,Shuo Jiang,Junhui Li,Lifan Guo,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出REDAgentBench，一个可执行的LLM智能体红队测试与安全测量框架，涵盖5个服务面1661个案例，发现宏平均攻击成功率为65.69%，并揭示了"识别-执行鸿沟"（智能体已识别风险但仍执行违规行为），同时验证了无需训练的策略提醒可将违规降低超70个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体安全评估往往将攻击成功率简化为单一指标，混淆了暴露、执行、观察和裁决等环节，可能将实际违规与证据可见性混为一谈，导致安全测量不忠实、不准确。

Method: REDAgentBench从显式安全约束及智能体系统漏洞中推导攻击用例，在隔离服务沙箱中执行，并通过服务收据和最终状态变化验证有害效果。评估涵盖6个模型、3种智能体框架，共1661个案例覆盖5种服务面，并设计了状态接地诊断队列和匹配回放策略提醒实验。

Result: 宏平均攻击成功率为65.69%；攻击成功率随框架和证据视图不同而变化，评估上下文披露会改变执行行为；近五分之一已确认违规发生在智能体明确陈述相关约束或风险之后，揭示了识别-执行鸿沟；训练无关的策略提醒在匹配回放中将确认违规降低超过70个百分点。

Conclusion: 可执行的评估框架能够提升安全测量的忠实度并识别可操作的干预点。智能体不仅需要在推理层面"知道"安全约束，更需要在执行层面"做到"，策略提醒为弥合这一鸿沟提供了有效的干预手段。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents combine language-based reasoning with external tools to perform complex tasks. Adversarial inputs can exploit interactions between the agent and its environment, causing the agent to violate safety policies during execution. Yet existing evaluations often reduce agent safety to a single attack success rate (ASR), collapsing exposure, execution, observation, and adjudication and potentially conflating actual violations with evidence visibility. We introduce REDAgentBench, an executable framework for autonomous red-teaming and faithful measurement. It derives attacks from explicit safety constraints and associated agent-system vulnerabilities, runs them in isolated service sandboxes, and verifies harmful effects from service receipts and final-state changes. The benchmark contains 1,661 cases across five service surfaces. Across six models and three agent harnesses, macro-average ASR is 65.69%; reported ASR varies with harness and evidence view, while evaluation-context disclosure changes execution behavior. In a state-grounded diagnostic cohort, almost one in five confirmed violations with resolved action anchors occurs after the agent states the relevant constraint or risk, revealing a Recognition--Execution Gap. Finally, a training-free policy reminder reduces confirmed violations by more than 70 percentage points in matched replay. These findings show that executable evaluation can improve safety measurement and identify actionable intervention points.

</details>


### [176] [Self-Correcting Long-Horizon Search Agents via Tree-Structured Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10676)
*Aijun Yang,Qianxue Guo,Ziyi Huang,Yuxuan Chen,Shiyou Qian,Jian Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReTree是一种自纠正的树状结构化记忆机制，用于基于LLM的搜索代理，通过有界的逐步骤推理上下文和源链接证据管理，在四个基准上将答案准确率最高提升25.6个百分点，同时将推理上下文压缩至Full-Trajectory ReAct的约1/1.27至1/1.51。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM搜索代理在多步交互中提供完整执行轨迹会导致上下文无限增长并引入噪声。现有的压缩方法在减少上下文时会丢失重要细节，且常常直接替换错误事实而不修复由这些事实推导出的下游推理，需要一种既能压缩上下文又能保持证据可追溯性并支持自我纠正的机制。

Method: ReTree将搜索过程建模为证据树，每个节点存储有界摘要、证据和修订历史。当新检索到的证据与先前声明冲突时，系统回溯到该声明引入的节点，替换过时证据、重新生成摘要、修剪受影响的子分支并恢复搜索。源接地证据溯源支持可靠的冲突定位，并确保最终声明可追溯至检索段落。

Result: 在四个公开的问答和搜索基准上，ReTree持续优于Full-Trajectory ReAct，答案准确率最高提升25.6个百分点；Full-Trajectory ReAct的平均最大逐步骤推理上下文是ReTree的1.27至1.51倍。

Conclusion: ReTree被证明是长时程搜索任务中一种有效的自纠正记忆抽象方法，在保持证据可追溯性的同时实现上下文压缩和错误自修复。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based search agents answer questions through multi-step interactions with external environments. However, providing complete execution trajectories to the LLM causes unbounded context growth and introduces noise. Existing compression methods reduce context at the cost of important details and often replace erroneous facts without repairing downstream reasoning derived from them. To address this problem, we propose ReTree, a self-correcting tree-structured memory mechanism for search agents. ReTree constructs a bounded per-step reasoning context while preserving source-linked evidence. It models search as an evidence tree whose nodes store bounded summaries, evidence, and revision histories. When newly retrieved evidence contradicts an earlier claim, ReTree traces back to the node where the claim was introduced, replaces outdated evidence, regenerates summaries, prunes affected branches, and resumes search. Source-grounded evidence provenance supports reliable conflict localization and keeps final claims traceable to retrieved passages. Experiments on four public question-answering and search benchmarks show that ReTree consistently outperforms Full-Trajectory ReAct, improving answer accuracy by up to 25.6 percentage points (pp); the average maximum per-step reasoning context of Full-Trajectory ReAct is $1.27$--$1.51\times$ that of ReTree. These results establish ReTree as an effective self-correcting memory abstraction for long-horizon search.

</details>


### [177] [Ex-Omni-2D: Expressive Omni-Modal Dialogue Models with Native Visual Presence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10720)
*Haoyu Zhang,Zhipeng Li,Xiaoying Tang,Tianshu Yu,Yiwen Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Ex-Omni-2D，一个全模态对话框架，能够生成包含文本、个性化语音和参考条件视频的协调响应。通过Visual Thought Plan（VTP）描述场景、情感和动作，利用多码本语音单元作为共享声学-时间接口，将语音解码与视频帧在线对齐，并通过Teacher-Student蒸馏实现高效流式生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态对话模型虽能理解多模态输入并合成语音回复，但其回复缺乏视觉具身性（visually disembodied），即没有与语音同步的视觉表现，无法提供更自然、更具交互感的对话体验。

Method: 该方法包含三个关键组件：（1）Visual Thought Plan（VTP）结构化描述场景、情感和动作；（2）利用多码本语音单元作为共享声学-时间接口，实现语音生成与视频帧的在线对齐；该接口使得可以分别利用异构的语音、对话和虚拟人视频数据进行训练，无需大量query-text-speech-video联合标注；（3）采用Teacher-Student蒸馏框架，全序列视频生成器作为Teacher，蒸馏为少步块因果的流式Student，通过Prefix Streaming机制在连续块间传递干净潜向量以减少累积延迟退化。

Result: 在四步推理下，完整的四GPU流水线实现了端到端RTF（实时因子）为1.293，分辨率为400x720/720x400，提供了一个兼顾质量与效率的实际运行点。

Conclusion: Ex-Omni-2D通过VTP规划和共享声学-时间接口，实现了文本、语音和虚拟人视频的协调生成，并通过Teacher-Student蒸馏获得流式高效推理能力，为全模态具身对话系统提供了可行的方案。

Abstract: Omni-modal dialogue models can understand multimodal inputs and synthesize spoken replies, yet their responses remain visually disembodied. We introduce \textbf{Ex-Omni-2D}, an omni-modal dialogue framework that generates a coordinated response comprising text, personalized speech, and reference-conditioned video. Given a multimodal query, reference image, and reference audio, the model predicts a structured \textit{Visual Thought Plan} (VTP) describing scene, emotion, and motion, followed by response text and native multi-codebook speech units. These units form a shared acoustic-temporal interface: they are decoded into speech and aligned online with video frames. This interface enables the response and avatar pathways to be learned from heterogeneous speech, dialogue, and avatar-video data, avoiding the need for large-scale query--text--speech--video supervision. A full-sequence Video Generator serves as the primary Teacher. For efficient incremental generation, we further distill it into a few-step block-causal \emph{Streaming Student} whose Prefix Streaming mechanism carries a clean latent across consecutive chunks to reduce cumulative late-chunk degradation. With four-step inference, the complete four-GPU pipeline achieves an end-to-end RTF of 1.293 at $400\times720$/$720\times400$, providing a practical quality--efficiency operating point.

</details>


### [178] [Tree-of-Ideas: Automated Research Ideation via Cross-Trajectory Reasoning over Scholarly Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10740)
*Xun Li,Yiying Yang,Pengtao Li,Xiao Yao,Suyu Liu,Xiaoyang Ye,Ziyu Lu,Yuan Yao,Yangning Li,Yinghui Li,Wenhao Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Tree-of-Ideas (ToI)两阶段框架用于科研选题。EvoTrace从引用关系中重建分支式学术轨迹，追踪方法演变、已解决问题和研究空白；EvoAgent跨轨迹推理识别收敛问题和互补方案，生成有据可依的研究想法。在六个AI研究主题上，ToI在自动方法中得分最高（6.27 vs 最强基线5.36），并接近人类论文参考水平（6.29）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科研选题方法要么将论文视为无结构上下文，要么将学术演变建模为孤立的引用链，忽略了研究轨迹之间的交互作用。作者希望通过跨轨迹推理来更全面地理解研究问题和解决方案的演变，从而生成更有效的研究想法。

Method: 提出ToI两阶段框架：第一阶段EvoTrace从引用关系重建具有分支结构的学术轨迹，追踪方法的演变、已解决的问题和存在的空白；第二阶段EvoAgent在这些轨迹之间进行推理，识别收敛的问题和互补的解决方案，进而生成有充分依据的研究想法。

Result: 在六个AI研究主题的评估中，ToI在10分制上达到6.27分，超过最强基线（5.36分）；在新颖性（6.36）和接地性/有据性（7.00）方面表现突出，且整体得分接近人类论文参考水平（6.29）。

Conclusion: 跨路径进化推理是生成高质量研究想法的有效途径，ToI框架验证了这一方法的可行性，其得分接近人类水平，展示了通过学术轨迹交互分析进行科研选题的潜力。

Abstract: Effective research ideation requires moving beyond a static understanding of prior work to trace how research problems and solutions evolve across the literature. Existing methods either treat papers as unstructured context or model scholarly evolution as isolated citation chains, overlooking interactions among research trajectories. We propose Tree-of-Ideas (ToI), a two-stage framework. EvoTrace reconstructs branching scholarly trajectories from citations, tracking evolving methods, resolved problems, and gaps. EvoAgent then reasons across trajectories to identify convergent problems and complementary solutions, generating grounded research ideas. Across six AI research topics, ToI achieves the highest score among automatic methods (6.27 vs. 5.36 for the strongest baseline on a 10-point scale), with strong Novelty (6.36) and Groundedness (7.00). Also, its score approaches that of human-paper references (6.29), demonstrating the value of cross-path evolutionary reasoning.

</details>


### [179] [Compositional Benchmark Synthesis for Hierarchical Human Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10765)
*Farnaz Soleimani,Abdelghani Chibani,Yacine Amirat,Ghazaleh Khodabandelou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个层级意图基准生成与评估框架，从扁平单标签动作语料中合成四层级（动作、活动、低层意图、高层意图）的层级意图基准，通过转移模型和覆盖率感知采样器构建具有主体一致性的片段，并解决合成基准的循环监督风险，释放出15,002个片段供社区再生成与扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行为识别数据存在两难：大规模语料仅有孤立的原子级标签缺乏时间组合，而记录式复合活动语料层级浅、领域窄、层级固定。需要一种从扁平数据合成多层级意图基准的方法。

Method: 提出基准生成与评估框架：从扁平动作语料中通过转移模型组装片段并施加主体一致性约束，用覆盖率感知采样器降低主体使用不均衡（Gini从0.566降至0.248）；评测使用与生成规则不相交的一阶逻辑规则以避免循环监督；通过组成性held-out差距、逻辑无关基线和顺序破坏对照等验证基准有效性。

Result: 生成包含15,002个片段的四层级基准。四种参考基线均呈现0.13-0.17的组成性held-out宏F1差距，即使图感知模型识别最好也无法消除差距，表明这是基准的结构性属性。逻辑无关基线仍违反held-out语义规则，顺序破坏对照在种子波动范围内改变宏F1。

Conclusion: 该基准具有结构性的组成性泛化挑战，并非模型缺陷所致。框架将本体、转移模型和生成器全部开源，使基准可再生成和扩展。

Abstract: Recognizing human behavior across levels of abstraction, from atomic actions to long-horizon intentions, requires data annotated along a semantic hierarchy. Large corpora provide isolated, atomically labeled clips without temporal composition, whereas recorded composite-activity corpora offer shallow, domain-narrow, fixedhierarchies. A benchmark-generation and evaluation frameworkis proposed that synthesizes a four-level hierarchical-intention benchmark, spanning actions, activities, low-level intentions (LLIs), and high-level intentions (HLIs), from a flat single-label action corpus while retaining real pre-extracted features at the action level. Episodes are assembled by a transition model under a subject-consistency constraint, and a coverage-aware sampler reduces the subject usage Gini from 0.566 to 0.248. Synthesizing such a benchmark raises a circular-supervision risk that recorded datasets avoid: if the rules generating the episodes also govern the evaluation, models can succeed by recovering the generator rather than through genuine reasoning. Validity is addressed by design, holding sequence-generation rules disjoint from the first-order-logic rules used at evaluation. The instantiation yields 15,002 episodes. Four reference baselines from different model families characterize difficulty, not as recognition methods. A compositional held-out gap of 0.13 to 0.17 macro-F1 appears across all baselines, including a graph-aware model that recognizes best yet does not close the gap, indicating a structural property of the benchmark rather than a model artifact. A logic-free baseline still violates the held-out semantic rules above their intrinsic data rate, and the order-destroying control changes macro-F1 within seed variation, serving as a generator-consistency check. Theontology, transition model, and generator are released so the benchmark can beregenerated and extended.

</details>


### [180] [Rule of Thumb: Explaining Artificial Intelligence Systems using Partial Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10766)
*Kaivalya Rawal,Daria Onitiu,Brent Mittelstadt,Sandra Wachter,Chris Russell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"经验法则"（Rule of Thumb, RoT）解释方法，一种新的可解释人工智能（XAI）方法，通过新颖的公式识别对AI系统行为预测最相关的特征。RoT适用于零样本分类、无模型访问的黑盒AI审计以及科学发现中的AI应用，同时满足主流AI法规要求，提供熟悉的界面和可视化，与模型无关且运行速度远快于替代方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法在解释AI系统决策时存在局限性，缺乏一种能够快速、通用且符合法规要求的解释方法。作者旨在提出一种新的XAI方法，以应对零样本分类、黑盒系统审计和科学发现等场景中对可解释性的迫切需求。

Method: 提出一种基于新颖公式的"Rule of Thumb"（RoT）解释方法，通过识别对预测AI系统行为最相关的特征来生成解释。该方法是模型无关的，能够应用于多种AI系统场景。

Result: RoT在零样本分类（LLM）、无模型访问的黑盒AI系统审计、以及AI辅助科学发现三个场景中均适用，且满足主流AI法规的特定要求，提供熟悉的XAI界面和可视化，运行速度显著快于现有替代方案。

Conclusion: RoT为XAI提供了一种高效、通用且合规的新方法，在速度、适用性和法规合规性方面具有明显优势，代码已开源供社区使用。

Abstract: Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) seeks to explain how an Artificial Intelligence (AI) system arrived at a particular decision. We propose ''Rule of Thumb'' (RoT) explanations, a new approach to XAI based upon a novel formulation that identifies the most relevant features for predicting the behaviour of an AI system, for a particular datapoint. We show how RoT is well-suited to enable XAI in: (a) zero-shot classification using large language models (LLMs), (b) auditing of opaque AI systems without model access, and (c) the use of AI in scientific discovery. Additionally, RoT meets specific requirements from leading AI regulations, provides a familiar interface and visualisations for XAI practitioners, is model-agnostic, and is substantially faster than alternatives.
  Code available at: https://github.com/KaiRawal/Rule-of-Thumb-Explaining-Artificial-Intelligence-Systems-using-Partial-Information

</details>


### [181] [SkillLens: Visual Skill Cards for Retrieval-Augmented GUI Action Prediction and On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10775)
*Zhou Liu,Ligang Huang,Zeli Su,Zewei Pan,Zhaoyang Han,Xing Chen,Yuanfeng Song,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Visual Skill Cards (VSCs)，一种状态条件化的记忆表示，将可复用的GUI操作流程与适用性线索、视觉证据和验证信号绑定。基于此构建SkillLens系统，从异构交互经验中构建VSCs，推理时检索相关卡片并选择性展开证据供视觉语言模型执行器进行接地GUI动作预测。此外，CardDistill利用VSC证据作为特权教师上下文训练无需运行时检索的学生模型。在Multimodal-Mind2Web和WebLINX-BrowserGym上，SkillLens使冻结的GPT-5.4-mini执行器分别提升+11.6 Step SR和+2.9 Overall；CardDistill使Qwen3-VL-2B学生模型分别提升+12.0和+3.2点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体能够感知丰富的软件界面，但其决策缺乏视觉程序性记忆：它们能识别单个控件，却不知道当前正在执行哪个熟悉的工作流程、接下来哪个控件重要、以及什么证据能确认进展。原始交互轨迹虽保留此类信息但过长且噪声大，而纯文本技能往往遗漏使过程适用所需的视觉状态。因此需要一种既能保留视觉状态又能高效支持检索和推理的记忆表示。

Method: 提出Visual Skill Cards (VSCs)，一种状态条件化的记忆表示，将可复用流程与适用性线索、视觉证据和验证信号绑定。SkillLens通过Trace-to-Visual-Skill-Card从异构交互经验中构建VSCs；推理时检索相关卡片，仅选择性扩展执行器所需的证据（而非整条轨迹），供固定的视觉语言模型执行器做接地的GUI动作预测。CardDistill则利用同一表示中的VSC证据作为特权教师上下文，训练一个无需运行时卡片检索的学生模型。

Result: 在Multimodal-Mind2Web和WebLINX-BrowserGym两个基准上，SkillLens使冻结的GPT-5.4-mini执行器分别提升+11.6点Step SR和+2.9点Overall；CardDistill使对应的Qwen3-VL-2B学生模型分别提升+12.0和+3.2点。

Conclusion: Visual Skill Cards作为状态条件化的视觉程序性记忆表示，能有效弥补计算机使用智能体在视觉工作流程识别与证据验证方面的不足。SkillLens通过VSC检索与选择性证据扩展显著提升了冻结执行器的性能，而CardDistill证明可将VSC的知识蒸馏到无需运行时检索的学生模型中，为高效部署提供了路径。

Abstract: Computer-using agents can perceive rich software interfaces, yet their decisions often lack visual procedural memory: they may recognize individual controls without identifying which familiar workflow is active, which control matters next, or what evidence would confirm progress. Raw interaction traces preserve such information but are long and noisy to condition on, whereas text-only skills often omit the visual state that makes a procedure applicable. We introduce Visual Skill Cards (VSCs), a state-conditioned memory representation that binds reusable procedures with applicability cues, visual evidence, and verification signals. SkillLens constructs VSCs from heterogeneous interaction experience through Trace-to-Visual-Skill-Card and, at inference time, retrieves relevant cards and selectively expands only the evidence needed by a fixed visual-language model executor for grounded GUI action prediction. The same representation also supports CardDistill, which uses VSC evidence as privileged teacher context to train a student that acts without runtime card retrieval. Across Multimodal-Mind2Web and WebLINX-BrowserGym, SkillLens improves the frozen GPT-5.4-mini executor by +11.6 points in Step SR and +2.9 points in Overall, respectively; CardDistill further improves the corresponding student-only Qwen3-VL-2B metrics by +12.0 and +3.2 points.

</details>


### [182] [ChemWorld: Programmable Chemical Worlds for Controlled and Replayable Agent Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10792)
*Jiangjie Qiu,Yijun Li,Xiaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ChemWorld，一个可编程化学实验环境，将可重用的过程和观测组件编译为可执行世界，分离公共实验契约与评估者拥有的化学定律，支持精确回放、审计和单一隐藏定律干预下的世界分叉，为自主化学研究提供可控且可重放的实验基底。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主化学研究需要代理能够反复行动、观测和适应的环境，但物理实验室成本高昂且难以进行严格匹配的干预实验，而大多数数字环境保持了底层实验世界基本不变，缺乏可控性和可编程性。

Method: ChemWorld将可重用的过程和观测组件编译为可执行世界，将公共实验契约与评估者拥有的化学和材料定律分离，支持变更世界组成和操作条件或修改单一隐藏定律，并通过事务性执行记录实现完整轨迹的精确回放和审计。

Result: 全量普查覆盖了参考注册表、52个生成组合及模块/接口/编译/无效操作测试；8个确定性实验案例展示了共享生命周期语义、失败恢复和精确回放，6对父子世界分叉隔离了单一私有定律干预的效果，独立代理也通过同一公共接口在非参考世界中完成了完整生命周期。

Conclusion: 在声明的组件和模型领域内，ChemWorld为跨系统化变化的化学世界中的实验研究提供了可控且可重放的基底，与物理实验室证据和校准互补。

Abstract: Autonomous chemistry increasingly depends on environments in which agents can repeatedly act, observe, and adapt.Physical laboratories provide essential real-material evidence but are costly to repeat and difficult to use for tightly matched interventions, whereas most digital environments keep the underlying experimental world largely fixed. We introduce ChemWorld, a programmable chemical environment in which reusable process and observation components are compiled into executable worlds. ChemWorld separates the public experimental contract available to an agent from evaluator-owned chemical and material laws. Researchers can therefore vary world composition and operating conditions, or change a single hidden law while holding the public task and interaction conditions fixed. Transactional execution records operations, failures, resource changes, and state transitions, allowing complete environment-action trajectories to be replayed exactly and audited. Full-census qualification covered the reference registry, 52 generated compositions, and module, interface, compilation, and invalid-action tests. Eight deterministic experimental cases demonstrated shared lifecycle semantics, failure recovery, and exact replay, while six parent-child world-fork pairs isolated the effects of single private-law interventions under matched public conditions. An independent agent also completed a full lifecycle in a non-reference world through the same public interface. Within the declared component and model domain, ChemWorld provides a controlled and replayable substrate for studying experimentation across systematically varied chemical worlds, complementary to physical-laboratory evidence and calibration.

</details>


### [183] [EvoMem: Memory-Augmented Evolution for Code Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10795)
*Viktor Volkov,Valentin Khrulkov,Andrey V. Galichin,Danil Sivtsov,Nikita Glazkov,Olga Volkova,Konstantin Pchelin,Iaroslav Bespalov,Dmitry V. Dylov,Petr Anokhin,Ivan Oseledets*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoMem是一种针对LLM驱动的进化程序搜索的持久记忆架构，通过将成功的突变事件转化为结构化的、任务感知的建议进行存储与复用，从而减少冗余探索并提升跨任务学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的进化搜索框架在每次运行后丢弃了可复用的突变知识，导致类似想法被反复重新发现，限制了跨运行和跨任务学习的机会。

Method: EvoMem采用两阶段架构：运行后阶段提取并存储带有来源信息的成功突变想法；后续进化阶段根据当前任务和程序上下文检索少量相关指令来指导突变过程。

Result: 在几何优化、多跳问答、GPU内核优化及相关基准测试中，EvoMem在大多数评估设置下取得了目标指标或搜索速度的正向平均改进，同时也显示出任务间的差异性。

Conclusion: 持久记忆能够减少部分冗余探索，改进LLM驱动进化搜索中成功策略的复用与适应能力。

Abstract: Successful mutation strategies in evolutionary code search may contain reusable knowledge that is useful beyond a single run, and in some cases may transfer across related tasks and domains. However, existing LLM-driven evolutionary frameworks largely discard such knowledge, repeatedly rediscovering similar ideas and limiting opportunities for cross-run and cross-task learning. We introduce EvoMem, a persistent memory architecture for LLM-based evolutionary program search that captures and reuses candidate mutation knowledge. EvoMem converts successful mutation events into structured, task-aware advice for future runs. It operates in two phases: after each run, it extracts and stores promising ideas with provenance, and during subsequent evolution, it retrieves a small set of relevant instructions based on the current task and program context to guide mutation. Across geometric optimization, multi-hop question answering, GPU kernel optimization, and related benchmarks, our experiments show positive average improvements in target metrics or search speed for most evaluated settings, while also revealing variability across tasks. Overall, EvoMem provides evidence that persistent memory can reduce some redundant exploration and improve the reuse and adaptation of successful strategies in LLM-driven evolutionary search.

</details>


### [184] [Hypothesis Frontier: Verifier Guided LLM and Symbolic Search for First-Order Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10843)
*Serafim Batzoglou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Hypothesis Frontier，一个验证器引导的神经符号框架，用于一阶概念合成任务。该框架逐对象评估LLM生成的公式，保留最强的已验证假设，并利用剩余错误指导后续生成，同时通过符号处理修复无效公式并简化有效公式，显著提高了问题求解率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶概念合成任务要求在多个有限关系结构上推断出能一致分类所有对象的公式。虽然每个候选公式可以被精确评估，但量化一阶公式的搜索空间极其庞大，且LLM输出往往在语义上可行却不够完全正确，需要一种方法来结合LLM的生成能力与符号推理的精确性。

Method: 提出Hypothesis Frontier框架：(1) 对每个LLM生成的公式在训练对象上逐一评估；(2) 跨轮次保留最强的已验证假设；(3) 利用剩余错误指导后续公式生成；(4) 符号处理修复无效公式但保持锚定于LLM生成的假设；(5) 在不改变训练预测的前提下简化有效公式。

Result: 在匹配的模型、问题集和LLM轮次预算下，Hypothesis Frontier相比重复原始提示生成能解决显著更多的问题。在最终公式选定后，精确简化在保持所有训练预测不变的前提下，大幅缩短了多数有效公式。

Conclusion: 精确符号推理既能帮助解决更多归纳问题，也能压缩许多最终生成的公式，证明了验证器引导的神经符号方法在一阶概念合成中的有效性。

Abstract: First-order concept synthesis asks a system to infer one formula that classifies labeled objects consistently across several finite relational structures. Every candidate can be evaluated exactly, but quantified first-order formulas form a vast search space, and LLM outputs are often semantically promising without being fully correct. We introduce Hypothesis Frontier, a verifier-guided neurosymbolic framework that evaluates each LLM formula on every training object, retains the strongest verified hypothesis across rounds, and uses its remaining errors to guide subsequent generation. Symbolic processing repairs invalid formulas while remaining anchored to the LLM-generated hypothesis, and simplifies train-valid formulas without changing any training prediction. Under matched models, problem sets, and LLM-round budgets, Hypothesis Frontier solves substantially more problems than repeated original-prompt generation. After the final formulas are selected, exact simplification shortens many train-valid formulas while preserving every training prediction. Exact symbolic reasoning therefore helps both to solve more induction problems and to compress many of the resulting formulas.

</details>


### [185] [Enhanced Filtering Algorithms for the Euclidean Traveling Salesperson Problem and its variants in Constraint Logic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10881)
*Alessandro Bertagnon,Marco Gavanelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对欧几里得旅行商问题（TSP），提出利用坐标几何信息的新的约束传播过滤算法，并扩展到欧几里得广义旅行商问题（EGTSP），实验证明该方法比现有方法更具计算优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统约束规划方法在处理欧几里得TSP时忽略了节点坐标携带的几何信息，仅通过计算完整的距离矩阵将其视为一般TSP处理，导致约束传播强度不足，计算效率不高。

Method: 在约束逻辑编程（CLP）框架下提出新的过滤算法，利用欧几里得空间中节点坐标的几何信息来实现更强的约束传播，并将该方法推广到欧几里得广义旅行商问题（EGTSP）。

Result: 通过实验验证，所提出的利用几何信息的过滤算法相比现有方法在计算性能上具有明显优势。

Conclusion: 利用几何信息可以显著增强欧几里得TSP及其变体（如EGTSP）的约束传播能力，为实际路由和物流应用提供更高效的求解方法。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP) is one of the best-known problems in computer science and arises in many engineering applications, such as smart vehicles and intelligent transportation systems. In the "Euclidean" case, each node is defined by its coordinates in the plane and distances are computed using the Euclidean metric. In the Constraint Programming (CP) literature, the Euclidean TSP is typically addressed by computing the full distance matrix and treating it as a general case; however this approach ignores the geometric information carried by the points' coordinates. In this work, we propose new filtering algorithms, implemented in Constraint Logic Programming (CLP), that exploit such geometric information to achieve stronger constraint propagation than existing approaches. Moreover, we show how this methodology can be extended to other Euclidean variants of the TSP, including the Euclidean Generalized Traveling Salesperson Problem (EGTSP), which is relevant in practical routing and logistics applications. Experimental results demonstrate the computational advantages of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [186] [ComBodied Agents: a New Paradigm of Human-Centric Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10915)
*Qianggang Ding,Xingyao Wang,Rui Feng,Zhibin Wang,Feixiang Wang,Kelong Mao,Hao Sun,Zhiyao Luo,Jiankai Tang,Lei Li,Jiadong Guo,Minheng Ni,Weicong Lin,Chenxi Yang,Hongxiang Gao,Zhenghua Chen,Yang Bai,Min Wu,Jun Cheng,Huazhu Fu,Dacheng Tao,Bang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Combodied Agents"（融合体智能体）范式，将数字智能体与具身智能体统一起来，以个体的动态状态和主体性为核心建模、干预和评估对象，构建从感知、记忆、世界模型到干预策略的闭环系统，推动Agentic AI从外部任务完成转向持续的人类福祉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体存在结构性缺口：数字智能体仅转换软件状态，具身智能体仅转换物理状态，二者都无法理解用户"为何"发生状态变化（如忘记服药的原因），也无法提供因人而异的恰当支持。人的动态状态和主体性未被作为建模与干预的首要对象。

Method: 提出闭环框架，包含四个核心组件：（1）基于事件的、多模态感知系统以重构有意义的个人事件；（2）纵向、可修正的记忆以提供时间上下文；（3）个人世界模型（Personal World Models）用于预测备选决策和干预下的未来个人状态与结果；（4）允许的策略选择在同意、不确定性、安全、可逆性和用户控制约束下给出适度支持。采用有目的边界、不确定性感知、用户可修正的表征，而非完备的数字孪生。

Result: 系统化组织了设计空间（按人类状态目标、关系情境和智能体角色划分），提出了以场景为中心的评估方法、主体性保持指标、基准需求、边缘端个人模型以及治理方向，为Combodied Agents的实际落地提供指导框架。

Conclusion: Combodied Agents将个人助理、健康智能体、AI伴侣和自适应人机系统的分散能力统一为一个闭环，使Agentic AI从单纯的任务执行转向对人类福祉的持续支持，代表了人本中心AI智能体的范式性转变。

Abstract: After an older adult misses a medication dose, a software agent can send another reminder and an embodied agent can bring the medication. Yet neither explains whether the person forgot, is confused, has side effects, or deliberately refused, nor what support is appropriate. This reveals a structural gap in Agentic AI: Digital Agents primarily transform software states, while Embodied Agents transform physical states; neither makes a person's evolving state and agency the primary object of modeling, intervention, and evaluation. We introduce Combodied Agents, a human-centered paradigm that perceives, models, predicts, and supports individual human-state trajectories over time, using software tools, sensors, wearables, robots, and human services as action channels rather than end goals. We unify fragmented capabilities across personal assistants, health agents, AI companions, and adaptive human--AI systems into a closed loop: event-based multimodal perception reconstructs meaningful personal events; longitudinal, correctable memory provides temporal context; Personal World Models estimate future personal states and outcomes under alternative decisions and interventions; and an admissible intervention policy selects proportionate support under consent, uncertainty, safety, reversibility, and user control. Feedback from the person and environment updates the loop. Rather than requiring an exhaustive Human Digital Twin, the framework uses purpose-bounded, uncertainty-aware, user-correctable representations. We organize the design space by human-state targets, relational contexts, and agent roles, and propose scenario-centered evaluation, agency-preservation metrics, benchmark requirements, edge-native personal models, and governance directions. Combodied Agents shift Agentic AI from external task completion toward sustained human benefit.

</details>


### [187] [IO Factory: Simulating AI-Enabled Influence Campaigns at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10920)
*Lukasz Olejnik,Wenchao Dong,Jonas R. Kunst,Signe Riemer-Sørensen,Tobias Herb,Meeyoung Cha,Daniel Thilo Schroeder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了IO Factory，一个AI驱动的框架，用于将信息与影响力活动模拟为完全集成、可追溯的过程，支持在受控模拟平台上分析多达10万个智能体的协调影响活动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字操纵威胁已从单一语言模型生成的劝服性文本扩展到AI智能体群（AI swarms）——即持续存在的、协调的智能体群体，它们能适应平台反馈并将有组织的活动伪装成普通社交互动。由于此类活动无法仅从孤立消息中识别，必须在规划、平台行动、曝光、解读、测量和适应的连续谱系中进行分析，因此需要一个能够完整表示该过程的框架。

Method: IO Factory在受控模拟平台内表示完整的活动过程，链接行为者角色、平台行动、曝光记录、结构化基于模型的评估以及模拟人口中的配置变化。实现了该架构，并在多达10万个智能体的配置上进行评估。

Result: 结果显示IO Factory能够按规模执行活动时间线，并产生可检查的曝光证据和配置信念变量的可测量变化。通过记录每次运行中的行为者、目标、行动约束、曝光路径和测量规则，框架支持可复现研究和协调影响的红队分析。

Conclusion: IO Factory为研究协调影响力活动提供了可复现、可审计的模拟框架，能够生成可检查的证据支持红队分析与安全研究。

Abstract: We introduce IO Factory, an AI-driven framework for simulating information and influence campaigns as fully integrated, traceable processes. The threat of digital manipulation now extends beyond persuasive text from individual language models to AI swarms, i.e., persistent groups of coordinated agents that adapt to platform feedback and disguise organized campaigns as ordinary social interaction. Because such campaigns cannot be identified from isolated messages alone, they must be analyzed across a continuous spectrum of planning, platform action, exposure, interpretation, measurement, and adaptation. IO Factory represents this process inside a controlled simulated platform, linking actor roles, platform actions, exposure records, structured model-based evaluations, and configured changes in the simulated population. We implement the architecture and evaluate it across configurations of up to 100,000 agents. The results show that IO Factory executes campaign timelines at scale and produces inspectable evidence of exposure and measured movement in configured belief variables. By recording the actors, objectives, action constraints, exposure paths, and measurement rules used in each run, IO Factory supports reproducible research and red-team analysis of coordinated influence.

</details>


### [188] [ThinkRetrieve: Retrieval-Augmented Reasoning Traces for Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10928)
*Vaibhav Singh,Soumya Suvra Ghosal,Sarvesh Gharat,Soumyabrata Pal,Ramasuri Narayanam,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ThinkRetrieve框架，通过在大型推理模型（LRM）的每一步推理过程中动态检索并注入已解决的示例，来增强推理轨迹，从而缓解顺序测试时扩展带来的收益递减问题，在多个推理模型和数据集上取得了最高60%的相对精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型通过分配额外的推理时计算来生成扩展的思维链以提升性能，但近期研究发现，顺序测试时扩展往往出现收益递减甚至负收益，因为更长的推理轨迹伴随不确定性增加、错误累积和偏离原始问题的问题。

Method: ThinkRetrieve是一个测试时扩展框架：在推理的每个中间步骤，从包含问题及逐步解答的外部语料库中动态检索相关的已解决示例，并将这些示例直接注入推理轨迹中，为模型提供"如何推理"的引导，而不仅仅是"哪些事实相关"的信息。

Result: 在五个推理模型（1.5B-8B参数）上，针对GSM-8K、MATH-500、AIME 2025和SciQ四个数据集进行实验，ThinkRetrieve相较于标准测试时扩展始终提升了准确率，在AIME 2025上取得了高达60%的相对增益。

Conclusion: ThinkRetrieve通过动态检索并注入已解决示例到推理轨迹中，有效缓解了顺序测试时扩展的收益递减问题，为大型推理模型的测试时扩展提供了一种新的增强方式。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) improve performance by allocating additional inference-time compute to generate extended chain-of-thought reasoning. However, recent studies reveal that sequential test-time scaling often yields diminishing or even negative returns, as longer traces exhibit increased uncertainty, error compounding, and drift from the original problem. We propose ThinkRetrieve, a test-time scaling framework that augments the reasoning traces of LRMs with dynamically retrieved solved examples at each reasoning step. Given an external corpus of problems paired with step-by-step solutions, ThinkRetrieve retrieves relevant exemplars at each intermediate step and injects them directly into the thinking trace, providing the model with guidance on how to reason rather than merely what facts are relevant. Experiments across five reasoning models (1.5B--8B parameters) on GSM-8K, MATH-500, AIME 2025, and SciQ demonstrate that ThinkRetrieve consistently improves accuracy over standard test-time scaling, with relative gains of up to $60\%$ on AIME 2025.

</details>


### [189] [FedCGR: Federated Cross-Domain Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10929)
*Zhuodong Liu,Hugen Lv,Xiangyu Li,Bohan Guo,Peiyu Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCGR，一种联邦生成式跨域推荐框架。其核心思想是将物品表示为基于公共元数据的离散语义ID（SID）序列，通过共享词汇表实现跨域对齐，避免交换私有交互数据。FedCGR通过可靠性感知语义接口注入局部协同过滤信号，并采用原型个性化生成器按域相关性选择性聚合共享参数，在六个Amazon跨域场景中优于联邦生成基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦部署下跨域推荐面临两难：跨域对齐需要行为锚点（如重叠用户、共享交互信号），但这些锚点在客户端间往往是稀疏的、不可用的或隐私敏感的。已有方法难以在保护隐私的同时实现有效的跨域知识迁移。

Method: 将物品表示为源自公共元数据的离散语义ID（SID）序列，利用共享词汇表诱导跨域物品对齐，避免交换私有交互或对齐域特定嵌入。FedCGR包含两个关键组件：(1) 可靠性感知语义接口，用于注入本地协同过滤证据；(2) 原型个性化生成器，根据域相关性选择性聚合共享参数，同时将域特定参数保留在本地。

Result: 在六个Amazon跨域推荐场景上的实验表明，FedCGR一致优于联邦生成式基线，并在完整排序和采样评估协议下，与强序贯和联邦CDR方法相比取得了具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 通过将联邦CDR重新构思为在稳定语义物品语言上的生成问题，FedCGR成功解决了联邦环境中跨域对齐的困难，验证了"稳定物品语言+显式适应性"这一设计范式在隐私保护和迁移效果之间的有效平衡。

Abstract: Cross-domain recommendation (CDR) transfers preference knowledge across related domains, but federated deployment makes cross-domain alignment difficult because the behavioral anchors that align item spaces, such as overlapping users and shared interaction signals, are often sparse, unavailable, or privacy-sensitive across clients. To address this tension, we revisit federated CDR as generation over a stable semantic item language. By representing items as discrete semantic ID (SID) sequences derived from public item-side metadata, cross-domain item alignment is induced by a shared vocabulary rather than by exchanging private interactions or aligning domain-specific embeddings. Directly federating SID-based generators, however, introduces two design constraints: the SID tokenizer must remain fixed to preserve cross-client token consistency, which creates a semantic-only bottleneck because local collaborative filtering (CF) signals cannot be globally shared or aligned; meanwhile, standard federated averaging can cause negative transfer under domain heterogeneity. To overcome these constraints, we propose FedCGR, a federated generative CDR framework that keeps the item language stable and makes adaptation explicit. FedCGR injects local CF evidence through a reliability-aware semantic interface and trains a prototype-personalized generator that selectively aggregates shared parameters according to domain relatedness while keeping domain-specific quantities local. Experiments on six Amazon cross-domain scenarios show that FedCGR consistently outperforms federated generative baselines and achieves competitive performance against strong sequential and federated CDR methods under both full-ranking and sampled evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [190] [XCoT-VLA: Executable Chain-of-Thought for Vision-Language-Action Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10976)
*Foundation Model Team,XPeng Inc*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: XCoT-VLA提出用紧凑的可执行CoT token替代冗长的自然语言思维链，通过Reason-Action监督和确定性token函数路由实现轨迹生成，显著降低推理开销并提升驾驶规划精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型使用冗长的自然语言Chain-of-Thought进行推理，但这种方式在实时驾驶控制中具有开放性、解码成本高且难以优化的问题，不适合作为面向动作的表征。

Method: 将描述性推理替换为从自动构建的Reason-Action监督中学习的紧凑可执行CoT token；预测的XCoT序列通过共享多模态自注意力保持上下文并条件化固定轨迹查询；采用确定性token函数路由，对XCoT token使用Reason FFN，对轨迹查询使用Control FFN进行流匹配轨迹生成；另引入XCPO作为可选的精化扩展。

Result: XCoT-VLA在通用分布集上将纵向ADE从1.645降至1.323，在变道场景将横向FDE从1.616降至0.648；仅用2-6个可执行token即实现实时规划预算内的推理。

Conclusion: 驾驶导向推理可以做到紧凑、可执行，并直接连接到轨迹生成，在保持实时性的同时提升规划精度。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can connect scene understanding, semantic reasoning, and trajectory generation for autonomous driving. However, verbose natural-language Chain-of-Thought (CoT) is poorly suited to real-time control because it is open-ended, costly to decode, and difficult to optimize as an action-facing representation. We propose XCoT-VLA, which replaces descriptive rationales with compact executable CoT tokens learned from automatically constructed Reason-Action supervision. Logged trajectories provide action evidence, while scene context supplies causal semantics. The predicted XCoT sequence remains in context and conditions fixed trajectory queries through shared multimodal self-attention. Deterministic token-function routing applies the Reason FFN to XCoT tokens and the Control FFN to trajectory queries for flow-matching trajectory generation. We further introduce XCoT Policy Optimization (XCPO) as an optional refinement extension in the same executable token space. XCoT-VLA reduces longitudinal ADE from 1.645 to 1.323 on a general-distribution set and lateral FDE from 1.616 to 0.648 in lane-change scenarios. By representing driving-oriented reasoning with only 2-6 executable XCoT tokens, our method substantially reduces autoregressive reasoning overhead and remains within the real-time planning budget. These results demonstrate that driving-oriented reasoning can be compact, executable, and directly connected to trajectory generation.

</details>


### [191] [V-FiLLM: Verified Financial LLM Reasoning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11047)
*Alicia Larsen,Victoire Laurent,Aulia Kharis Rakhamsari,Lara Turgut,Nino Antulov-Fantulin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出V-FiLLM框架，通过可执行计算树从真实表格生成金融推理基准，以符号化求值获得正确答案，从而避免人工标注和模型误差。基准提供四个独立可控制的难度轴，实验发现LLM在金融表格推理上随深度和数值扰动显著下降，LoRA微调可有效提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准在STEM领域评估LLM已有显著进展，但金融领域基于结构化数据的推理仍缺乏充分探索，需要可扩展、低成本、答案可靠的金融推理基准来推动该方向研究。

Method: V-FiLLM通过可执行计算树从真实表格生成基准问题：树通过符号化求值获得真实答案，再渲染为自然语言问题。计算树支持四个独立可控的难度轴：计算深度、表达式广度、金融概念复杂度和上下文大小。此外结合LoRA微调验证链式推理轨迹以提升模型能力。

Result: 实验表明，随着推理深度增加，准确率下降高达51%；在对抗性数值扰动下准确率下降高达47个百分点。LoRA微调使保留集准确率从81.1%提升至85.6%，并在FinQA基准上比基础模型高出5个百分点。

Conclusion: LLM在金融表格推理中仍面临推理深度和数值扰动等鲁棒性挑战；低成本的LoRA微调在组合推理的金融问答上是有效的改进方向。

Abstract: While existing benchmarks have made substantial progress in evaluating LLMs across STEM domains, financial reasoning over structured data remains comparatively less explored. We introduce V-FiLLM, a framework that generates financial reasoning benchmarks from executable computation trees grounded in real tables, yielding items whose answers are correct by construction. Trees are evaluated symbolically to obtain ground truth and rendered into natural-language questions, removing any model from the labeling loop, so items can be generated at arbitrary scale without annotation cost and without inheriting a generator's error rate. V-FiLLM exposes four independently controllable axes of difficulty including computation depth, expression breadth, financial concept complexity, and context size. By evaluating on open-source models, we find that accuracy falls up to 51% as reasoning depth increases, and up to 47% points under adversarial numerical perturbations, highlighting remaining challenges in robust financial reasoning over tables. We further show that lightweight LoRA fine-tuning on verified chain-of-thought traces improves accuracy from 81.1% to 85.6% on held-out problems and outperforms the base model by 5% points on FinQA (Chen et al., 2022a), s), suggesting that targeted, low-cost adaptation is a promising direction for compositional reasoning in financial QA.

</details>


### [192] [SkillZip: Evaluation-Free Skill Compression for Self-Evolving Agents by Discovering Reusable Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11079)
*Xiaofan Bai,Hongqiang Lin,Chao Liu,Yantao Zhang,Xuan Jin,Xipeng Cao,Yuhong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillZip是一种无需评估的无评测提示压缩方法，通过找到最短的忠实结构解释来压缩自进化智能体的技能。它将技能视为带类型的结构（触发条件、工作流、工具契约、输出契约），用最小描述长度目标在技能契约和残差之间寻找最优划分，同时通过硬覆盖约束保证所有关键规则被保留，支持一次性压缩和持续Zip-on-Write模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化智能体不断追加成功流程和失败修复，导致技能膨胀、重复内容多、注入成本高且难以维护。通用提示压缩方法不适用于技能压缩，因为技能不是平面文本——其名称与描述定义适用场景、工作流控制执行、工具与输出契约约束有效性，且罕见异常即使在采样任务中未被激活也可能至关重要。评估引导压缩虽有帮助但引入rollout成本和对评估集的依赖。

Method: SkillZip采用"解释一次，多处引用"的核心直觉：在适用作用域中仅陈述一次重复规则，将重复动作序列分解为共享过程，并仅保留差异作为显式异常。形式上，方法将直觉建模为带类型的极小描述长度目标，在技能契约与残差之间划分，并附带硬覆盖约束以覆盖每个触发条件、工作流边、工具需求、义务和输出字段。提供简单共享阈值、按构造保留唯一罕见规则，并支持高效的局部更新。有两种模式：一次性模式（一次结构化提取调用加确定性优化）和持续Zip-on-Write模式（集成每个自进化补丁，无需重放任务或重新解析全部历史）。

Result: 通过全面的实验评估，SkillZip在压缩性能、泛化能力和成本开销方面均展现出有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: SkillZip通过无评估、结构感知的压缩方式解决了自进化智能体技能膨胀问题，既能有效压缩又保证技能语义完整性，同时支持持续更新的场景，为自进化系统的技能管理提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents accumulate reusable skills by appending successful procedures and failure fixes. Over time, the same requirement is often restated in several branches, examples, and warnings, while common action sequences are copied rather than reused. The resulting skill becomes expensive to inject and difficult to maintain. Generic prompt compression is ill-suited to this setting because a skill is not a flat passage: its name and description define when it applies, its workflow controls execution, its tool and output contracts constrain validity, and rare exceptions may remain essential even when no sampled task activates them. Evaluation-guided compression can test these behaviors, but it introduces rollouts, cost, and dependence on the compression-time evaluation set. We present SkillZip, an evaluation-free method that compresses a skill by finding its shortest faithful structural explanation. The intuition is explain once, reference many: state a repeated rule once at the scope where it applies, factor a repeated action sequence into a shared procedure, and keep only the differences as explicit exceptions. We formalize this intuition as a typed minimum description-length objective over a skill contract and a residual, subject to a hard coverage constraint for every extracted trigger, workflow edge, tool requirement, obligation, and output field. The formulation provides simple sharing thresholds, preserves unique rare rules by construction, and supports efficient local updates. SkillZip has a one-shot mode with one structured extraction call and deterministic optimization, and a continual Zip-on-Write mode that integrates each self-evolution patch without replaying tasks or reparsing the full history. Through comprehensive experimental evaluations, we demonstrate the effectiveness and superiority of SkillZip in compression performance, generalizability, and cost overhead.

</details>


### [193] [RTSKG: Building a Rail Transit Station Knowledge Graph Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11080)
*Shutong Zhu,Tianxing Wu,Runfeng Liu,Yuang Gu,Xuan He,Yuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个轨道交通站点知识图谱（RTSKG）数据集，通过统一模式显式建模不同城市实体（如站点、道路、兴趣点）之间的空间与语义交互，以支持城市级轨道交通站点相关任务。实验表明其在站点周边商店推荐和知识增强的客流预测任务中有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市级轨道交通站点相关任务（如客流预测）需要大规模城市数据，但现有研究在数据组织上往往忽略了各类城市实体之间复杂的交互关系，导致信息利用不充分。

Method: 构建了RTSKG数据集，采用精心设计的统一模式整合异构城市实体（轨道交通站点、道路段、兴趣点），并将知识图谱作为Linked Data公开发布。

Result: 在站点周边商店推荐和知识增强的客流预测两个任务上的评估验证了RTSKG的有效性，展示了其支持城市级轨道交通站点分析的潜力。

Conclusion: RTSKG能够有效建模城市实体间的复杂交互，为城市级轨道交通站点分析提供有价值的数据支撑，并可作为Linked Data开放获取。

Abstract: Rail transit systems play a vital role in urban mobility and economic development. As key components of such systems, rail transit stations function as critical transport hubs that enhance urban accessibility and stimulate development in surrounding areas. City-level rail transit station related tasks (e.g., ridership prediction) require large-scale urban data, but current studies often neglect complex interactions among various urban entities in terms of data organization. In this paper, to address the above issue, we build a Rail Transit Station Knowledge Graph (RTSKG) dataset which explicitly models the spatial and semantic interactions among different kinds of urban entities, to benefit city-level rail transit station related tasks. RTSKG integrates heterogeneous urban entities, such as rail transit stations, road segments, and points of interest, with a specially designed unified schema, and is accessible as Linked Data at https://w3id.org/rtskg/. Evaluations on station-area store recommendation and knowledge-enhanced ridership prediction demonstrate the effectiveness of RTSKG, highlighting its potential to support city-level rail transit station analysis.

</details>


### [194] [Why Does CLAUDE.md Keep Growing? Catastrophic Remembering in Agentic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11095)
*Kushal Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了Agentic编码README（如CLAUDE.md）在真实仓库中无限增长的问题，将其命名为"灾难性记忆"（与持续学习中的"灾难性遗忘"相对）。通过分析1,867个仓库中247,694条指令的生命周期，发现Agentic提示词平均增长+226%，并发现提示词注释可以有效抑制增长，移除99.3%的多余指令，并在真实世界Agentic指令遵循任务上提升最高23.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic编码README在真实仓库中无限增长，但缺乏系统的理解和解决方案。研究者发现追加指令成本低但删除成本高（呈指数级），导致提示词不断膨胀，因此希望对这一现象进行表征并找到有效的控制方法。

Method: 1) 对1,867个仓库中247,694条指令生命周期进行大规模实证分析；2) 通过反转IFEval构建可验证世界，得到已知最优提示词，利用提示词注释编码潜在推理来减少冗余指令；3) 将同一反转方法应用于WildIFEval，测试真实场景下的效果。

Result: Agentic提示词无限增长，平均+226%膨胀，每次提交净增+4.9条指令；指令越老越难被删除（log-hazard -0.032/commit）。提示词注释可移除99.3%的多余指令（从+211.3%降至+1.4%），在WildIFEval上可提升实际指令遵循能力最高23.1%。

Conclusion: 提示词注释是控制Agentic提示词无限增长的有效机制，正如代码注释有助于代码维护一样。作者提出"如果英语是新代码，为什么还没有注释？"的反思，呼吁将代码工程的注释实践引入提示词工程。

Abstract: Agentic coding READMEs like CLAUDE.md grow without bound in real repositories, stopping only when the repository retires or someone rewrites the file wholesale. We trace this to imperfect recall: appending an instruction is always cheap, but once an instruction's rationale is gone, deleting it without risking a correctness regression costs O(2^|D|) in a prompt of |D| instructions. We name the resulting divergence catastrophic remembering, the inverse of catastrophic forgetting around which continual learning is organized. First, we characterize this phenomenon across 247,694 instruction lifetimes in 1,867 repositories: agentic prompts grow without bound, more than tripling over their lifetime (+226%), gaining +4.9 net instructions every commit; further, the older an instruction gets, the less likely it is to be deleted (log-hazard -0.032/commit). Then, we show that prompt comments can halt the growth: inverting IFEval yields verifiable worlds whose optimal prompts are known, and there comments encoding latent reasoning remove 99.3% of excess instructions (+211.3% to +1.4%). Finally, applying the same inversion to WildIFEval, we show that prompt comments can improve real-world agentic instruction-following by up to 23.1%. If English is the new code, why don't we have comments yet?

</details>


### [195] [sLTN: Structural Logic Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11136)
*Davide Rinaldi,Luciano Serafini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出sLTN，一个扩展逻辑张量网络（LTN）的神经符号框架，将结构维度（如时间步、序列位置、图节点）作为语言中的一等公民，使其能够直接在逻辑层面表达时序、序列和关系约束，并通过模糊张量语义形式化，同时提供了PyTorch实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始LTN框架主要适用于扁平化的个体数据集合，无法显式捕获时间顺序、序列位置或图连接等结构组织信息，这限制了其在时序推理和结构化数据任务上的应用。

Method: sLTN将结构维度引入为一等语言元素，结构维度代表与领域特定组织（如时间步、序列位置、图节点）相关联的命名张量轴。它们可以被显式量化、通过结构关系关联，并用于在逻辑层面直接表达时间、序列和关系约束。论文形式化了sLTN的语法和模糊张量语义，并证明在无结构维度时退化为原始LTN语义。同时提供基于声明式签名、公式解析和张量解释的PyTorch实现。

Result: 论文在代表性时序和序列推理示例上展示了框架的有效性，并发布了sltn库（GitHub上可获取），作为该库的配套文档。

Conclusion: sLTN扩展了LTN的表达能力，使得神经符号推理能够直接处理具有内在结构组织的数据，同时保持与原始LTN的兼容性，为结构化的时序和关系推理提供了统一的逻辑级框架。

Abstract: Logic Tensor Networks (LTN) provide a neurosymbolic framework in which first-order logic is interpreted through tensor operations, enabling logical constraints to be integrated with differentiable learning. However, the original formulation of LTN is primarily suited to data represented as flat collections of individuals, and does not explicitly capture structural organization such as temporal order, sequential position, or graph connectivity. We introduce sLTN, an extension of LTN that makes structural dimensions first-class elements of the language. Structural dimensions represent named tensor axes associated with domain-specific organization, such as time steps, sequence positions, or graph nodes. They can be quantified explicitly, related through structural relations, and used to express temporal, sequential, and relational constraints directly at the logical level. We formalize the syntax and fuzzy tensor semantics of sLTN and show that, in the absence of structural dimensions, the framework recovers the original LTN semantics as a special case. We further describe a PyTorch implementation based on a declarative signature, formula parsing, and tensorial interpretation. The framework is illustrated on representative temporal and sequential reasoning examples. This paper serves as a companion to the sltn library, available at https://github.com/logictensornetworks/sltn.

</details>


### [196] [Long-Horizon AI Research for Grothendieck Constant: A Case Study in Human-AI Mathematical Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11195)
*Alan Li,Rahul Saha,Anton Xue,Swarat Chaudhuri,Adam Klivans,Pravesh K Kothari,Raghu Meka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过一个详细的案例研究，展示了AI如何在数学研究中被有效使用——具体而言，AI帮助将Grothendieck常数K_G的最佳已知界收紧到[6π/11, π/(2log(1+√2)) - 10^-4]，且AI产生的洞察被领域专家视为新颖。文章还讨论了对AI用于数学研究的经验教训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理在数学研究中的使用日益增多，但如何有效利用它们并不明确。作者希望通过具体案例，说明AI在数学研究中能够带来实质性突破的条件和方法。

Method: 作者以Grothendieck常数K_G的界改进为案例，使用AI研究系统辅助推导和发现新的数学洞察，并将结果与领域专家的判断进行对照，同时反思AI的优缺点及创造理想条件的经验。

Result: 成功地将K_G的上下界收紧至6π/11 ≤ K_G ≤ π/(2log(1+√2)) - 10^-4，且AI发现的见解被领域专家认为具有新颖性。这是对K_G已知最佳界的一次重要改进。

Conclusion: AI可以在数学研究中产生被领域专家认可的新颖突破性洞察，但要实现这一点需要精心设计合适的条件；同时AI也有明显的优势与局限，值得深入讨论与反思。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used in mathematics research, but it is often unclear how to use them effectively. Towards this, we present an extensive case study of how AI was used to improve bounds on the Grothendieck constant $K_G$, which captures the hardness between combinatorial problems and their continuous relaxations. Specifically, while the precise value of $K_G$ is not known, we recently tightened the best known bounds to \[
  \frac{6π}{11}
  \;\le\;
  K_G
  \;\le\;
  \fracπ{2\log(1+\sqrt2)} - 10^{-4}. \] Crucially, these improvements were achieved using an AI research system that could arrive at insights deemed novel by domain experts. We give a detailed discussion of our experience using AI for mathematics research, particularly touching upon its strengths and weaknesses, as well as our experience with creating ideal conditions for AI to arrive at breakthrough insights.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [197] [Protection Levels for Vision-Based Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10023)
*Olivia Beyer Bruvik,Romeo Valentin,Marc R. Schlichting,Don Walker,Mykel J. Kochenderfer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在跑道视觉导航中计算保护级别（Protection Levels）的算法，为基于视觉的飞行器位姿估计提供概率性误差边界，即使在存在未检测故障的情况下也能确保安全完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉导航作为GNSS的补充需要完整性保证，但传统方法缺乏对故障测量情况下位姿误差的概率性上界，无法满足认证要求。

Method: 在先前基于RAIM启发的概率计算机视觉管道基础上，针对非线性PnP问题推导保护级别算法，直接覆盖飞行器六自由度位姿（位置与姿态），并分析测量冗余、像素级预测不确定性和跑道距离对保护级别的影响。

Result: 通过一个示意性跑道算例展示了保护级别的权衡关系，说明了算法在实际飞行场景中的可用性。

Conclusion: 该工作为视觉导航在航空认证场景下的完整性保障提供了理论框架与实用算法，使未检测故障下的位姿误差有概率性边界可循。

Abstract: Vision-based navigation complements Global Navigation Satellite Systems, but certification demands integrity guarantees that account for faulty measurements. Previous work presented a probabilistic computer vision pipeline for runway-based pose estimation with fault detection inspired by Receiver Autonomous Integrity Monitoring. This work extends that framework by deriving protection levels, which provide probabilistic bounds on pose error that remain valid under undetected faults. We present an algorithm for computing protection levels for the nonlinear Perspective-$n$-Point problem applied to an aviation setting. The algorithm covers all six degrees of freedom of the aircraft pose (position and orientation) directly. We analyze the effect of measurement redundancy, pixel-level prediction uncertainty, and runway distance on the resulting protection levels. To make the results tangible, we demonstrate tradeoffs in the protection levels on an illustrative runway example.

</details>


### [198] [The Impact of Operational-Data Fidelity when Assessing Safety-Critical Autonomous-Vehicle Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10025)
*Kizito Salako,Rabiu Tsoho Muhammad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过扩展保守贝叶斯推断（CBI）技术，研究了操作数据细节不足对软件可靠性评估鲁棒性的影响，并以自动驾驶汽车安全评估为案例，证明低精细度数据可能导致的可靠性声明过于乐观，首次给出了数据保真度影响评估的保守估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键软件的操作历史数据虽然能为可靠性声明提供统计支持，但这些数据往往缺乏足够的细节来描述过去的软件故障事件，导致基于这些数据的可靠性评估无法充分考虑故障的重要特征，进而可能产生误导性的评估结果。

Method: 扩展保守贝叶斯推断（CBI）技术，提出一种原则性的统计方法来检验从细节不足的操作数据中得出的可靠性声明的稳健性，并将其应用于自动驾驶车辆安全评估场景进行实证分析。

Result: 研究发现，从细节不足的数据中得出的可靠性声明可能危险地过于乐观，即使评估者努力保守地使用这些数据也是如此。研究首次给出了数据保真度对评估影响的保守估计，并澄清了为什么尝试保守地使用低保真度数据可能是天真的。

Conclusion: 操作数据的精细度对软件可靠性评估有显著影响，低保真度数据即使被保守使用也可能导致过度乐观的可靠性声明。研究结果与先前关于贝叶斯软件可靠性评估中统计模型保真度影响的工作一致，但进一步明确了问题本质并提供了首个保守的影响估计。

Abstract: For safety-critical software, data from the software's operational past (e.g. a sequence of success and failure events experienced by the software) can provide strong statistical support for reliability claims about the software. However, such data might not describe past software failure events in sufficient detail, and this might leave a reliability assessment (based on this data) unable to account for important features of past software failures. In this paper, by extending conservative Bayesian inference (CBI) techniques used in reliability assessment, we illustrate a principled statistical approach for checking the robustness of reliability claims derived from insufficiently detailed operational data. We demonstrate the extent to which insufficient detail in operational data can undermine software reliability claims in autonomous vehicle (AV) safety assessment scenarios. Reliability claims derived from insufficiently fine-grained data might be dangerously optimistic, despite a concerted effort by an assessor to use such data conservatively during the assessment. While these findings are consistent with previous work on the impact of statistical model fidelity in Bayesian software reliability assessments, our work clarifies why attempts to use low-fidelity data conservatively can be naive, and we give the first conservative estimates of the impact of data fidelity on assessments.

</details>


### [199] [Navigating the Proximity-Safety Balance: Constraint Decomposition for Human Following in Pedestrian Crowds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10056)
*Shiting Gong,Jianpeng Yao,Jinfeng Wang,Marco Pavone,Jiachen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多约束强化学习框架用于人群环境中的目标跟随任务，通过将稀疏任务奖励与独立成本约束分离，并引入人类运动预测不确定性估计，实现了可显式调节的接近-安全平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集人群环境中跟随目标存在接近目标与安全导航之间的固有冲突，现有RL方法将竞争目标编码为单一稠密奖励，导致接近-安全平衡隐式且难以跨条件调节。

Method: 将人类跟随任务分解为稀疏任务奖励和独立成本约束，采用多约束RL公式，每个约束通过具有直接行为含义的成本阈值管理，并将人类运动预测不确定性量化后集成到RL成本中。

Result: 在分布内和分布外场景的广泛实验中，方法相比基线实现了有效的接近-安全平衡；真实机器人部署验证了其在现实场景中的可行性。

Conclusion: 通过显式且可调的成本阈值代替隐式奖励权重，结合不确定性感知的成本设计，能够在密集和不可预测人群中实现更可靠的目标跟随。

Abstract: Following a target human in crowded environments involves an inherent conflict between staying close to the target and navigating safely among surrounding pedestrians and obstacles. This conflict becomes more severe in dense scenarios, where aggressive following risks collisions and conservative margins lead to target loss, especially when pedestrian behaviors are unfamiliar or unpredictable. Existing reinforcement learning (RL) methods typically encode these competing objectives into a single dense reward, but the resulting proximity-safety balance is implicit and difficult to adjust across conditions. To address this, we decompose the human-following task into a sparse task reward and independent cost constraints within a multi-constraint RL formulation, where each constraint is managed through cost thresholds with direct behavioral meaning rather than implicit reward weight ratios, allowing explicit and tunable control over the trade-off. We further quantify the prediction uncertainty of human motions and integrate these estimates into the RL costs to enhance safety under unpredictable conditions. Extensive experiments across both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings demonstrate that our method achieves an effective proximity-safety balance compared to baselines. Real-robot deployment further validates the feasibility of our method in real-world scenarios. More details are available on our project page: https://nav-ps-balance.github.io/.

</details>


### [200] [Whole-Body Planning for Humanoids Navigating Confined Spaces via Self-Collision Avoidance References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10220)
*Carlos Gonzalez,Luis Sentis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三阶段全身规划框架，通过可微碰撞避免和可达性约束生成体积感知的引导路径，用于在高度受限环境中实现人形机器人运动规划，并在Unitree G1上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在高度受限环境中的运动规划面临挑战：传统轨迹优化器在处理密集障碍物和自碰撞约束时容易陷入局部最优，且基于粒子抽象的样条方法难以有效导航大范围碰撞空间。

Method: 提出三阶段全身规划框架：(1)在运动学可达的刚体体积上直接进行运动学路径规划；(2)将可微碰撞避免集成到可达性约束的公式化中，合成体积引导信息；(3)将优化后的轨迹作为高质量参考，训练残差强化学习策略用于在线鲁棒执行。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上进行了验证，覆盖三个超越NIST应急响应标准的基准测试平台，实现了受限约束比(C_r < 1.5)。框架能在12至18秒任务中生成涉及复杂手脚接触的可行轨迹，而标准基线方法无法完成；学习策略在广泛的领域随机化下成功跟踪这些计划。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架通过体积感知的路径规划和强化学习策略相结合，有效解决了高度受限环境下的全身运动规划难题，为复杂狭小空间中的鲁棒人形运动执行提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Humanoid locomotion in highly confined environments requires navigating dense environmental obstacles and complex self-collision bounds while maintaining multi-contact dynamic feasibility. Traditional trajectory optimizers frequently struggle in these restricted spaces, as navigating the large collision space with splines on particle abstractions is insufficient and leads to poor local minima. To address this, we propose a three-stage whole-body planning framework that formulates kinematic path planning directly over kinematically reachable rigid-body volumes. By integrating differentiable collision avoidance into a reachability-constrained formulation, our framework synthesizes volume-informed guides that reliably guide a full-order trajectory optimizer over long horizons. We show that these optimized plans serve as high-quality references to train a residual reinforcement learning policy for robust online execution. We validate our approach on the Unitree G1 humanoid across three benchmark testbeds exceeding NIST emergency response standards, achieving restricted confinement ratios ($C_r < 1.5$). Our framework generates feasible trajectories across 12-to-18-second tasks with complex foot and hand contacts where standard baselines fail, while the learned policy successfully tracks these plans under extensive domain randomization in physics simulation.

</details>


### [201] [FACT: Failure-Aware Causal Training for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10232)
*Quanquan Peng,Yutong Liang,Rui Yan,Nicklas Hansen,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FACT，一种因果世界-动作模型（World-Action Model），通过动作条件化的未来视频和任务进度预测接口，将失败轨迹纳入训练，使模型能够感知成功和失败动作的结果，从而提升策略性能并减少成功偏差的未来幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAMs）大多仅在成功示范上训练，缺乏对坏动作后果的预测能力，导致模型对失败场景的感知不足，生成策略在真实任务中鲁棒性有限。

Method: FACT通过学习动作条件化的未来视频预测和任务进度预测构建因果世界模型。在训练中，引入失败轨迹来监督动作后果，将坏动作转化为有效的未来目标而非丢弃，同时可选地在推理时用进度预测器对候选动作进行打分。

Result: 在仿真和真实双臂操作任务上的大量实验表明，FACT优于多个现有基线，随着失败数据加入训练性能持续提升，并显著减少了在坏动作下的成功偏差未来幻觉。

Conclusion: FACT证明了将失败数据纳入世界模型训练的有效性，为世界-动作模型提供了更全面的物理先验，增强了决策的鲁棒性，也为未来相关研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Recent world-action models (WAMs) show that co-training policies with future prediction can provide physical priors for action generation. Building on the future-prediction ability of video models, many WAMs generate future videos and recover actions with inverse-dynamics models, or use these predicted videos as goal conditions for action generation. In both cases, the world model is trained mostly on successful demonstrations and has little reason to predict the consequences of bad actions. We introduce FACT, a causal World-Action Model that predicts future video and task progress conditioned on the executed action. This action-conditioned interface allows failure rollouts to supervise action consequences, turning bad actions into valid future targets rather than being discarded. Failure-aware training makes the progress predictor aware of both successful and failed action outcomes, which can optionally be used to score sampled action candidates at inference. Extensive experiments on simulation and real-world bimanual manipulation tasks show that FACT outperforms many existing baselines, improves as failure data are incorporated into training, and reduces success-biased future hallucination under bad actions. See more details at https://fact-wam.github.io/

</details>


### [202] [A Neural Network Based Teleoperation for Remote Controlled Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10367)
*Ning Ding,Azim Eskandarian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向车辆直接遥操作的单向遥操作框架，结合波变量（WV）方法保证随机时延下的无源性稳定，以及自适应径向基函数网络（RBFN）在线补偿车辆不确定动态。通过仿真对比PID、LQR、MPC和NMPC验证了RBFN在抗扰性和计算效率上的优势，并利用1/10比例车辆在4G网络下进行了硬件在环实验，验证了系统的实际可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆直接遥操作面临两大核心瓶颈：通信时延导致的不稳定性，以及操作者无法物理感知未建模环境扰动（如气动阻力、侧倾角）和高度非线性的轮胎-路面动力学。现有方法难以同时解决上述问题。

Method: 构建单向遥操作框架，采用波变量方法在随机时延下保证系统无源性稳定；引入自适应RBFN对车辆特定不确定性进行主动补偿，并设计了针对车辆纵横向动力学解耦的自适应律，区别于面向双边机械臂的现有WV-神经网络架构。

Result: 仿真基准测试表明，RBFN相比PID、LQR、MPC和NMPC具有更强的抗未建模扰动鲁棒性，且执行时间比MPC和NMPC低数个数量级；基于1/10比例车辆和4G网络的硬件在环实验验证了系统的实际可行性、安全性和鲁棒轨迹跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的WV+RBFN单向遥操作框架在随机时延和物理道路不确定性条件下具有实际可行性和有效性，且计算开销低，适合资源受限的车辆边缘计算场景，为车辆遥操作提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Direct teleoperation of vehicles faces critical technical bottlenecks: communication latency and the operator's inability to physically perceive unmodeled environmental disturbances (e.g., aerodynamic drag, bank angles) coupled with highly nonlinear tire-road dynamics. To address these challenges, we propose a tailored unilateral teleoperation framework. The system integrates the Wave Variable (WV) approach to passively guarantee stability under stochastic delays, and an adaptive Radial Basis Function Network (RBFN) to actively compensate for vehicle-specific uncertainties. Unlike existing WV-neural network architectures designed for bilateral robotic arms, our framework features decoupled adaptive laws specifically designed for vehicle longitudinal and lateral dynamics. Furthermore, compared to model-heavy predictive controllers, the model-free RBFN offers rapid online adaptation without heavy computational overhead. Building upon our preliminary theoretical formulation, this brief paper presents comprehensive comparative analyses and real-world hardware validations. Simulation benchmarks against PID, LQR, MPC, and NMPC demonstrate that the RBFN achieves superior robustness against unmodeled disturbances while requiring orders of magnitude less execution time than MPC and NMPC, making it ideal for resource-constrained vehicle edge computing. Finally, hardware-in-the-loop experiments using a 1/10th scale vehicle over a 4G network validate the system's practical feasibility, safety, and robust trajectory tracking under physical road uncertainties.

</details>


### [203] [Real-World Cooperative Bimanual Dexterous Grasp of Large Objects from Single-View Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10383)
*Ziming Li,Mingxuan Wu,Jiaqi Zhang,Hongfei Li,Yan Gan,Deqiang Ouyang,Ning Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个真实世界的双臂灵巧抓取框架，包含多模态数据集、基于DDPM的抓取配置生成模块，以及结合运动规划与在线抓取优化的执行策略，能够从单视角输入生成可执行的双臂抓取动作，在真实双机械臂上实现了对未知物体的高成功率抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂灵巧抓取大物体是机器人操作中的关键挑战，但现有研究多聚焦于顺序操作而非协同抓取，且相关方法大多局限于仿真环境。这主要是因为获取完整3D物体模型困难以及生成物理上合理的抓取动作具有挑战性。

Method: 提出一个真实世界双臂抓取框架，包括三个核心部分：1）一个包含关节角度、视觉观测和力信号的多模态数据集；2）基于去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）的模块，从分割点云生成关节级抓取配置；3）集成运动规划与在线抓取细化的执行策略，确保物理稳定性和可行性。该框架仅需单视角输入即可合成可执行的双臂抓取。

Result: 在双机械臂上的实验表明，该方法对具有不同几何形状和姿态的未知物体实现了较高的抓取成功率，消融研究验证了系统各关键组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 该框架有效减少了对完整3D物体模型的依赖，实现了稳定的真实世界双臂抓取性能，为双臂协同抓取任务提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Bimanual dexterous grasping of large objects is a critical challenge in robotic manipulation. However, most existing studies focus on sequential manipulation rather than cooperative grasping, and methods addressing such bimanual tasks have largely been limited to simulation. These limitations stem from the difficulty of acquiring full 3D object models and generating physically plausible grasping actions. To fill this gap, we propose a real-world bimanual grasping framework that includes: a multimodal dataset capturing joint angles, visual observations and force signals; a Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model (DDPM)-based module that generates joint-level grasp configurations from segmented point clouds; and an execution strategy that integrates motion planning with online grasp refinement to ensure physical stability and feasibility. Our approach enables the synthesis of executable bimanual grasps from single-view inputs, reducing dependence on complete 3D object models and ensuring stable real-world performance. Experiments on a dual-arm robot demonstrate high success rates across unseen objects with varying geometries and poses, and ablation studies confirm the contributions of key components of our system.

</details>


### [204] [Hip Energized Monopedal Hopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10387)
*Shane Rozen-Levy,Griffon McMahon,Daniel Koditschek*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对无俯仰锁定平面单足机器人的新型步态策略，通过移动质心位置增强俯仰稳定力矩来抵消阻尼导致的能量损失，实现稳定行走。混合平均分析给出固定点和特征值的闭式解，仿真与物理实验（Penn Jerboa，速度1.02-1.77 m/s）均验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PD+前馈控制器稳定俯仰时产生的反作用力矩未被有效利用，而阻尼带来的能量损失需要额外的控制策略来补偿。作者希望利用俯仰稳定力矩来恢复步态能量损耗，从而在无需额外驱动的情况下实现更高效的稳定行走。

Method: 提出一种通过移动质量中心位置来增强俯仰稳定力矩的控制器，从而向步态注入能量；设计新的步态策略在径向与角向自由度之间分配能量，以抵消耗散损失并实现用户指定的稳态前进速度与顶点高度平衡；采用混合平均（hybrid averaging）分析方法推导步态固定点与特征值的闭式表达式。

Result: 在通用5连杆双足机器人及Penn Jerboa的精确模型上进行了仿真验证，结果与解析预测高度吻合；在Penn Jerboa实体机器人上实现稳定行走，速度范围达1.02至1.77 m/s（5.10至8.85腿长/秒），实验结果与数学分析有效对应。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，俯仰稳定力矩可以成为一种可调用的能量来源以抵消步态阻尼损耗，混合平均分析为理解物理与控制参数对步态性能的影响提供了有力工具，理论与实验的一致性证明了该步态策略的实用性与有效性。

Abstract: We present a novel stepping strategy for pitch unlocked planar monopeds where the reaction torques from stabilizing pitch with a conventional PD + feedfoward controller are recruited to counteract energetic losses from damping. By moving the location of the mass center, our controller increases the pitch stabilization torque, thereby adding energy to the gait. A new stepping policy adjusts the distribution of energy between the radial and angular degrees of freedom to counteract dissipative losses and achieve a user specified balance between steady state fore-aft speed and apex height. Hybrid averaging analysis yields closed form expressions for the fixed points and eigenvalues of the resulting gait, lending insight into the interplay between the physical and control parameters' influence on performance. Simulation studies on a generic 5 link biped and a careful model of the Penn Jerboa reveal a useful correspondence to these analytical predictions. Physical experiments on the Penn Jerboa exhibit stable locomotion with speeds ranging from 1.02 m/s to 1.77 m/s (5.10 leg lengths/s to 8.85 leg lengths/s) in a manner effectively approximated by the mathematical analysis.

</details>


### [205] [PBD-AG: Persistent Baseline-Delta Active Graphs with Uncertainty-Aware Inspection for Long-Horizon Service Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10449)
*Shuo Bao,Wei Dong,Shuyue Zhang,Ming Shang,Yuchen Huang,Han Yu,Chengjie Xu,Yiheng Bi,Kai Sun,Fuchun Sun,Xinzhou Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PBD-AG提出了一种"基线-动态"（Baseline-Delta）主动图框架，将机器人已验证的稳定固定场景与可修订的动态物体事件解耦，自主构建可持续更新的世界模型，支持长时间跨度的服务机器人感知任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时间跨度的服务机器人需要持久的、可自主构建并在物体变化时进行修订的世界模型。现有方法存在定位与观测误差累积、静态场景无法捕获物体变化、或缺乏可验证3D几何证据的局限性。

Method: PBD-AG框架将结构基线（通过机载探索自主引导）与动态物体事件解耦；利用几何可见性门控缓解遮挡导致的错误删除；采用图条件策略选择检查视点，平衡目标覆盖率、行进成本、碰撞风险和冗余观测。

Result: 在多种环境及受控动态评估下的仿真实验中，PBD-AG在聚合粗固定F1分数上优于能力匹配的对照组，并在身份连续性和事件召回方面表现更强；物理机器人定性演示进一步展示了与机载感知的集成能力。

Conclusion: PBD-AG提供了可追溯、可验证的世界模型，有效解决长时程机器人感知中场景静态描述与动态变化之间的矛盾，为持久化世界建模提供了一种可解释、可更新的新范式。

Abstract: Long-horizon service robots require persistent world models that can be built autonomously in unseen environments and revised as task-relevant objects change. Existing methods rely on online mapping, which accumulates localization and observation errors, static scene representations that cannot capture persistent object changes, or holistic vision-language predictions that lack verifiable 3D geometric evidence. We present PBD-AG, a persistent baseline-delta active graph framework that decouples robot-verified stable fixtures from revisable dynamic object events. Under our framework, the robot autonomously bootstraps the structural baseline from onboard exploration and inspects discovered fixtures to ground hierarchical object beliefs. PBD-AG maintains reliability-weighted object states over geometry, semantics, identity, existence, and support relations, utilizing a geometric visibility gate to mitigate false deletions under occlusion. Inspection viewpoints are selected by a graph-conditioned policy that balances target coverage, travel cost, collision risk, and redundant observation. Simulation experiments in multiple environments and under controlled dynamic evaluation show higher aggregate coarse-fixture F1 than capability-matched controls, as well as stronger identity continuity and event recall. A qualitative physical-robot demonstration further illustrates integration with onboard sensing, providing a traceable world model for long-horizon robotic perception.The project page of PBD-AG is available at https://shuobao214.github.io/PBD-AG/

</details>


### [206] [Lost in Reconstruction: Aligning Action Representations with Language in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10484)
*Li Wenjie,Yash Jangir,Ignacy Stepka,Yash Agarwal,Marion Kipsang,Yonatan Bisk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了SALT（语义对齐动作分词器），通过在VQ-VAE风格分词器上增加辅助目标——让冻结的视觉-语言模型从量化动作潜在表示中恢复指令——来保留动作轨迹中的动词接地信息，在SimplerEnv上实现了71.9%的平均成功率，远优于仅重建的分词器（42.7%）和FAST（31.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作模型（VLAs）在原始动作空间中仅通过L1/L2损失优化重建，但数值接近并不等同于语言上有意义的区分。研究发现在BridgeV2数据集上，动作轨迹包含超出视觉状态变化的动词接地信息，而仅重建的离散分词会系统性侵蚀这些信息，导致语言条件控制性能下降。

Method: SALT在VQ-VAE风格分词器的基础上引入辅助目标：要求一个冻结的视觉-语言模型能够从量化后的动作潜在表示中恢复出回合指令（episode instruction），从而确保离散化过程保留语义相关的动作信息，使动作表示与语言含义对齐。

Result: 使用SALT训练的策略在SimplerEnv中达到71.9%的平均成功率，对比仅重建的VQ-VAE分词器为42.7%，FAST为31.2%。SALT还发展出了动词专用的离散编码，同时保持了重建保真度。

Conclusion: 机器人动作轨迹本身提供了语言接地（language grounding）的来源，在动作表示中保留这种结构能够显著提升语言条件控制性能。SALT证明了语义对齐的分词策略比纯重建策略更有效地捕捉动作背后的语言含义。

Abstract: Action verbs describe not only the physical outcomes of actions, but also how those actions are performed. Yet action representations in vision-language-action models (VLAs) are typically optimized for reconstruction under L1/L2 losses in raw action space, where numerical proximity need not reflect linguistically meaningful distinctions. On BridgeV2, we show that action trajectories contain verb-grounding information beyond visual state changes, and that reconstruction-only discrete tokenization systematically erodes this information. To address this problem, we introduce SALT, a Semantically ALigned action Tokenizer that augments a VQ-VAE-style tokenizer with an auxiliary objective requiring a frozen vision-language model to recover the episode instruction from quantized action latents. Policies trained with SALT achieve 71.9% average success in SimplerEnv, compared with 42.7% for a reconstruction-only VQ-VAE tokenizer and 31.2% for FAST. SALT also develops verb-specialized codes while maintaining reconstruction fidelity. These results show that robot action trajectories provide a source of language grounding and that preserving this structure in action representations can substantially improve language-conditioned control.

</details>


### [207] [JitTrack: Onboard Multi-Object Tracking Against Viewpoint Jitter for Agile UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10485)
*Yachun Shan,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出JitTrack，一个面向敏捷无人机机载多目标跟踪的主动跟踪框架，通过语义细化、运动感知查询修正和运动启发去噪训练策略，结合感知-规划-控制闭环管线，解决视角抖动下的目标关联和实时跟踪问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机多目标跟踪方法主要基于离线基准评估，忽略了真实机载部署中的实际需求，如对相机运动（视角抖动）的鲁棒性以及主动目标跟随能力。无人机快速姿态变化会导致目标跨帧位移显著，造成目标关联不准确和跟踪性能下降。

Method: JitTrack基于查询式Transformer跟踪器，引入三大核心组件：1）语义细化模块以改进新出现目标的检测；2）运动感知查询修正以补偿视角抖动造成的目标错位；3）运动启发去噪训练策略模拟相机运动模式以提供鲁棒监督。同时开发了感知-规划-控制闭环跟踪管线，支持敏捷无人机上的无碰撞、物理可行的目标跟随。

Result: 在公开无人机MOT基准上的大量实验表明，相对于基线方法取得了一致的性能提升；真实飞行实验验证了JitTrack在视角抖动条件下进行鲁棒机载视觉跟踪的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: JitTrack有效解决了无人机在视角抖动条件下的机载多目标跟踪难题，通过结合运动感知的设计和完整的部署管线，实现了从仿真到真实飞行的性能验证，为无人机主动视觉跟踪提供了实用化解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-object tracking (MOT) onboard agile unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) remains challenging due to severe viewpoint jitter induced by camera ego-motion. Rapid attitude changes during flight often lead to significant target displacement across frames, causing inaccurate target association and degraded tracking performance. Existing UAV MOT methods are primarily evaluated on offline benchmarks and seldom address the practical requirements of real-world onboard deployment, including robustness to camera motion and active target following.
