To address these challenges, we propose JitTrack, an active onboard multi-object tracking framework that accommodates drone dynamics and camera ego-motion. Built upon a query-based transformer tracker, JitTrack introduces semantic refinement to improve the detection of emerging targets, motion-aware query rectification to compensate for target misalignment caused by viewpoint jitter, and a motion-inspired denoising training strategy that simulates camera motion patterns for robust supervision. Furthermore, we develop a perception-planning-control closed-loop tracking pipeline for real-world deployment, enabling collision-free and physically feasible target following on agile UAVs. Extensive experiments on public UAV MOT benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over the baseline method, while real-world flight experiments validate the effectiveness and practicality of JitTrack for robust onboard visual tracking under viewpoint jitter.

</details>


### [208] [Nonlinear Model Predictive Control via Sequential Convex Programming for Drone-to-Drone Docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10542)
*Neeraj Balachandar,Shriram Hari,Vishnu R. Unni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于序贯凸规划（SCP）的模型预测控制框架，用于多旋翼飞行器在扰动驱动目标运动下的自主空中对接，通过降阶非线性模型结合扰动状态、噪声状态估计和高保真MuJoCo仿真验证，实现了低至10度锥半角、最高0.5标准差风力扰动下的可靠对接性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多旋翼飞行器在扰动驱动的目标运动下进行自主空中对接是一个受约束的非线性轨迹优化挑战，现有的方法难以在动态目标、噪声测量和外部扰动同时存在时保证可靠的对接性能，因此需要一种鲁棒的轨迹优化与执行框架。

Method: 将对接任务建模为基于降阶非线性模型并增广扰动状态的有限时域最优控制问题；采用序贯凸规划（SCP）在滚动时域框架内求解，生成动态可行的对接轨迹；结合带噪声测量下的状态估计实现鲁棒相对运动预测，并在高保真刚体MuJoCo仿真环境中验证。

Result: 在静止和匀速目标运动场景下均能可靠收敛到对接接口并满足几何捕获约束；对接锥违规可忽略，终端状态误差在预设容差内；低至10度锥半角仍能保持一致的对接性能；在标准差达0.5的风力扰动下仍保持鲁棒操作，同时保持有界的接近速度和稳定的控制输出。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，基于SCP的轨迹优化框架在估计不确定性下对扰动鲁棒的空中对接是有效的，能够同时处理目标运动、外部扰动和感知噪声等实际挑战。

Abstract: Autonomous mid-air docking of multi-rotor vehicles under disturbance-driven target motion poses a constrained non-linear trajectory optimization challenge. This work formulates the docking task as a finite-horizon optimal control problem based on a reduced-order nonlinear model augmented with disturbance states. The resulting problem is solved using sequential convex programming within a receding-horizon framework to generate dynamically feasible docking trajectories. State estimation with noisy measurements is incorporated to enable robust relative motion prediction, while trajectory execution is validated in a high-fidelity rigid-body MuJoCo simulation environment. The proposed framework is evaluated for stationary and constant-velocity target motions, demonstrating reliable convergence to the docking interface while satisfying geometric capture constraints. Quantitatively, the method maintains negligible docking-cone violations and terminal state errors within prescribed tolerances, and achieves consistent, safe docking performance for cone half-angles as low as 10 degrees. Robust operation is observed for wind disturbance levels up to a standard deviation of 0.5, while preserving bounded approach velocities and stable control effort. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of the SCP-based trajectory optimization framework for disturbance-robust aerial docking under estimation uncertainty.

</details>


### [209] [BooST: Bridging Semantics and Motions for Efficient Skill Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10600)
*Jusuk Lee,Daesol Cho,Jonghun Shin,Seungyeon Yoo,Jonghae Park,Taekbeom Lee,H. Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BooST是一个两阶段技能抽象框架，通过跨模态VQ-VAE统一捕获语义意图与运动动力学，并蒸馏为轻量级策略，实现少样本适应、跨域迁移和动态视觉干扰鲁棒性，兼顾实际部署效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人技能学习方法要么只捕获高层语义意图（what），要么只捕获低层运动动力学（how），难以同时满足跨任务泛化、跨域迁移、视觉与动力学鲁棒性以及部署效率这三项需求，导致技能迁移不完整，需要大量域内数据进行下游适应。

Method: BooST采用两阶段框架：第一阶段利用跨模态VQ-VAE同时捕获语义意图和运动动力学，形成统一的技能表示；第二阶段将该表示蒸馏为轻量级策略，用于高效的下游新任务适应。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人上的大量实验表明，BooST在少样本适应、跨域技能迁移和对动态视觉干扰物的鲁棒性方面均取得优异表现，同时保持轻量且表达能力强的设计，适用于实际部署。

Conclusion: BooST通过显式桥接语义与运动两个层面，同时满足技能泛化、鲁棒性和部署效率三重要求，为机器人技能抽象与迁移提供了一种更完整的解决方案。

Abstract: Skill abstraction---the process of learning reusable and temporally extended behaviors---has emerged as a key paradigm for improving sample efficiency and generalization in robot learning. For efficient skill transfer to real robots, learned skills must generalize across tasks and domains, remain robust to visual and dynamic perturbations, and be efficient enough for practical deployment. However, existing methods typically satisfy only a subset of these properties, as they capture either high-level semantic intent (what) or low-level motion dynamics (how). This incomplete skill transfer yields weak priors for policy learning, thereby demanding substantial in-domain data for downstream adaptation. To address these challenges, we introduce BooST, a two-stage framework that explicitly bridges semantics and motions to satisfy all three desiderata. BooST first leverages a cross-modal VQ-VAE to capture both semantic intent and motion dynamics, yielding a unified skill representation. It then distills this representation into a lightweight policy for efficient downstream adaptation to new tasks. Extensive experiments across simulation and real-robot settings demonstrate that BooST achieves superior few-shot adaptation, cross-domain skill transfer, and robustness to dynamic visual distractors, while maintaining a lightweight yet expressive design suitable for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [210] [Toward the Cognitive--Physical Limits of Embodied Intelligence through a World-Model-Centric Autonomous Racing Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10618)
*Zitong Shan,Baichuan Lou,Yanxin Zhou,Shuge Wu,Xianqi He,Bolin Zhao,Sheng Zhao,Zhouheng Li,Chee Kiong Ong,King Ho Holden Li,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以世界模型为核心的自动驾驶赛车智能体，通过闭环优化同时建模认知和物理极限，在真实车辆最高256.3 km/h速度下采集数据，并在全尺度模拟中达到88.3%的交互成功率，展现了边界感知方法对具身智能安全部署的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数具身智能系统在保守安全裕度或中等交互条件下评估，极端条件下的能力边界理解不足。自动驾驶赛车结合高频感知、对抗交互、近饱和动力学和严格安全约束，为探索认知与物理耦合极限提供了严苛测试平台。

Method: 提出世界模型为中心的自动驾驶赛车框架：从近极限成功与失败中学习预测性世界模型，捕获交互演化、自车动力学和可行运动边界；将世界状态构建、前瞻推理和近极限控制耦合在闭环细化过程中，实现持续优化。

Result: 真实车辆自主赛车系统在高达256.3 km/h速度和26.8 m/s²峰值横向加速度下保持稳健定位与感知；全尺度模拟赛车中，训练好的世界模型智能体在多种挑战性场景下达到88.3%的交互成功率；闭环细化进一步提升了认知-物理极限利用率、故障恢复能力以及跨条件和未知赛道的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了边界感知方法论：世界模型帮助具身智能体表示、预测并持续细化自身能力边界，从而为实现更安全的实体世界部署提供可行路径。

Abstract: Embodied artificial intelligence aims to develop agents that perceive, reason, and act through continuous interaction with the physical world. However, most embodied systems are still evaluated within conservative safety margins or moderate interaction regimes, leaving their capability boundaries under extreme conditions insufficiently understood. Autonomous racing provides a stringent testbed by combining high-frequency localization and perception, adversarial interaction, near-saturated vehicle dynamics, and strict safety constraints. Existing systems push high-speed performance but rarely model and refine cognitive and physical limits jointly. Here we show that a world-model-centric autonomous racing agent provides a concrete step toward exploring these coupled limits. The framework learns predictive world models from near-limit successes and failures to capture interaction evolution, ego dynamics, and feasible-motion boundaries, coupling world-state construction, future-aware reasoning, and near-limit control in a closed-loop refinement process. Training data were collected from real-vehicle autonomous racing, where the onboard system maintained robust localization and perception at speeds up to 256.3 km/h and peak lateral acceleration of 26.8 m/s$^2$. In full-scale simulated racing, the well trained world-model-centric agent achieves an 88.3% interaction success rate across various challenging simulated racing scenarios. Closed-loop refinement of the world model and policy further improved utilization of cognitive-physical limits, recovery from failure modes, and generalization across varying conditions and unseen circuits. These results suggest a boundary-aware methodology in which world models help embodied agents represent, predict, and continually refine their capability boundaries for safer real-world deployment.

</details>


### [211] [When Your State Estimator Has Lost The Plot: Detecting Estimator Failures Via Spectral Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10623)
*Christian Lanegger,Helen Oleynikova,Roland Siegwart,Michael Pantic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种传感器无关的频域内省方法，通过分析速度估计的功率谱分布来评估估计器健康状态，在三种不同的状态估计框架（视觉-惯性、LiDAR-惯性、雷达-惯性里程计）上检测51%-58%的故障，精确率60%-84%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的机载状态估计对机器人安全运行至关重要，但未建模扰动（如传感器混叠或分布外噪声）会导致估计器性能退化甚至完全失效。现有不确定性度量（如协方差）依赖理想假设且过于自信，而数据驱动方法受限于训练数据分布，缺乏轻量级、可泛化的内省机制来评估估计质量。

Method: 提出一种传感器无关的内省方法，通过对最近速度估计的频域功率分布进行分析来评估估计器健康状态。使用户外飞行数据评估多个频域指标（信号功率、频谱带宽、熵），涵盖视觉-惯性、LiDAR-惯性和雷达-惯性三种里程计框架。

Result: 健康与退化估计之间存在一致的频谱功率差异，可检测51%-58%的标记故障，精确率达60%-84%，且跨越三种根本不同的状态估计框架均有效。

Conclusion: 即使是简单的频域分析也能作为轻量级内省工具，补充现有鲁棒性技术，适用于真实机器人部署，并为未来研究开辟了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Reliable onboard state estimation is essential for safe robotic operation, yet unmodeled disturbances, such as sensor aliasing or out-of-distribution noise, still cause estimators to degrade or fail completely. While many methods aim to improve estimator robustness, only a few provide introspective mechanisms to assess estimate quality. Existing uncertainty measures, such as covariances, rely on idealized assumptions and tend to be overconfident, and more recent data-driven approaches are typically tied to their training data distributions.
  We propose a sensor-agnostic introspective method that assesses estimator health by analyzing the frequency-domain power distribution of recent velocity estimates. The method is evaluated using outdoor flight data from an aerial robot running visual-inertial, LiDAR-inertial, and radar-inertial odometry. The dataset includes multiple estimator failures, enabling analysis of several frequency-domain indicators, such as signal power, spectral bandwidth, and entropy. We observe consistent spectral power differences between healthy and degraded estimates, allowing detection of 51%-58% of labeled failures with 60%-84% precision across three fundamentally different state estimation frameworks. Our results show that even a simple frequency-domain analysis of a state estimator's output can serve as a lightweight introspective tool to complement existing robustness techniques in real-world robotic deployments, and opens promising avenues for future investigation.

</details>


### [212] [OAA: Three Phases of Vocal Guidance in Human-Drone Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10651)
*Allan Henry,Christian Graff,Solange Rossato,José-Ernesto Gomez-Balderas,Sylvain Huet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过两种实验配置（人手引导和无人机遥控）的动捕和语音数据，发现人类空间引导语音一致地组织为三个阶段：定向（Orientation）、接近（Approach）和调整（Adjustment），可通过变点检测自动识别，并具有统计显著性和跨配置的可重复词汇特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数语音控制机器人系统将语音指令视为静态流，忽略了引导者沟通行为随任务进展而动态演变的事实，缺乏对引导阶段结构的理解，难以实现真正自适应的语音遥操作控制。

Method: 采集两种配置下的动捕和语音数据（人手引导10组、无人机游戏手柄遥控29组），通过3D轨迹信号上的变点检测自动识别阶段，用Kruskal-Wallis检验进行统计验证，并分析词汇家族和停顿特征在不同阶段的分布模式。

Result: 自发语音引导一致地组织为三个运动学和语言学上截然不同的阶段；三种词汇家族在两种配置中均可复现：旋转词汇标记定向阶段、平移词汇在定向阶段稀少、弱化词在调整阶段累积；结合句间停顿可标记定向边界，而语速单独无法标记该边界。

Conclusion: 三阶段结构在两种截然不同的运动接口条件下均出现，表明它是人类空间引导的内在属性而非实验设置的产物；该发现对基于操作员活动感知（OAA）的自适应控制在语音引导遥操作中的应用具有重要启示。

Abstract: Voice-guided teleoperation requires systems that adapt to the evolving dynamics of human guidance. Yet most voice-controlled robot systems treat spoken commands as a stationary stream, ignoring how the guide's communicative behavior changes as the task progresses. Using motion capture and speech data from two experimental configurations, humanhuman guidance (finger pointing, N =10 dyads) and humandrone teleoperation (gamepad control, N =29 dyads), we show that spontaneous vocal guidance consistently organizes into three kinematically and linguistically distinct phases: Orientation, Approach, and Adjustment. These phases are identified automatically via change point detection on 3D trajectory signals, and validated statistically (Kruskal-Wallis, p<.001). Three lexical families replicate across configurations: rotation vocabulary marks Orientation, translation vocabulary is scarce there, and attenuators accumulate toward Adjustment. Together with inter-utterance silence, these cues mark the Orientation boundary that speech rate alone leaves unmarked. The same three-phase structure emerges in both configurations despite radically different motor interfaces, suggesting it is an intrinsic property of human spatial guidance rather than an artifact of the experimental setup. We discuss implications for OAA-aware adaptive control in voice-guided teleoperation.

</details>


### [213] [Robust Sliding Mode and Admittance Control of Underactuated Aerial Manipulators for Contact-Based Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10656)
*Tareq Aziz Alqutami,Yvan Petillot,Matthew W. Dunnigan,Mustafa Suphi Erden*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对欠驱动六旋翼+1自由度机械臂的鲁棒控制框架，用于持续接触式工业检测。该框架将积分增强滑模控制（SMC）与导纳控制相结合，实现轨迹跟踪与力调节，通过软件在环仿真验证了其优越性，力调节RMSE为0.12N，可稳定施加高达20N的接触力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触式工业检测需要飞行平台在保持稳定交互的同时抑制扰动，而欠驱动空中机械臂因飞行器姿态与力生成之间的动态耦合带来显著控制挑战。

Method: 采用积分增强滑模控制（SMC）进行轨迹跟踪，结合导纳控制律实现力调节；接触力被映射为前馈姿态项，1-DoF机械臂主动补偿倾斜以保持表面对齐。

Result: 软件在环仿真表明，SMC方法在跟踪精度和耦合抑制方面优于传统PID；交互策略实现了0.12N的力调节RMSE，并能稳定施加高达20N的接触力。

Conclusion: 所提出的控制框架验证了其在稳定、可靠的接触式工业检测中的有效性，能够实现精确的力调节和强扰动抑制能力。

Abstract: Contact-based industrial inspection requires aerial platforms to maintain stable interaction while rejecting disturbances. Underactuated aerial manipulators present control challenges due to the dynamic coupling between vehicle attitude and force generation. This paper proposes a robust control framework for an underactuated hexarotor equipped with a 1-DoF manipulator to perform sustained contact inspection. The architecture integrates integral-augmented Sliding Mode Control (SMC) for trajectory tracking with an admittance control law for force regulation. The contact force is mapped to a feedforward attitude term, while the 1-DoF arm actively compensates for the tilt to maintain surface alignment. Software-in-the-loop simulations demonstrate that the SMC-based approach achieves superior tracking and coupling rejection compared to traditional PID. Furthermore, the interaction strategy achieved precise force regulation with an RMSE of 0.12N and was able to stably exert up to 20N force, confirming the system's efficacy for stable, reliable contact-based inspection.

</details>


### [214] [TCAM for Autonomous Deformable Manipulation: The RMC2 Champion System for WBCD 2026 Track 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10718)
*Guangrui Shen,Zhili He,Shigang Wang,Yuanjun Sun,Qing Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RMC2团队提出了基于TCAM框架的全自动可变形操作（T恤装载与印刷区整平）系统，在WBCD 2026 Track 4竞赛中获得第一名，25件T恤平均每件约23秒完成装载，其中22件达到表面光滑度要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞赛强烈激励全自主执行，任务涉及单层分离、可变形传输、精确定位和接触丰富的表面调整等多个复杂环节，这对策略学习提出了很高的物理交互复杂性要求，需要系统性降低这些复杂性。

Method: 围绕TCAM框架构建全自主系统，核心设计原则是让硬件、感知、数据和学习的联合设计共同降低策略需要处理的物理交互复杂度。具体包括：定制3D打印夹具用于单层织物分离；腕部四摄像头配置（上部鱼眼用于任务上下文，下部RGB用于近距离夹爪-布料接触观察）；结合便携UMI演示与部署平台上的真实机器人演示；TCAM闭环分析每个轨迹的物理影响因素，驱动定向数据采集和策略微调；策略从多视角VLA骨干输出30步末端执行器delta位姿动作块。

Result: 在最终竞赛中，系统以平均约23秒/次的效率装载25件T恤，其中22件达到要求的表面光滑度，在Track 4中获得第一名。

Conclusion: 通过硬件、感知、数据与学习的联合优化，加上TCAM因果分析驱动的闭环迭代，可以有效降低可变形操作任务的物理交互复杂度，实现全自主的高性能表现。

Abstract: This technical report describes the RMC2 Team's champion solution for the WBCD 2026 Track 4: Deformable Manipulation Challenge. The task requires a robot to pick a single T-shirt from a stack, load it onto a printing pallet, align the collar with a target area, and smooth the printing region, a sequence that involves single-layer separation, deformable transport, precise placement, and contact-rich surface adjustment. The competition strongly incentivizes fully autonomous execution, motivating the development of an autonomous solution. We built a fully autonomous system around the TCAM (TermiBrain Causal Action Model) framework, with the design principle that hardware, perception, data, and learning should jointly reduce the physical interaction complexity the policy must handle. A custom 3D-printed gripper designed for single-layer fabric separation improves picking reliability on a dual-arm ARX X5 platform. A wrist-centric four-camera setup pairs upper fisheye cameras for task-level context with lower RGB cameras for close-range gripper-cloth contact observation. We combine portable UMI-style demonstrations with real-robot demonstrations collected on the deployable platform to provide both broad manipulation priors and deployment-specific dynamics. TCAM ties these components into a closed loop: each trajectory is analyzed to identify the physical factors contributing to its outcome, driving targeted data recollection and policy fine-tuning. The policy outputs 30-step end-effector delta-pose action chunks from a multi-view VLA backbone. In the final competition, our system loaded 25 T-shirts at an average of approximately 23 seconds per attempt, with 22 achieving the required surface smoothness, securing first place in Track 4.

</details>


### [215] [Embodied Multimodal Grounding for Open-Vocabulary Mobile Manipulation via Semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10756)
*Huosen Ou,Dongni Song,Yuncong Wang,Tao Zhou,Yiding Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种具身多模态接地框架，融合主动多视角语义3D高斯泼溅（Semantic-3DGS）、可达性感知的基座定位和基于扩散的视觉-语言-动作策略，用于开放词汇目标的少样本操作，在真实机器人实验中显著优于现有VLA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身移动操作需要语言、视觉观察、三维场景结构和动作可行性在执行前对齐，但现有方法在杂乱环境、遮挡、视角变化和形态约束下表现不佳，缺乏显式的、可刷新的3D语义接地。

Method: 提出一个端到端具身多模态接地框架，包含三个关键组件：任务驱动的局部Semantic-3DGS作为共享接口（连接主动感知、语言条件3D定位、障碍感知场景推理、基座准备和动作模型语义条件）；可达性感知的基座定位；扩散式视觉-语言-动作策略。为了保留预训练动作先验，3D语义线索仅注入到后期的动作专家模块中。

Result: 在50次真实机器人试验中，完整系统在长时程任务上达到60%成功率（对比PointVLA的40%和DexVLA的28%）；在重度杂乱操作中达到74%成功率（对比单视角变体的52%和PointVLA的46%）；在75cm高度偏移下保持75%成功率，并消除了照片诱导的假抓取。

Conclusion: 显式、可刷新的3D语义接地能够改善杂乱环境、遮挡、视角变化和形态约束下的操作鲁棒性，验证了Semantic-3DGS作为共享接口在具身操作中的有效性。

Abstract: Embodied mobile manipulation requires language, visual observations, three-dimensional scene structure, and action feasibility to be aligned before execution. We study open-vocabulary target grounding with few-shot manipulation in local household workspaces and present an embodied multimodal grounding framework that integrates active multi-view Semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting (Semantic-3DGS), reachability-aware base positioning, and a diffusion-based vision-language-action policy. A task-driven local Semantic-3DGS serves as a shared interface across active sensing, language-conditioned 3D localization, obstacle-aware scene reasoning, base preparation, and semantic conditioning of the action model. To preserve pretrained action priors, the 3D semantic cues are injected only into the late action-expert blocks. In expanded 50-trial real-robot evaluations against representative vision-language-action (VLA) approaches, the full system achieves 60% long-horizon success compared with 40% for PointVLA and 28% for DexVLA, and reaches 74% success in heavily cluttered manipulation compared with 52% for the single-view variant and 46% for PointVLA. It also maintains 75% success under a 75 cm height shift and eliminates photo-induced false grasps. These results indicate that explicit, refreshable 3D semantic grounding can improve robustness under clutter, occlusion, viewpoint variation, and embodiment constraints.

</details>


### [216] [JEPA-WAM: Stage-Level Joint-Embedding Prediction for World-Action Models in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10780)
*Xiao Liu,Yuguang Yang,Xi Wang,Kai Jiang,Cheng Chi,Yong Xu,Wenchao Ding,Yilun Chen,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出JEPA-WAM，通过Stage-JEPA（基于目标条件的JEPA预测器）增强基于Motus的世界动作模型，以显式建模任务阶段级语义未来，在50个RoboTwin 2.0任务中达到90.25%的成功率，并将平均执行步骤减少5.97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用机器人策略通常将未来表示为固定、短期的视频-动作块，只能捕捉局部场景演变，但缺乏对任务从当前阶段推进到下一阶段的阶段级语义未来的显式描述。

Method: 区分两种互补的未来：短期物理未来（局部场景演变）和阶段级语义未来（任务进展）。提出JEPA-WAM，将基于Motus的世界动作模型与Stage-JEPA相结合，后者使用冻结的V-JEPA2编码器提取当前状态表示，并预测下一推断阶段的潜在目标。

Result: 在50个RoboTwin 2.0任务的干净和随机化环境中，JEPA-WAM达到90.25%的整体成功率，成功轨迹的平均执行步数比最强基线减少5.97%。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模阶段级语义未来，JEPA-WAM在任务成功率和执行效率上均优于现有方法，验证了区分并建模两种互补未来对通用机器人策略的有效性。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies aim to map multimodal observations and linguistic task instructions to actions across diverse tasks. However, existing methods typically represent the future as a fixed, short video-action chunk. This short-term future captures local scene evolution for action execution, but it does not explicitly describe the stage-level future that specifies how a task should progress from its current stage to the next. We therefore distinguish two complementary futures for robot manipulation: a short-term physical future to capture local scene evolution and a stage-level semantic future to represent task progress. We introduce JEPA-WAM, which augments a Motus-based World Action Model (WAM) with Stage-JEPA, a goal-conditioned Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) predictor. Given the current observation and task instruction, Stage-JEPA uses a frozen V-JEPA2 encoder to extract the current-state representation and predicts the latent target of the next inferred stage. Across 50 RoboTwin 2.0 tasks in clean and randomized environments, JEPA-WAM achieves 90.25% overall success and reduces the mean number of execution steps in successful rollouts by 5.97% relative to the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [217] [Dual Stress: Runtime Safety Monitoring for Safety-Constrained MPC Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10791)
*Jamil Chahine,Wenqi Cai,John Abanes,Anthony Tzes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于KKT乘子的"对偶应力"危险信号，用于自主导航的运行时危险监测，并与传统几何检测器相比，在物理仿真测试中能互补性地标记更多未被几何检测器发现的碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的运行时危险监测器仅依赖几何量（如预测间隙、碰撞时间、所需减速度），忽略了模型预测控制器在每个控制步计算出的KKT乘子这一额外信息通道，后者能够度量为维持安全所付出的边际控制努力。

Method: 将KKT乘子的水平加权求和定义为"对偶应力"信号，作为几何警告的补充；在物理仿真驱动的预注册保留穿越场景中，将其与十五个几何检测器进行对比，并对误报预算进行匹配校准。

Result: 压力警报标记了85个几何检测器组遗漏的碰撞，而几何检测器反向标记了18个；联合两种通道可警告四分之三仍可刹车的碰撞，而仅几何检测器组警告不到一半。

Conclusion: 对偶应力信号是一种与几何警告互补的有效危险监测手段，两者结合能够显著提升自主导航系统的碰撞预警能力。

Abstract: Runtime hazard monitors for autonomous naviga- tion are conventionally built from geometric quantities: predicted clearance, time to collision, and required deceleration. A model-predictive controller that enforces safety through explicit con- straints computes, as a by-product of every control step, a second information channel that such monitors ignore: the Karush-Kuhn-Tucker multipliers of its constrained optimization, which measure the marginal control effort spent to maintain safety against each obstacle. This paper evaluates whether a horizon-weighted sum of those multipliers, a dual stress signal, provides a hazard monitor complementary to the geometric warnings the same state already supports. We compare it against a battery of fifteen geometric detectors tuned to a matched false-alarm budget, on preregistered held-out crossing scenarios driven through a physics simulator. The stress alarm actionably flags 4.7 times as many collisions missed by the entire geometric battery as the geometric battery flags in return (85 versus 18); combined, the two channels warn of three quarters of the collisions for which braking remained feasible, against under half for the geometric battery alone.

</details>


### [218] [AECNav: Active Evidence Consolidation for Efficient Zero-Shot Open-Vocabulary Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10817)
*Guanlin Liu,Shaobin Ling,Renyuan Liu,Zeying Gong,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AECNav，一个基于证据驱动的无训练零样本目标导航管线，通过证据门控感知、证据整合和主动证据获取三个组件，显著提升了零样本目标导航的成功率与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本目标导航（ZSON）在开放词汇场景中面临高延迟和精度不足的问题，主要源于冗余的感知流程和缺乏可靠的证据来确定目标位置。现有方法难以在未见环境中高效、准确地定位任意指定的物体。

Method: AECNav将ZSON重定义为证据驱动的感知到决策问题，包含三个组件：1) 证据门控感知：在所有推理阶段使用共享编码建立统一语义基础，消除冗余计算；2) 证据整合：将检测结果聚合成聚类级别的对数几率信念，明确区分真实目标支持与视觉相似干扰物的虚假置信度，并将预期检测的缺失视为负面证据；3) 主动证据获取：在弱语义线索下通过选择最大化信息增益且最小化遍历成本的前沿来维持有效探索。

Result: AECNav在HM3D-v2、HM3D-OVON和MP3D上分别达到84.7%、57.3%和51.3%的成功率，超越现有方法取得最优结果，同时推理开销显著降低。在物理四足机器人上40次试验达到95%的成功率，运行频率约5Hz。

Conclusion: AECNav通过将目标导航重构为证据驱动的感知-决策问题，以训练无关的无训练管道实现了高效、准确的零样本目标导航，验证了证据整合与主动获取策略在开放词汇导航任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Zero-shot object-goal navigation (ZSON) in open-vocabulary scenarios is challenging, as it requires a robot to locate an arbitrarily specified object in an unseen environment without task-specific training. Currently, the task still suffers from high latency and limited accuracy due to redundant perception pipelines and insufficient evidence for reliable target confirmation. In this letter, we reframe ZSON as an evidence-driven perception-to-decision problem and present AECNav, a training-free pipeline built on three components: i) Evidence-gated perception, which utilizes a shared encoding across all reasoning stages to establish a unified semantic basis and eliminate redundant computations; ii) Evidence consolidation, which aggregates detections into cluster-level log-odds beliefs. This explicitly separates genuine target support from the false confidence of visually similar distractors, while treating the absence of expected detections as negative evidence; and iii) Active evidence acquisition, which sustains productive exploration under weak semantic cues by selecting frontiers that maximize information gain at minimal traversal cost. As a result, AECNav significantly outperforms previous methods and achieves state-of-the-art success rates of 84.7%, 57.3%, and 51.3% on HM3D-v2, HM3D-OVON, and MP3D, respectively, with substantially lower inference overhead, and attains 95% success across 40 trials on a physical quadruped robot at roughly 5Hz. Code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [219] [Neural Introspection Gating for Adaptive KV-Cache Reuse in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10824)
*Zhijie Wu,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Gated VLA-Cache，一种轻量级、无需训练的扩展方法，通过在视觉相似性缓存基础上引入神经内省机制（监控解码时logit margin），在模型置信度下降时触发缓存失效并完全重算，在LIBERO基准上恢复了盲缓存损失的准确率，同时保留了80%的计算节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在实时控制中，对于相邻帧中几乎不变的视觉token，会重复计算KV表示，浪费大量计算资源。现有的VLA-Cache虽然通过复用视觉静态patch的KV状态来降低开销，但其策略仅依赖观测空间的启发式规则，未考虑模型自身的不确定性，导致在某些任务中牺牲了可靠性。

Method: 提出Gated VLA-Cache，在视觉相似性缓存基础上增加神经内省机制。方法监控解码过程中前两个预测动作token之间的logit margin（零成本的置信度信号），当margin低于阈值时使缓存失效并触发完整重算。该方法为轻量级、训练无关的扩展。

Result: 在四个LIBERO基准套件上使用OpenVLA和OpenVLA-OFT进行评估。在LIBERO-Goal和LIBERO-Long任务中，恢复了超过100%的因盲缓存导致的精度损失，同时保留了80%的计算节省。

Conclusion: Gated VLA-Cache通过将模型自身的置信度信号引入缓存策略，有效解决了盲目缓存带来的可靠性下降问题，实现了计算效率与任务准确率之间的更好平衡。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action(VLA) models map camera images and language instructions directly to motor commands through a single autoregressive transformer. In real-time control, they still spend substantial compute recomputing key-value(KV) representations for visual tokens that barely change across neighboring frames. Recent work such as VLA-Cache reduces that cost by reusing KV states for visually static patches, but its policy relies only on observation-space heuristics and does not account for the model's own uncertainty. We propose Gated VLA-Cache, a lightweight, training-free extension that augments visual-similarity caching with neural introspection. The method monitors the logit margin between the top two predicted action tokens, a zero-cost confidence signal available during decoding. When the margin drops below a threshold, the cache is invalidated and a full recompute is triggered. Evaluated on four LIBERO benchmark suites with both OpenVLA and OpenVLA-OFT, Gated VLA-Cache improves reliability when blind caching hurts. On LIBERO-Goal and LIBERO-Long, it recovers over 100% of the lost accuracy while retaining 80% of the compute savings.

</details>


### [220] [Enabling Scalable Kinesthetic Teaching via Observer-based Hand-guiding with Active Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10847)
*Anna Tuma,Giuseppe Monetti,Jochen J. Steil,Niels Dehio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RHOAS，一种无需额外硬件的手引导机器人方案，通过基于模型的力估计主动支持操作者意图运动，在KUKA LWR iiwa上16名参与者的用户研究中显著降低了操作体力消耗，提升了精确和敏捷任务的操控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人手引导教学方式在长时间操作中会导致操作者疲劳，降低演示质量并限制可扩展性。当前工业手引导方法通常缺乏主动辅助，而替代方案要么需要昂贵的腕部力-力矩传感器，要么依赖新任务不可用的学习运动先验。

Method: RHOAS将手引导视为操作者主动控制交互而非被动环境交互，采用基于模型的外部扭矩估计、内部关节扭矩传感和冗余机器人运动学，在人体交互频率带宽内主动支持物理输入。同时解决了基于观测器的力估计的实际挑战，包括抑制未建模关节弹性动态效应、反馈路径中的测量噪声、运动学奇异点附近的估计精度下降以及静态重力补偿误差。

Result: 在KUKA LWR iiwa上进行了16名参与者的用户研究，结果显示物理努力显著减少，精确和敏捷任务的操控性均有改善，且用户偏好明确。

Conclusion: RHOAS通过模型化力估计实现无需额外硬件的主动手引导辅助，有效降低操作者疲劳并提升演示质量，为仿人学习和编程演示提供了更具可扩展性的方案。

Abstract: Kinesthetic teaching through robot hand-guiding provides a natural interface for collecting demonstrations in imitation learning and programming-by-demonstration. However, extended sessions cause operator fatigue, reducing demonstration quality and limiting scalability. Current industrial hand-guiding approaches typically provide no active assistance, and alternatives require costly wrist-mounted force-torque sensors or rely on learned motion priors unavailable for new tasks. We propose RHOAS, a hand-guiding scheme that actively supports operator-intended motions using model-based force estimation without additional hardware. Our approach considers robot hand-guiding as an actively controlled interaction by the human operator, rather than an interaction with a passive environment. Standard methods used for hand-guiding typically rely on general passivity-based compliant control architectures that unnecessarily increase operator effort and limit the range of demonstrable motions without providing the intended stability guarantees in active interaction. Instead, our design utilizes model-based external torque estimation, internal joint torque sensing, and redundant robot kinematics to actively support human physical input within the human interaction frequency bandwidth. We address practical challenges of relying on observer-based force estimation, including suppression of unmodeled joint elastic dynamic effects and measurement noise in the feedback path, reduced estimate accuracy close to kinematic singularities, and static gravity compensation errors. In a user study with 16 participants on a KUKA LWR iiwa we demonstrate statistically significant reductions in physical effort, improved maneuverability for both precise and agile tasks, and clear user preference.

</details>


### [221] [Flex-$π$: A Multi-Stream World-Action Model with Compute Flexibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10860)
*Ge Yan,Jinghao Liu,Yuzhi Fan,Lei Cai,Minwen Liao,Jesse Zhang,Dieter Fox*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Flex-π，一个6B参数的世界动作模型（WAM）。其核心发现是：冻结的视频生成VAE在编码RGB的同时，无需额外训练即可几乎无损地编码3D点图（pointmaps）和DINO语义信息。Flex-π利用这一"免费午餐"，将3D几何与物体语义监督与RGB联合去噪，并通过Mixture-of-Transformers主干、按流dropout与跨模态强制技术，使单一训练好的模型可在任意流子集上运行（从快速仅动作模式到全联合生成）。在灵巧、精确的真实世界双手操作任务中，Flex-π以2-7倍优势超越最强基线，且运行速度优于π₀.₅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型几乎都仅预测RGB潜在表示，纯粹为像素重建训练，缺乏对3D几何和物体语义的显式监督信号，而这正是操作任务所必需的。作者旨在不引入新传感器、新预训练和推理延迟的前提下，为WAM注入3D几何与语义能力。

Method: (1) 发现冻结视频生成VAE可无损编码3D点图与DINO语义，无需点图特定训练；(2) 将RGB、3D几何、DINO语义等所有视觉信号投影到同一共享潜在空间，与动作一起在Mixture-of-Transformers主干中联合去噪；(3) 采用逐流dropout与跨模态强制策略，使单一检查点支持任意流子集输入，覆盖从仅动作快速模式到全联合生成的多种运行模式。

Result: Flex-π在灵巧、精确的真实世界双手操作任务中，在分布内和分布外场景下均以2-7倍的性能优势超越最强基线，同时推理速度比π₀.₅更快，展现出出色的演示效率和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了预训练视频VAE作为多模态（RGB+3D几何+语义）共享潜在编码器的潜力，证明在不增加额外成本的情况下，通过共享潜在空间联合去噪可以显著提升世界动作模型的性能，为具身操作策略提供了一条高效、泛化强的技术路径。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) predict the future to act better, but nearly all of them predict only RGB latents, trained purely for pixel reconstruction, with no explicit signal for the 3D geometry or object semantics manipulation needs. We find a surprising free lunch: the same frozen video-generation VAE that encodes RGB also encodes 3D pointmaps almost losslessly, with no pointmap-specific training at all. This lets us supervise Flex-$π$, a 6B-parameter WAM, on 3D geometry and object-centric DINO semantics alongside RGB, at no cost in new sensors, new pre-training, or inference latency. Every visual signal is projected into this shared latent space and denoised jointly with actions inside a Mixture-of-Transformers backbone; per-stream dropout with cross-modality forcing then lets a single trained checkpoint run on any subset of these streams, from a fast action-only mode to full joint generation. The result is a policy that is exceptionally demonstration-efficient and generalizes well, beating the strongest baselines by up to 2-7$\times$ on dexterous, precise, real-world bimanual manipulation tasks both in and out of distribution, all while running faster than $π_{0.5}$. Our project website: https://flex-pi.github.io/

</details>


### [222] [Aerial Layouting: Design and Control of a Compliant and Actuated End-Effector for Precise In-flight Marking on Ceilings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10987)
*Christian Lanegger,Marco Ruggia,Marco Tognon,Lionel Ott,Roland Siegwart*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型空中机器人末端执行器，基于稳定性优化的Gough-Stewart并联机构，实现毫米级定位精度，可用于天花板划线等空中接触式任务，无需对空中机器人进行精确建模或复杂控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机器人已有厘米级精度可用于接触式检测任务，但建筑施工布局、推滑等任务需要毫米级定位精度，现有系统难以达成这一要求。

Method: 设计了一种配备"智能"末端执行器的空中系统，采用稳定性优化的Gough-Stewart并联机构，结合柔顺性、多点接触、驱动和自包含等设计原则。

Result: 实验验证了末端执行器的设计选择对获得所需定位精度是必要的，并成功实现了在天花板上以毫米级精度可靠地标记线条，无需精确建模或对空中机器人进行复杂控制。

Conclusion: 所提出的智能末端执行器方案能够有效弥补空中机器人控制精度的不足，以较低复杂度实现毫米级定位精度，为空中接触式精密操作任务提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Aerial robots have demonstrated impressive feats of precise control, such as dynamic flight through openings or highly complex choreographies. Despite the accuracy needed for these tasks, there are problems that require levels of precision that are challenging to achieve today. One such problem is aerial interaction. Advances in aerial robot design and control have made such contact-based tasks possible and opened up research into challenging real-world tasks, including contact-based inspection. However, while centimetre accuracy is sufficient and achievable for inspection tasks, the positioning accuracy needed for other problems, such as layouting on construction sites or general push-and-slide tasks, is millimetres. To achieve such a high precision, we propose a new aerial system composed of an aerial vehicle equipped with a novel "smart" end-effector leveraging a stability-optimized Gough-Stewart mechanism. We present its design process and features incorporating the principles of compliance, multiple contact points, actuation, and self-containment. In experiments, we verify that the design choices made for our novel end-effector are necessary to obtain the desired positioning precision. Furthermore, we demonstrate that we can reliably mark lines on ceilings with millimetre accuracy without the need for precise modeling or sophisticated control of the aerial robot.

</details>


### [223] [Seeing above the waves: A modular sensing framework for data acquisition at sea](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10997)
*Jonathan E. Schmidt,Julius Wirbel,P. Nicholas Hansen,Morgan Louédec,Christian L. H. Westerdahl,Dimitrios Dagdilelis,Roberto Galeazzi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向水面船舶自主导航的模块化、可复现传感器平台设计蓝图，集成了RADAR、LiDAR、IMU、GNSS、AIS、RGB/LWIR相机及气象传感器等多模态感知设备，并配套ROS2软件框架，以支持长期数据采集、硬件在环测试及跨平台可比性研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海事环境对自主船舶的感知系统评估提出独特挑战：传感器安装受船体布局限制、雾霾和海杂波等环境条件难以复现、长时任务导致数据收集困难。如何设计模块化且可复现的传感器平台成为核心研究问题。

Method: 提出综合性的传感器平台设计蓝图，包含多模态传感器组合（RADAR、LiDAR、IMU、GNSS、AIS、RGB/LWIR相机、气象传感器）；基于ROS2的软件框架实现数据管理、长期采集、硬件在环测试及与现有传感器和算法的集成；统一硬件设计与数据采集方法学以增强可复现性和可比性。

Result: 该平台通过模块化设计统一了硬件实现与数据捕获方法，提升了跨船舶和跨研究项目的复现性及可比性，为标准化、可验证数据集的建设奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架连接了工程实现与研究方法的鸿沟，为推进水面船舶的态势感知与自主导航能力提供了基础设施支撑。

Abstract: Advancing autonomy for surface vessels requires systematic evaluation of their sensing and perception subsystems. Yet, maritime environments impose unique challenges: sensor installation is constrained by vessel layout, environmental conditions such as fog or sea clutter are difficult to reproduce, and long-duration missions complicate data collection. This work addresses the question: How can we design a modular and reproducible sensor platform for maritime autonomy?
  We present a comprehensive design blueprint that incorporates diverse modalities - RADAR, LiDAR, IMU, GNSS, AIS, RGB and LWIR cameras, and weather sensors - to enhance environmental awareness and vessel proprioception. Supported by a dedicated ROS2-based software framework for data management, our modular platform enables long-term data collection, hardware-in-the-loop testing, and integration with existing sensors and algorithms. By unifying hardware design and data capture methodology, the platform enhances reproducibility and comparability across vessels and research projects. The proposed framework bridges engineering implementation and research methodology, providing the foundation for standardized, verifiable datasets essential to advancing situational awareness and autonomous maritime navigation.

</details>


### [224] [Deployment Is Not Destiny: Robot Recomposition in the Field with Unseen Software, Hardware, and Compute Payloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11063)
*Steven Swanbeck,Jonathan Salfity,Jeffery Gunawan,Corrie Van Sice,Mitch Pryor,Robert Blake Anderson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套运行时重组框架及配套抽象，允许非专家用户在野外通过即插即用的方式为机器人快速集成未曾见过的模块化软件、硬件和计算负载，且新资源不仅可供宿主机器人使用，还能与分布式对等节点共享。该框架将配置时间从传统数小时专家工作缩短至数分钟内无需开发者干预，并在两个灾难响应场景中（核反应堆放射性源定位和黑暗狭窄空间的搜救）得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数机器人的子系统紧密耦合，虽然这是其复杂性的自然结果，但导致了整体式设计（monolithic design），部署后难以快速调整，适应动态需求效率低下。

Method: 提出一个运行时重组框架及其支撑抽象，支持即插即用式集成未预见的模块化软件、硬件和计算负载；新资源不仅即时可用于宿主机器人，还可共享给分布式对等节点，使算力受限系统能够访问远程新能力。

Result: 框架将重组（配置）时间从传统的数小时专家工作缩短至数分钟且无需开发者干预；在两个灾难响应场景中成功验证，包括核设施放射性源定位和黑暗狭窄空间中的热引导人员搜索。

Conclusion: 野外实时重组为动态需求提供了及时、灵活和可及的适应性，是实现机器人能够随任务、技术和环境快速演化的重要一步。

Abstract: The tight coupling of subsystems in most robots, though a natural consequence of their complexity, leads to monolithic designs that are time-consuming and difficult to adapt after initial deployment. To address this challenge, we present a framework and supporting abstractions for recomposition during runtime that enable robots to quickly integrate previously unseen modular software, hardware, and compute payloads. Our approach allows non-expert users to quickly add new capabilities in the field through a true plug-and-play process. Crucially, new resources are not only immediately available to a host robot but are also shared with distributed peers, enabling compute-constrained systems to access powerful new remote capabilities. Our framework reduces reconfiguration time to a matter of minutes with no developer intervention, in stark contrast to the hours of expert effort often required for traditional manual integration. We demonstrate our method in two disaster response scenarios, including radioactive source localization at an operational nuclear reactor facility and a thermal-guided search for people in dark, difficult-to-reach spaces. These demonstrations show how in-field recomposition provides timely, flexible, and accessible adaptation to dynamic requirements, representing a critical step toward creating robots that can quickly evolve alongside the tasks, technologies, and environments they support.

</details>


### [225] [VIScore: Diagnosing Planning-Relevant Quality in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11174)
*Haiyu Wu,Randall Balestriero,Morgan Levine*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VIScore指标，从Veracity（真实性）、Influence（影响力）、Sobriety（清醒度）三个维度综合评估世界模型规划的成功率，比现有指标（如直度、物理状态探针、 empower）更准确地预测跨任务的成功率（Spearman相关性>0.75），且校准误差更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究将潜空间正则化为各向同性高斯分布以实现稳定规划，但潜空间性质与规划成功之间的关系仍未被充分理解。现有评估指标仅聚焦于编码器输出的潜特征，忽略了预测器和规划器的作用，无法准确解释规划成功率。

Method: 通过比较SIGReg和VISReg两种正则化损失（同目标分布但不同性质）的规划表现，发现VISReg在OOD数据集上更优。基于此提出VIScore指标，同时量化编码器的可达性、预测器的容量以及基于搜索规划器的幻觉程度，覆盖编码器-预测器-规划器三个环节。

Result: VIScore在已见和未见模型及数据集上均获得超过0.75的Spearman相关性与跨任务成功率，是唯一在所有测试场景中校准误差低于常数拟合的指标，优于straightness、物理状态探针和empowerment。

Conclusion: 同时考虑编码器、预测器和规划器的综合指标对于理解和诊断世界模型规划成功至关重要，VIScore可作为未来世界模型设计与诊断的有力工具。

Abstract: Regulating the latent space to an isotropic Gaussian distribution provides a stable and information-maximized landscape for world model planning. However, the latent space property and successful planning remain disconnected. We first study this by comparing SIGReg and VISReg, two regularization loss functions with the same distribution target but different properties. Compared with SIGReg, VISReg has more flexibility in controlling the weights of center, scale, and shape regularization, and a larger batch size brings a finer distribution approximation. We find that the former, despite being beneficial in self-supervised learning (SSL), does not help the planning, whereas the latter improves the planning success on out-of-domain (OOD) datasets. This motivates a deep understanding of the factors that correlate with the success rate. Unlike the previous metrics focusing on the encoded latent only, we propose the Veracity-Influence-Sobriety score (VIScore), a metric that quantifies the reachability and capacity of a predictor given the encoded feature, and the hallucination of the searching-based planner. Compared with straightness, physical-state probing, and empowerment, we show that, with the measurement covering encoder, predictor, and planner, VIScore explains the success rate better than the others, as reflected by a strong Spearman correlation. Specifically, VIScore consistently achieves a Spearman correlation over 0.75 on both seen and unseen models and datasets on the cross-task success rate pool. Moreover, VIScore is the only metric that has a calibration error below the constant fit across all testing scenarios, showcasing the importance of these three aspects in planning success. We hope this metric can help future studies on world model design and diagnosis.

</details>


### [226] [Risk-Aware Kinodynamic Motion Planning Under Uncertainty For Safe Navigation on Planetary Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11175)
*Sachin Sunil Kelkar,Tanmay Dokania,Yashwanth Kumar Nakka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两步式风险感知的运动规划方法：首先使用采样规划器AO-RRT生成动态可行、风险感知且渐近成本最优的轨迹，然后将其作为初始解，通过序列凸规划（SCP）求解非线性优化问题，最终在仿真和硬件实验中实现了超过97%的风险降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主空间探索中，机器人运动规划面临环境交互未知的挑战，地形力学等学习性交互会引入不确定性，导致运动计划具有风险甚至引发任务失败；此外，基于感知系统带来的不确定性进一步加剧了安全运动规划的难度。

Method: 采用两步方法：第一步，使用采样规划器AO-RRT生成动态可行、风险感知且渐近成本最优的轨迹；第二步，将运动规划转化为非线性优化问题，利用序列凸规划（SCP）求解，并以AO-RRT生成的轨迹作为初始解，风险度量采用条件风险价值（CVaR）。

Result: 在仿真和硬件实验中，使用CVaR量化风险，轨迹中的风险降低了约97%以上。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效实现成本最优的动力学运动规划，同时具备风险感知能力，显著降低了由于环境交互不确定性和感知不确定性带来的运动风险。

Abstract: For autonomous space exploration, robotic agents need to perform motion planning in which environmental interactions may be unknown. Learning these interactions, such as terrain mechanics for wheeled robots, can introduce uncertainties that lead to risky motion plans and potentially hazardous operations or mission failures. Moreover, uncertainties induced by perception-based systems can exacerbate the problem of safe motion planning. In this letter, we address the problem of performing cost-optimal kinodynamic motion planning with risk awareness. We approach this in two steps. First, a sampling-based planner (AO-RRT) generates a dynamically feasible, risk-aware, and asymptotically cost-optimal trajectory. Second, we formulate motion planning as a nonlinear optimization problem and solve it using sequential convex programming (SCP), using the AO-RRT trajectory as an initial solution. By quantifying risk using conditional value-at-risk (CVaR), we demonstrate a reduction in risk by over $\sim$97\% across trajectories in simulation and hardware experiments.

</details>


### [227] [Surgical WAM: A World-Action Model for Data-Efficient Surgical Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11204)
*Wenrui Bao,Tianyun Jiang,Zhiben Chen,Ser-Nam Lim,Peter D. Peng,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Surgical WAM模型，通过从无动作标注的内窥镜视频中预训练视觉动力学，再在有限的带动作标签数据上微调，实现手术机器人闭环操控。在四个模拟手术任务上，视频预训练将平均成功率从63.5%提升至77.8%，尤其在接触密集和双臂协调任务上提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人操控策略学习受限于动作标注演示数据稀缺（如dVRK轨迹采集成本高），而内窥镜视频数据相对丰富。现有手术世界模型仅将视频用于仿真或策略评估，未能转化为闭环控制。核心问题是在固定动作标注预算下，无动作视频预训练能否提升闭环手术操控性能。

Method: 提出Surgical World-Action Model (Surgical WAM)，基于Cosmos Policy构建的统一生成模型，联合预测未来内窥镜观测和可执行的手术机器人动作块。首先从无动作视频中学习手术视觉动力学，然后在固定动作标注预算下微调；部署时作为闭环滚动时域控制器，执行预测动作块的前缀后基于新观测重新规划。

Result: 在四个模拟手术操控任务中，视频预训练将平均成功率从63.5%提升到77.8%，PegTransfer任务上取得20个百分点的绝对提升，且对接触密集和双臂协调任务的改进最为显著。

Conclusion: 无动作视频为手术机器人控制提供了可迁移的视觉动力学先验，在有限动作监督下显著提升学习效果，为数据高效地扩展手术机器人学习提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Learning reliable surgical manipulation policies is bottlenecked by the scarcity of action-labeled demonstrations: teleoperated surgical robot (e.g., dVRK) trajectories with synchronized kinematics are costly to collect, while surgical tasks demand precise contact handling, long-horizon reasoning, and bimanual coordination. Endoscopic video is comparatively inexpensive and abundant relative to synchronized video--kinematics trajectories, and a natural way to exploit it is to learn world models of surgical scenes. However, existing surgical world models use video primarily for simulation or policy evaluation, and rarely translate the learned dynamics into closed-loop control. This gap raises our central question: under a fixed budget of action-labeled demonstrations, does action-free video pretraining improve closed-loop surgical manipulation? To answer it, we introduce the Surgical World-Action Model (Surgical WAM), a unified generative model built on Cosmos Policy that jointly predicts future endoscopic observations and executable surgical robot action chunks. Surgical WAM first learns surgical visual dynamics from action-free video and is then fine-tuned on the fixed action-labeled budget; at deployment, it acts as a closed-loop, receding-horizon controller that executes a short prefix of each predicted action chunk and replans from the resulting observation. On a suite of four simulated surgical manipulation tasks, video pretraining improves the average success rate from 63.5% to 77.8%, including an absolute gain of 20 percentage points on PegTransfer, with the largest improvements on contact-rich and bimanual tasks. These results demonstrate that action-free video provides transferable visual dynamics priors for learning surgical robot control with limited action supervision, positioning data-efficient video pretraining as a practical path toward scaling up surgical robot learning.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [228] [Transformer Geometry Observatory TGO-IV: Developmental Topology Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09997)
*Kaustubh Kapil,Kishor P. Upla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Transformer Geometry Observatory (TGO-IV)，一个基于持续同调（Persistent Homology）的拓扑分析框架，用于研究Transformer各层之间表征点云（representation point clouds）的演化过程，从而揭示原始输入表征如何逐步转变为任务相关特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Transformer可解释性研究主要分析孤立层或整个网络的表征，但忽略了单个表征及其流形在跨层间的发展演化过程，缺乏对"Transformer如何学习"这一核心问题的深入理解。

Method: TGO-IV采用拓扑学方法，从token级表征点云构建Vietoris-Rips单纯复形，并利用多种拓扑观测器（包括Persistence Diagrams、Barcode Diagrams、Betti Curves、Persistence Landscapes、Bottleneck Distance和Wasserstein Distance）来追踪表征全局拓扑结构在Transformer各层前向传播过程中的演化。

Result: 该框架提供了全面的拓扑分析工具集，能够识别表征演化的关键层或趋势，有助于定位原始输入表征向任务相关特征表征转化的具体阶段。

Conclusion: TGO-IV通过整合多种持续同调观测器，为深入理解Transformer表征如何在层间演化提供了新的全局拓扑视角，弥补了以往仅关注局部几何特性或孤立层分析的不足。

Abstract: Transformers have had a profound impact on the world of language processing and computer vision. As efforts to answer the million-dollar question of ``How does a Transformer learn?" have been increasing, existing interpretability studies primarily analyze representations at isolated layers or the network as a whole, while the developmental evolution of individual representations and its manifolds across transformer layers remains underexplored. With this work, we aim at providing a comprehensive analysis of the evolution of representations as the representation point cloud transforms across the layers; thereby attempting to isolate layers or establish a trend which comes closer to justifying how and when raw input representations evolve into task-relevant feature representations. Thus, Transformer Geometry Observatory-TGO-IV introduces a topological framework for analysing the evolution of Transformer representations through the lens of Persistent Homology. Rather than studying local geometric properties alone, TGO-IV constructs Vietoris--Rips simplicial complexes from token-level representation point clouds and investigates the evolution of their persistent topological signatures across Transformer layers. The proposed framework comprises complementary topological observatories including Persistence Diagrams, Barcode Diagrams, Betti Curves, Persistence Landscapes, Bottleneck Distance, and Wasserstein Distance, enabling a comprehensive analysis of how the global topology of representation point clouds develops throughout the forward pass.

</details>


### [229] [Uncertainty-Aware Ensemble Deep Randomized Neural Networks for Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10007)
*M. Sajid,A. Quadir,A. Rahaman,P. N. Suganthan,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于直觉模糊理论的深度随机向量功能链接网络框架（IF-dRVFL和IF-edRVFL），通过同时考虑隶属度与非隶属度来为训练样本分配自适应权重，从而增强模型对噪声和异常值的鲁棒性，在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上优于现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度随机神经网络（如dRVFL和edRVFL）对所有训练样本一视同仁，忽视样本差异，导致在含有噪声和异常值的真实数据上鲁棒性和有效性受限；且污染特征在隐藏层间的传播会负面影响模型决策能力。

Method: 提出IF-dRVFL和IF-edRVFL框架，将直觉模糊理论融入模型，在核空间中利用样本邻域信息，联合考虑每个样本的隶属度（基于样本到类中心的距离计算）和非隶属度（量化局部邻域内的样本异质性），以此分配自适应权重，有效区分干净样本、噪声样本和异常值。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上进行大量实验（包括有无高斯噪声两种场景），结果表明所提出的IF-dRVFL和IF-edRVFL模型在性能上优于现有的SOTA模糊和非模糊方法。

Conclusion: 通过直觉模糊理论实现了对训练样本的差异化加权处理，显著提升了深度随机向量功能链接网络在噪声环境下的鲁棒性和泛化能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: The current state-of-the-art (SOTA) deep randomized neural networks, such as deep Random Vector Functional Link (dRVFL) and ensemble deep RVFL (edRVFL), treat all training samples uniformly, which limits their robustness and effectiveness when applied to real-world datasets containing noise and outliers. Furthermore, the propagation of contaminated features across hidden layers negatively influences the decision-making capability of these models. To overcome these limitations, we propose intuitionistic fuzzy dRVFL (IF-dRVFL) and intuitionistic fuzzy edRVFL (IF-edRVFL) frameworks that enhance model robustness. The proposed models unify intuitionistic fuzzy theory to exploit sample neighborhood information in the kernel space by jointly considering membership and non-membership degrees for each sample. Membership degrees are computed based on the distance of samples from their respective class centroids, while non-membership degrees quantify sample heterogeneity within local neighborhoods. These measures are employed to assign adaptive weights to training samples, enabling effective discrimination among clean, noisy, and outlier data points. Extensive experiments conducted on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets, with and without the presence of Gaussian noise, demonstrate the superiority of the proposed IF-dRVFL and IF-edRVFL models over existing SOTA fuzzy and non-fuzzy approaches. The source code is available at https://github.com/mtanveer1/IF-edRVFL.

</details>


### [230] [CurveFP: Rational-Radix Logarithmic Datatypes with Closed Products for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10010)
*Ye Qiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CurveFP，一种封闭乘积码本族，通过在紧凑块缩放下的交错对数曲线间分配量化幅度，实现FP8级别的数值性能、七位推理推断以及更简单的乘积路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低精度数据类型虽然能降低语言模型成本，但大多数格式仅优化标量保真度，而乘积引入的算术误差保持不变。现有FP8等格式在缩放乘积的数值行为上仍有改进空间，需要一种同时兼顾数值精度、算术封闭性和推理效率的格式。

Method: CurveFP通过有理基数调谐动态范围与局部分辨率，使用均匀曲线索引使所有非零乘积代数封闭。乘积形成变为精确的符号异或和整数索引更新，并通过推导的有穷相位数确定累积调度。实例化为CurveFP 8（E4C3/E5C2）用于训练和CurveFP 7（E3C3）用于紧凑部署。

Result: CurveFP 7在四个7B-9B模型上以少一个元素比特超越张量级FP8困惑度，并保持原生质量1.32%以内；CurveFP 8在全部36个前向/反向GEMM对比中降低操作数NMSE。在三个128.3M参数三重奏中，所有模式完成每个种子的3B token预训练，CurveFP 8达到平均BF16推理困惑度22.5366（FP8为22.5407），并在全部三个种子中产生更低的格式引发惩罚。36单元下游矩阵中，CurveFP 8训练的检查点在12个种子格式比较中全部取得更低的WikiText-103困惑度。

Conclusion: CurveFP是一种算术协同设计，结合了FP8类数值行为、七位推理和显著更简单的乘积路径，为低精度语言模型训练与部署提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: Low-precision datatypes reduce language-model cost, but most formats optimize scalar fidelity while leaving the arithmetic induced by their products unchanged. We introduce CurveFP, a closed-product codebook family that distributes quantized magnitudes across interleaved logarithmic curves under compact block scales. A rational radix tunes dynamic range against local resolution, while uniform curve indices make every nonzero product algebraically closed. Product formation becomes an exact sign XOR and integer-index update, and a derived finite phase count determines the accumulation schedule. We instantiate this algebra as CurveFP eight E4C3/E5C2 for training and CurveFP seven E3C3 for compact deployment. In evaluation, CurveFP seven beats tensor-wise FP8 perplexity on four 7B--9B models with one fewer element bit and stays within 1.32\% of native quality. CurveFP eight lowers operand NMSE in all 36 paired forward and backward GEMM comparisons. Across three matched 128.3M-parameter triplets, every mode completes 3B-token pretraining per seed; CurveFP eight reaches mean BF16-inference perplexity 22.5366 versus 22.5407 for FP8 and incurs a lower format-induced penalty in all three seeds. A 36-cell downstream matrix finds lower WikiText-103 perplexity for the CurveFP eight-trained checkpoints in all 12 seed-format comparisons, with mixed PG-19 and task deltas. Together, these results establish CurveFP as an arithmetic co-design that combines FP8-class numerical behavior, seven-bit inference, and a substantially simpler product path.

</details>


### [231] [Sheaf-Based Federated Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10016)
*Gabriele D'Acunto,Enrico Grimaldi,Valeria Avino,Mario Edoardo Pandolfo,Leonardo Di Nino,Sergio Barbarossa,Paolo Di Lorenzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于层（Sheaf）的联邦表示学习框架SFRL，通过可学习的层限制映射和流形约束几何对齐正则项，在不共享全局潜在空间的前提下实现异构联邦系统中的表示学习与信息交换，并开发了交替优化的分散式算法Sheaf-FRL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构联邦系统中各智能体在数据分布、感知模态、模型架构、潜在维度及本地学习目标等方面存在显著差异，然而现有方法大多假设存在共享的全局潜在空间，这一假设在真实异构场景中过于严格，难以有效对齐不同智能体的表示。

Method: SFRL通过最小二乘拼接正则项（由层拉普拉斯算子诱导）联合优化本地目标函数与流形约束的几何对齐正则项；基于可学习的层限制映射，通过正交变换和等距嵌入实现相邻潜在表示的几何对齐；在共享的小规模导引样本上评估惩罚项以保证可扩展性和通信效率；Sheaf-FRL算法交替执行本地模型的梯度更新和边级限制映射的闭式Procrustes更新。

Result: 在语义通信的协作分类任务中，Sheaf-FRL在不同本地分布偏移水平下均优于基线方法，且对潜在空间维度压缩表现出更强的鲁棒性；同时证明了算法在确定性和随机设置下均能收敛到一阶驻点。

Conclusion: 通过层几何实现无需共享潜在空间的异构联邦表示学习是可行且有效的，局部对齐的不断累积可以涌现出全局一致性，为异构联邦系统中的表示学习提供了新的理论框架和实用算法。

Abstract: Heterogeneous federated systems require agents to learn and exchange informative representations despite differences in data distributions, sensing modalities, model architectures, latent dimensionalities, and local learning objectives. To address this challenge, we propose Sheaf-based Federated Representation Learning (SFRL), a general framework that jointly optimizes local objectives with a manifold-constrained geometric alignment regularizer based on learnable sheaf restriction maps. Unlike most existing approaches, SFRL does not assume a shared global latent space. Instead, global consistency emerges from the alignment of neighboring latent representations through orthogonal transformations and isometric embeddings. This alignment is enforced by a quadratic gluing regularizer induced by the sheaf Laplacian, whose learnable restriction maps adapt the geometry to the observed data. The penalty is evaluated on a small set of shared pilot samples, ensuring scalability and communication efficiency. We develop a decentralized algorithm for solving SFRL, termed Sheaf-FRL, which alternates between gradient updates of the local models and closed-form Procrustes updates of the edge-wise restriction maps. We further establish convergence of Sheaf-FRL to first-order stationary points in both deterministic and stochastic settings. As an application, we consider a cooperative classification task in the context of semantic communication, under model and data heterogeneity. Our results show that Sheaf-FRL outperforms baseline approaches in terms of local and post-communication classification accuracy across different levels of local distribution shift and exhibits greater robustness to latent-space dimensionality compression.

</details>


### [232] [DOCSCHISEL: Adaptive Tool Documentation Optimization Framework for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10037)
*You Lu,Kun Zhang,Bihuan Chen,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究发现工具文档中的信息字段在不同任务域、LLM骨干和智能体范式下效果高度异质，不存在可通用的固定文档格式。据此提出了DocsChisel框架，一种自适应工具文档优化方法，通过分析失败执行轨迹迭代地增删和精炼工具文档中的信息字段，相比原文档将任务成功率提升95.89%，相比现有基线平均提升75.15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注提升LLM智能体的工具使用能力，而将工具文档视为固定输入。尽管有少量工作尝试重写或压缩工具文档，但缺乏对工具文档中信息如何影响智能体性能的系统性理解。

Method: 首先进行大规模实证研究揭示工具文档信息字段的异质性及其效果对任务域、LLM骨干和智能体范式的依赖性；随后提出DocsChisel框架，通过分析目标LLM智能体的失败执行轨迹识别文档相关问题，并迭代地对每个工具进行信息字段的添加、移除和精炼优化。

Result: DocsChisel相比原始工具文档将LLM智能体的任务成功率提升95.89%，相比EasyTool和DRAFT两个SOTA基线平均提升75.15%，同时保持了有限的时间开销和token开销。

Conclusion: 工具文档优化不应采用固定策略，需要根据具体智能体设置进行自适应调整。DocsChisel通过失败轨迹驱动的迭代优化方式，能够有效提升LLM智能体的工具使用性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly rely on external tools to accomplish complex real-world tasks, making tool documentation a critical grounding resource for LLM agents. Existing studies mainly focus on improving the tool-use capabilities of LLM agents, while largely treating tool documentation as a fixed input. Although several recent works attempt to optimize tool documentation through rewriting or compression, little is known about how the information contained in tool documentation affects agent performance across different settings.
  To bridge this gap, we conduct a large-scale empirical study on tool documentation for LLM agents. Our study reveals substantial heterogeneity in the information fields provided by existing tool documentation. Moreover, the effectiveness of different information fields is highly dependent on the task domain, LLM backbone, and agent paradigm, indicating that no fixed tool documentation can consistently generalize across diverse agent settings.
  Motivated by these findings, we propose DocsChisel, an adaptive tool documentation optimization framework for LLM agents. DocsChisel analyzes failed execution traces of a target LLM agent to identify documentation-related issues, and iteratively optimizes tool documentation by adding, removing, and refining information fields for each tool. We evaluate DocsChisel against two state-of-the-art baselines, i.e., EasyTool and DRAFT. Experimental results show that DocsChisel improves the task success rate of LLM agents by 95.89% over the original tool documentation and by 75.15%, on average, over existing baselines, while incurring limited optimization time and token overhead

</details>


### [233] [FlowScout: From Execution Feedback to Reliable Tool-Using Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10039)
*Shuo Hao,You Lu,Bihuan Chen,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowScout是一个执行引导的框架，用于从历史任务求解记录中自动生成集成真实工具调用的智能体工作流，通过挖掘工具协调骨架和蒙特卡洛树搜索优化工作流拓扑，在四个任务域上相比基线方法在工具调用正确性和执行质量上均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量智能体工作流的构建主要依赖人工且需要大量领域专业知识。现有自动生成方法主要产生以LLM为中心的工作流，其中真实工具执行被抽象和模拟，限制了生成工作流的可用性和稳定性。

Method: FlowScout将智能体工作流表示为由LLM节点、工具调用节点和依赖边组成的有向图。首先从历史记录中挖掘公共工具协调骨架构建初始工作流，然后通过蒙特卡洛树搜索结合执行反馈来优化工作流拓扑结构。

Result: 在四个代表性任务域上，与PM4Py、ReAct和AFlow三个基线相比，FlowScout生成的智能体工作流在工具调用正确性上提升至少92.69%，在执行质量上提升至少17.66%，且跨重复运行的性能波动更低。

Conclusion: FlowScout通过执行引导的方式有效解决了现有方法中工具执行被LLM模拟的问题，生成的工作流集成了真实工具调用，在正确性、质量和稳定性方面均优于基线方法。

Abstract: Agentic workflows have become an important abstraction for building reliable LLM-based automation systems by organizing large language models (LLMs), tools, and control logic into explicit execution structures. However, constructing high-quality agentic workflows remains largely manual and requires substantial domain expertise. Recent studies have explored automatic agentic workflow generation from historical task-solving records, but they mainly produce LLM-centric workflows, where real tool executions are abstracted and simulated by LLM nodes, limiting the usability and stability of generated workflows. To address these limitations, we propose FlowScout, an execution-guided framework for generating tool-integrated agentic workflows from historical task-solving records. Specifically, FlowScout represents an agentic workflow as a directed graph composed of LLM nodes, tool-calling nodes, and dependency edges. It first mines a common tool coordination skeleton from historical records to construct an initial workflow, and then refines the workflow topology through Monte Carlo tree search guided by execution feedback. We evaluate FlowScout on four representative task domains and compare it with three baselines, i.e., PM4Py, ReAct and AFlow. Experimental results show that agentic workflows generated by FlowScout improve tool invocation correctness by at least 92.69% and execution quality by at least 17.66% over the baselines, while achieving lower performance variation across repeated runs.

</details>


### [234] [UserToolBench: A User-Profile-Hidden Benchmark for Personalized Decision Making in Tool-Use LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10042)
*Xuexiong Yin,Zechuan Chen,Yongsen Zheng,Yuxiang Zhang,Jingyuan Yang,Bin Wang,Yubin Wang,Keze Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UserToolBench，一个用于评估工具型LLM在个性化决策能力的基准测试，包含10个用户画像、36个工具集、1,065轮对话和170个工具，实验表明当前模型在个性化委托方面仍存在困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的评估基准多关注档案回忆、风格模仿、通用工具使用或响应级别个性化，缺乏对工具型LLM在代理用户进行决策时是否真正理解用户潜在偏好的评估。

Method: 基于隐私清洗后的真实交互轨迹构建基准，结合结构化人物画像、公共API风格工具生态和长周期多轮轨迹，涵盖信息缺失、单工具和多工具三类任务。

Result: 实验表明当前强工具型LLM仍难以完成个性化委托，多工具协调、缺失约束推断和长周期行为一致性是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 个性化评估应从关注输出是否"听起来像用户"转向关注LLM是否为所代表的用户做出正确决策。

Abstract: Tool-use LLMs are increasingly asked to act on users' behalf, but existing benchmarks usually focus on profile recall, style imitation, generic tool use, or response-level personalization. We introduce UserToolBench , a benchmark for personalized decision making in tool-use LLMs. UserToolBench tests whether a model can infer latent user preferences from interaction history, recognize when clarification is needed, and produce user-aligned tool-call trajectories under incomplete information. The benchmark is built from privacy-sanitized real interaction traces and combines structured persona profiles, public API-style tool ecosystems, and long-horizon multi-turn trajectories. It includes 10 user profiles, 36 tool sets, 1,065 turns, 170 unique tools, and evaluation-focused task types covering lack-of-information, single-tool, and multi-tool settings. Experiments with strong tool-use LLMs show that current models still have difficulty with personalized delegation. Multi-tool coordination, missing-constraint inference, and long-horizon behavioral consistency remain major bottlenecks. These results suggest that personalization evaluation should move beyond asking whether outputs sound user-specific and instead ask whether LLMs make correct decisions for the users they represent.

</details>


### [235] [Finding the Signal in the Spam: Jointly Learning Rewards and Worker Reliability from Pairwise Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10045)
*Kaustubh Shivshankar Shejole,Tanish Agarwal,Arpit Agarwal,Avishek Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究从成对比较中学习物品奖励的问题，采用玻尔兹曼-理性模型（扩展了Bradley-Terry-Luce模型），引入Pólya-Gamma隐变量将逻辑似然转化为条件高斯形式，提出EM算法同时学习工人能力和物品奖励，并通过矩阵感知问题获得理论收敛保证。实验证明该方法对垃圾信息发送者和对抗性工人具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 众包平台上的工作者往往因领域知识有限或最大化收益（刷屏）行为而不可靠，现有的成对比较学习方法大多假设工作者完全可靠，忽略了工作者的能力差异。因此需要理解是否可以将工人可靠性（胜任能力）与物品奖励联合学习。

Method: 采用玻尔兹曼-理性模型（扩展BTL模型），引入Pólya-Gamma潜变量将逻辑似然变换为条件高斯形式，由此推导EM算法。该技术将问题转化为矩阵感知问题，进而建立算法的理论收敛性保证。

Result: 在真实和合成数据集上的广泛实验表明，该算法相较于多个基线方法具有优势，并且对垃圾信息发送者和对抗性工作者表现出较强的鲁棒性，验证了其在实际众包和奖励学习场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过同时学习工人能力和物品奖励，所提出的EM算法在实际众包环境中展现了优越的性能和鲁棒性，为解决不可靠工作者场景下的成对比较学习问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: The problem of learning from pairwise comparisons has been widely studied across many domains such as recommendation systems, social choice, and more recently, fine-tuning large language models. In this problem, the goal is to learn item rewards based on pairwise comparisons between them. In many scenarios, these comparisons are elicited from crowdworkers using platforms such as Amazon Mechanical Turk, Scale AI, etc. However, crowdworkers are often unreliable due to limited domain knowledge or revenue-maximizing (spamming) behavior. In this work, our goal is to understand whether worker reliability (competency) can be learned jointly with item rewards. To this end, we adopt the Boltzmann-rational model for pairwise comparisons, which extends the Bradley-Terry-Luce model by incorporating worker competencies. We derive an EM-based algorithm for learning under this model by introducing Polya-Gamma latent variables to transform the logistic likelihood into a conditionally Gaussian form, enabling tractable optimization and leading to a simplified $Q$ function in the E-step of the algorithm. This technique allows us to reduce our formulation to a matrix sensing problem, using which we establish theoretical convergence guarantees for our algorithm. We conduct extensive experiments on real-world and synthetic datasets. These experiments demonstrate the advantages of using our algorithm over several baselines and confirm its strong robustness to both spammers and adversarial workers, highlighting its practical effectiveness in realistic crowdsourcing and reward learning settings. The code and data is publicly available at https://github.com/KaustubhShejole/BoRa_EM.

</details>


### [236] [Detecting Soft Skills in ML Engineering Roles CVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10046)
*Aidin Azamnouri,Nouran Ayad,Justus Bogner,Stefan Wagner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究使用300份经过人工筛选的简历语料库，通过LLM流水线提取求职者明示和隐含的软技能，并针对需求侧文献提出的13个可证伪假设进行效应量检验，其中11个假设获得支持、1个部分支持、1个被否定。研究发现求职者主要通过叙述而非关键词列表披露软技能（比例约3:1），在雇主最看重的领导力、协调和指导能力上叙述占比高达88-96%，且资历越深表述领导力的几率约增三倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于ML团队软技能的研究几乎全部来自需求侧（招聘广告、调查、招聘经理访谈），缺乏对求职者自身如何表述这些能力的分析；且既有简历挖掘工作基于关键词提取，无法识别通过叙述传达的技能，仅作描述性频率统计而不检验群体差异是否超出抽样误差。

Method: 构建包含ML工程师、数据科学家和软件工程师三个角色的300份精选简历平衡语料库；采用基于LLM的提取流水线，区分显式列出的软技能与隐含叙述的软技能，并以人工标注的真值进行验证；将需求侧文献的主张转化为13个可证伪假设，使用家族误差控制下的效应量检验进行假设验证。

Result: 13个假设中11个得到支持、1个部分支持、1个被否定。求职者以叙述而非关键词列表披露软技能的比例约为3:1，在领导力、协调和指导能力上叙述占比达88-96%。资历使表述领导力的几率增加近三倍。软件工程师表述领导力的比率仅为同行的一半。技术型求职者确实会表述软技能，但基于关键词的筛选系统会系统性遗漏这些表述。

Conclusion: 研究表明求职者主要通过叙述方式披露软技能，现有基于关键词的简历筛选方法存在系统性盲区，可能低估技术型候选人的软技能水平。软技能的角色与资历差异显著，需求侧假设总体上可在供给侧得到印证，但需要超越关键词提取的方法才能完整捕捉。

Abstract: Soft skills shape collaboration among ML engineers, data scientists, and software engineers building ML-enabled systems, yet what we know about them comes almost entirely from the demand side. Job advertisements, surveys, and hiring manager interviews capture what employers ask for. How candidates themselves articulate these competencies has not been studied, and existing CV-mining work is both keyword-based, so it cannot see skills conveyed through narrative, and descriptive, reporting frequency rankings without testing whether group differences exceed sampling variation. We close both gaps. Using a balanced corpus of 300 curated CVs spanning the three roles, we extract explicitly listed and implicitly narrated soft skills with an LLM-based pipeline validated against a human-annotated ground truth, a distinction that existing extractors were not designed to make. We then convert the demand-side literature's claims into 13 falsifiable hypotheses about role signatures, seniority progression, and disclosure style, and test them with effect sizes under family-wise error control, so that candidate-side data can corroborate or contradict the demand-side account rather than merely illustrate it. Eleven hypotheses are supported, one partially, and one refuted. Candidates disclose soft skills through narrative rather than keyword lists by roughly three to one, and most so for the competencies employers value most: leadership, coordination, and mentoring (88-96% narrative). Seniority nearly triples the odds of articulating leadership. That competency, assumed universal in prior work, is articulated by software engineers at half the rate of their peers. Technical candidates do articulate soft skills, but a keyword-based screening systematically misses them.

</details>


### [237] [Physics-Informed Machine Learning in Prognostics and Health Management: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10047)
*Christopher Braun,Julian Raible,Marco F. Huber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过系统综述212项研究，提出四类物理信息机器学习（PIML）分类方案（观测偏置、归纳偏置、学习偏置和混合方法），并发现PIML在PHM各任务中持续优于传统基线，但文献严重偏向锂离子电池和轴承，且缺乏通用性设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的机器学习模型在PHM应用中存在泛化能力差、无法推断因果关系和缺乏可解释性等固有局限，物理信息机器学习（PIML）通过将先验物理知识融入ML流程来缓解这些问题，但缺乏系统性的综述来评估其实际效果。

Method: 对212项研究进行系统文献综述，提出四类PIML分类方案（观测偏置、归纳偏置、学习偏置和混合方法），并按PHM任务（诊断、预测等）进行二次分类。

Result: 四类方法均较传统基线表现更优，覆盖多种资产；但文献高度集中于锂离子电池和轴承，且以特定问题解决方案为主导。关于泛化性、因果推断和可解释性等局限的改善声称缺乏充分证据支持。

Conclusion: 物理信息方法在PHM中已提供切实收益，但未来研究应优先关注可迁移的设计模式、集成策略的基准比较，以及轻量、稳健、适用于在线部署的不确定性感知模型。

Abstract: In modern industry, keeping complex systems reliable, safe, and efficient hinges on Prognostics and Health Management (PHM). Machine Learning (ML) has largely driven advancements in diagnostics and prognostics, yet purely data-driven models face inherent limitations, such as poor generalization, an inability to infer causal relationships, and a lack of interpretability. Physics-Informed Machine Learning (PIML) helps mitigate these limitations by incorporating prior physical knowledge directly into the ML pipeline, thereby fostering growing interest in its application to PHM. This work investigates how PIML is being leveraged in the context of PHM through a systematic literature review of 212 studies. The review introduces a four-class classification scheme, consisting of observational bias, inductive bias, learning bias, and hybrid approaches, and further categorizes studies by PHM task. Across all four classes, the reviewed studies consistently demonstrate improved predictive performance over conventional baselines across a broad range of assets, although the literature is heavily skewed toward lithium-ion batteries and bearings, and dominated by problem-specific solutions. Overall, the review indicates that physics-informed approaches already provide tangible benefits, whereas claims of improvements concerning some of the aforementioned limitations lack sufficient supporting evidence. Future research should prioritize transferable design patterns, benchmarks comparing integration strategies, and uncertainty-aware models that are lightweight and robust enough for online deployment in real-world settings.

</details>


### [238] [Observational Policy Ranking for SMB Financial Guidance from Multi-Action Accounting Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10050)
*Shrutendra Harsola,Vignesh Subrahmaniam,Vikas Raturi,Kamalika Das,Xiang Gao,Kratika Gupta,Ruocheng Guo,Padmaja Jonnalagedda,Ananya Pramod,Sricharan Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对中小企业在缺乏随机推荐数据下需要财务指导的问题，本文提出观测性策略排序框架，并引入协变量调整残差策略学习（CAR-PL）方法，在75,508个中小企业月度观测数据上比较了五种方法，发现CAR-PL与T-Learner在增长型指标上统计上无显著差异，但CAR-PL在类别选择上更分散，各KPI指标的最佳方法不同，支持按特定目标进行排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中小企业需要及时的财务指导，但历史会计日志记录的是自我选择的、往往同时发生的商业变化，而非随机化推荐结果，因此需要从观测数据中学习策略排序。

Method: 提出CAR-PL（协变量调整残差策略学习），一种直接在多热日志上操作的action-wise R-learner方法，通过观测支持进行正则化。与T-Learner、保守上下文价值模型、零样本LLM及非个性化基线进行比较，采用模型辅助评分规则在公司不重叠的留出样本上评估。

Result: CAR-PL在毛利上点估计最高（0.084），T-Learner在收入上最高（0.085），上下文价值模型在速动比率上最高（0.062）。CAR-PL与T-Learner在增长KPI上无统计显著差异，但CAR-PL选择33-34个类别且分布更分散。结果对评分规则和参考治疗的替换保持稳健。

Conclusion: 支持从多动作会计日志中进行目标特定（objective-specific）的中小企业财务指导排序，不同KPI适用不同方法。

Abstract: Small and medium-sized businesses need timely financial guidance, yet historical accounting logs record self-selected and often co-occurring business changes rather than randomized recommendations. We formulate this setting as observational policy ranking: from pre-decision financial information, a policy selects one of 34 ledger-derived business-change categories for a target financial KPI. Using 85,078 company-month observations from 7,505 firms, we introduce Covariate-Adjusted Residual Policy Learning (CAR-PL), an action-wise R-learner that operates directly on multi-hot logs and regularizes selection by observational support. We compare CAR-PL with an uplift T-Learner, a conservative contextual value model, a zero-shot LLM, and non-personalized references on company-disjoint held-out firms under a shared model-assisted scoring rule. CAR-PL has the highest Gross Profit point estimate (0.084), the T-Learner has the highest Revenue point estimate (0.085), and the contextual value model has the highest Quick Ratio point estimate (0.062). CAR-PL and the T-Learner are not statistically separated on either growth KPI in matched company-clustered comparisons, while CAR-PL selects 33-34 categories and produces less concentrated selections across the catalog. Outcome-model-only scoring retains the same KPI-level point-estimate leader or top pair, and category rankings remain similar when the all-zero treatment reference is replaced by the most common training co-action pattern. These findings support objective-specific ranking of SMB financial guidance from multi-action accounting logs.

</details>


### [239] [ChronoSSM: Training for Temporally Aware Representations in Autoregressive State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10120)
*Adrien Schoen,Nachiketa Ratnakar Patil,Arjun Bhagoji,Francesco Bronzino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ChronoSSM，一种联合建模事件与时间戳的自回归状态空间模型，通过共享骨干网络和组合生成目标进行训练。实验表明，联合训练相比两阶段方法（先用事件预测学习表示，再冻结表示训练时序模型）能更有效地将时间间隔信息编码进表示中，且不会显著损害事件生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列模型（如Transformer和状态空间模型）通常只关注预测"发生了什么"而忽视"何时发生"，在带显式时间信息的数据挖掘场景中，这种分离会限制时序推理、异常检测和事件时间线的忠实重建。常见的两阶段策略（先用事件表示训练时序模型）隐含假设事件预测的表示已包含足够的时间结构，但这种假设未必成立。

Method: 提出ChronoSSM，一个自回归状态空间模型，使用共享骨干网络联合建模事件与时间戳，通过组合的token生成和时间生成目标进行训练。作者对比了联合训练框架（时间监督更新骨干网络）与两阶段框架（仅在冻结事件表示上学习时序），在四个涵盖密集和部分时间戳监督的领域上进行评估。

Result: 联合训练在所有四个领域中一致地使时间间隔信息更容易从冻结表示中恢复，且没有系统性地损害内容生成质量。结果表明，时间监督可以产生更具时间信息性的表示，而不会明显降低自回归事件建模的性能。

Conclusion: 事件与时间的联合建模是一种有效策略，能够在不牺牲事件生成质量的前提下，提升表示中时间结构的可恢复性，从而更好地支持时序推理、异常检测和事件时间线重建等下游任务。

Abstract: Modern sequence models, from Transformers to State Space Models, have enabled powerful generative modeling across diverse domains, yet they are typically trained to predict what happens while treating when it happens as a secondary concern. In data-mining settings where events are associated with explicit timing information, this separation can limit temporal reasoning, anomaly detection, and faithful reconstruction of event chronology. A common strategy is to treat timing as an auxiliary signal, training a separate timing model using representations learned solely for event prediction. However, this two-stage approach implicitly assumes that representations optimized for event prediction already contain sufficient temporal structure.
  We introduce ChronoSSM, an autoregressive State Space Model (SSM) that jointly models events and timestamps with a shared backbone trained using combined token and temporal generation objectives. We compare the joint regime, where temporal supervision updates the backbone, with the two-stage regime, where timing is learned only using the frozen event representations. Across four domains spanning dense and partial timestamp supervision, joint training consistently makes inter-arrival information more recoverable from frozen representations without any systematic degradation in content-generation quality overall. Our results show that temporal supervision can produce more temporally informative representations without materially degrading autoregressive event modeling.

</details>


### [240] [Procedural Fairness Failures in RLHF from Preference Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10126)
*M P V S Gopinadh,Karthik Kamuju,Kummari Avinash,John Joshua,Srinivasa Raju Rudraraju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出RLHF在偏好异质性场景下存在程序公平性缺失问题，并提出PA-RLHF方法在奖励学习阶段分离不同偏好模式进行优化，在受控实验中将对齐准确率从46.9%提升至67.9%，公平差距从15.9个百分点降至9.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLHF将异质性偏好聚合成单一奖励模型，假设偏好同质，导致多数群体偏好主导奖励学习而少数群体偏好被系统性低估，产生程序性公平失败。

Method: 提出Preference-Aware RLHF (PA-RLHF)，通过在奖励学习阶段将优化过程按不同偏好模式进行分离，保留不同偏好信号的区分度，避免偏好平均化导致的公平性问题。

Result: 在受控实验中，PA-RLHF将整体对齐准确率从46.9%提升至67.9%，并将最优与最差对齐群体之间的公平差距从15.9个百分点降低至9.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 对齐中的程序性公平失败可能源于奖励学习的结构性设计选择，即使在受控无噪声环境中也会出现；偏颇的奖励模型会在顺序决策中累积不平等，对LLM和智能体系统产生直接影响。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) aggregates heterogeneous preferences into a single reward model, assuming preference homogeneity. When preferences are heterogeneous, this aggregation induces a procedural fairness failure where majority preference groups dominate reward learning while minority preferences are systematically under-represented. This work defines procedural fairness in alignment as preserving distinct preference signals during reward modeling and shows that standard RLHF violates this via preference averaging. Preference-Aware RLHF (PA-RLHF) is introduced, separating optimization across preference modes at the reward learning stage. In a controlled setting, PA-RLHF improves overall alignment accuracy from 46.9% to 67.9% and reduces the fairness gap between best and worst aligned groups from 15.9 to 9.6 percentage points. These results show that procedural fairness failures in alignment can arise from structural design choices in reward learning, even in controlled, noise-free settings, with direct implications for large language models and agentic systems, where biased reward models can compound inequities across sequential decisions.

</details>


### [241] [SeFoRA: Sketch-Aggregated Federated Low-Rank Adaptation with Heterogeneous Client Ranks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10144)
*Yue Xia,Tayyebeh Jahani-Nezhad,Mayank Bakshi,Rawad Bitar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SeFoRA，一种基于草图聚合的联邦LoRA（低秩适配）微调算法。客户端传输本地更新的线性草图，使联邦服务器能够直接聚合，缓解了双线性不匹配问题，并在小子空间中完成聚合。同时提出同秩版本SeFoRA-Ho，在秩同质场景下实现直接适配器聚合，证明了收敛到一阶驻点邻域的速度为O(1/T)，并在GLUE数据集上对RoBERTa-Large进行微调实验，展现优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LoRA与联邦参数高效微调（PEFT）结合时，面临单独设置中不存在的挑战：不同客户端可能使用不同的LoRA秩，导致因子矩阵维度不兼容；逐因子平均存在双线性不匹配问题。现有方法无法很好地解决这些联邦场景下的特有难题。

Method: 提出SeFoRA算法，客户端将本地更新的线性草图传输给联邦服务器，实现直接聚合，从而缓解双线性不匹配并允许在模型全量的小子空间中聚合；同时设计同秩版本SeFoRA-Ho，支持该设置下的直接适配器聚合。

Result: 在秩同质设置下证明了以O(1/T)的速率收敛到一阶驻点的邻域；在GLUE数据集上对RoBERTa-Large进行微调实验，结果显示所提算法优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: SeFoRA及SeFoRA-Ho有效解决了联邦LoRA中的维度不兼容与双线性不匹配问题，具备理论收敛保证和实验上的性能优势，为联邦参数高效微调提供了新的有效方案。

Abstract: We consider federated parameter efficient fine-tuning of large neural networks with low-rank adaptation (LoRA,~Hu et al.\ 2022). Combining LoRA with federated PEFT introduces challenges absent from either setting alone: clients may use different LoRA ranks, making their factor matrices dimension-incompatible, and factor-wise averaging suffers from a bilinear mismatch. We propose SeFoRA, a sketch-aggregated federated LoRA algorithm in which each client transmits a linear sketch of its local updates, enabling direct aggregation at the federator.
