TL;DR: 本文提出MOSAIC框架，将模型架构与系统协同设计统一为优化问题，通过耦合预测性缩放定律与校准后的性能模型（估算MFU、通信成本、内存占用及最优并行布局），在稀疏MoE语言模型中证明：在仅考虑计算效率（FLOPs预算）时不存在最优稀疏度，而系统约束下才会涌现最优稀疏度，论证了架构与系统应进行统一协同设计的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练中，算法/架构决策与系统决策被分开处理：缩放定律阶段在计算约束下优化loss，系统阶段再优化硬件效率。这种割裂的流程无法捕捉架构选择与系统效率之间的耦合关系，可能导致次优决策。

Method: MOSAIC将架构与系统协同设计建模为优化问题，耦合两个组件：(1) 预测性缩放定律，拟合稀疏MoE模型在文本数据上的loss，缩放维度包含稀疏因子；(2) 校准后的性能模型，估算MFU、通信成本、内存占用及最优并行布局。实验覆盖从1.04亿到27亿激活参数、总规模达790亿参数的MoE模型。

Result: 在仅考虑模型FLOPs预算（忽略系统效率）时，loss随模型稀疏度增加而单调降低，计算最优位于数据支持的上边界，不存在内部最优稀疏度。然而，在集群系统约束下（如通信、内存、并行布局），最优稀疏度才会出现，说明系统因素塑造了最优架构选择。

Conclusion: 结果是架构与系统割裂优化的传统范式会导致次优选择，前沿语言模型训练应转向统一的架构与系统协同设计框架。

Abstract: In large-scale pretraining, the algorithm, architecture, and systems decisions are conventionally made in disconnected stages. A scaling law stage selects an architecture and training recipe, optimizing loss under compute constraints, and a separate systems stage then optimizes the implementation for hardware efficiency. In this work, we develop MOSAIC, which formulates model architecture and systems co-design as an optimization problem. MOSAIC couples a predictive scaling law with a calibrated performance model that estimates Model FLOPs Utilization (MFU), communication cost, memory footprint, and the best parallel layout. We instantiate the framework for sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models, where expert count, routing sparsity, and other MoE layer dimensions affect both the loss and systems efficiency. We fit a scaling law on sparse MoE models trained on text data, whose scaling dimensions include the sparsity factor, which is the fraction of model parameters inactive per token in a forward pass. The scaling law sweeps in our work span active parameters from $104$ million to $2.7$ billion and total model sizes reaching $79$ billion parameters. We show that, within the calibrated sparsity range, an efficiency-agnostic model-FLOPs budget admits no interior optimal sparsity. The fitted loss decreases monotonically with sparser models and the compute optimum lies at the upper boundary of the data support. An optimal sparsity in MoE models instead emerges under the cluster's systems constraints, as captured by MOSAIC. Our results argue for a shift towards unified architecture and systems co-design for frontier language model training.

</details>


### [277] [Pair-Centric Graph Rewiring for Over-Squashing via Optimal Transport-Guided Communication Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10619)
*Yan Wang,Chuan-Xian Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PairAlign，一个以"对"为中心的图重连框架，通过需求-支持短缺（demand-support shortage）量化图通信中哪些节点对最需要结构支持，并结合最优传输（OT）引导的机制在有限重连预算下协调分配新增边，有效缓解oversquashing问题，在多个消息传递基准上取得改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消息传递神经网络（MPNN）在处理图中远距离节点间信息传递时，局部传播会因有限的结构接口压缩远距离信号，导致oversquashing问题。现有图重连方法大多依赖边级瓶颈分数或图级连通性替代指标，但在有限重连预算下，关键问题是如何识别哪对节点间的通信最需要结构支持，这一问题尚未被显式回答。

Method: PairAlign首先将原始图的结构需求与当前图的有限跳传播支持相结合，用二者的比值作为需求-支持短缺分数，并证明该分数可作为Jacobian短缺的可计算代理。理论揭示了边插入的两面效应：新边既能创造有用通路，也可能稀释现有归一化转移质量。基于此，PairAlign优化短缺分数以偏好能缓解oversquashing的边添加；同时引入最优传输（OT）引导的重连机制，在候选边预算和短缺目标之间进行通信对齐，实现比贪婪局部分配更广泛有效的短缺目标覆盖。

Result: 在标准图基准上，PairAlign在多个消息传递骨干网络上均展现出性能改进，验证了以节点对级别进行"修复"是缓解oversquashing的有效途径。

Conclusion: PairAlign通过将重连问题显式转化为对级需求-支持短缺的优化，并结合最优传输进行预算协调，为缓解oversquashing提供了新的理论依据和实用框架，证明了pair-level repair的有效性。

Abstract: Message-passing neural networks (MPNNs) often struggle when task-relevant information is distributed across distant regions of a graph, since local propagation must compress remote signals through limited structural interfaces. Graph rewiring provides a structural response to over-squashing. Most existing methods rely on edge-level bottleneck scores or graph-level connectivity surrogates. With a limited rewiring budget, the key question is which pairwise communications most need structural support. This paper proposes PairAlign, a pair-centric graph rewiring framework that makes this question explicit through demand-support shortage. Specifically, PairAlign combines original-graph structural demand with current-graph finite-hop propagation support; their ratio highlights interactions whose communication demand is poorly supported by topology, and our theory shows that this score provides a computable proxy for the corresponding Jacobian-based shortage with a pair-level interpretation of over-squashing. Our theory reveals a two-sided effect of edge insertion: a new edge can create useful walks and simultaneously dilute existing normalized transition mass. Guided by this observation, PairAlign optimizes shortage to favor edge additions that alleviate over-squashing. Beyond selecting useful additions, PairAlign further introduces an Optimal Transport-guided rewiring mechanism to coordinate the finite edge budget for pair-level structural compatibility and shortage-target coverage. It formulates communication alignment between the candidate edge budget and the shortage targets, and the theory shows that this allocation covers shortage targets more broadly and effectively than a greedy-local assignment. Experiments on standard graph benchmarks show PairAlign's improvement across message-passing backbones, validating pair-level repair as an effective route for alleviating over-squashing.

</details>


### [278] [ProbGuard: Calibrated Safety Risk Estimation from LLM Output Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10621)
*Xinzhe Huang,Biwu Yao,Kedong Xiu,Mengnan Zhao,Di Wang,Puning Zhao,Tianhang Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProbGuard，这是首个完全概率化的、架构无关的LLM安全护栏方法。它利用LLM早期的输出分布信号来估计和校准安全概率，实现对不安全输出的早期停止。在9个模型-数据集组合中，ProbGuard将平均Brier分数和ECE分别降低了79.6%和71.9%，并在仅观察前10个解码步骤后，将6种越狱攻击的成功率限制在1%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全护栏将安全评估视为确定性分类任务（离散token序列映射到离散安全标签），存在两个局限性：一是安全评估本质上是不确定性问题，尤其在生成早期阶段；二是仅依赖离散token序列丢失了LLM输出分布中丰富的概率信息。因此需要一种利用概率信息的概率化护栏方法。

Method: ProbGuard通过对LLM生成前缀分布进行蒙特卡洛采样来估计持续生成动态的不安全概率，将安全风险形式化为继续生成的不安全概率。然后通过对分布信号和校准后的安全风险进行后训练（post-training），实现对安全概率的估计与校准，从而支持对不安全输出的早期停止。

Result: 在全部9个模型-数据集组合设置中，ProbGuard取得最佳校准性能；与最优基线相比，平均Brier分数降低79.6%，ECE降低71.9%。在仅观察LLM前10个解码步骤的早期输出分布后，ProbGuard将6种代表性越狱攻击的成功率限制在至多1%。

Conclusion: ProbGuard验证了利用LLM输出分布中的概率信息进行安全评估的有效性，相比传统的确定性分类护栏，能够更早、更准确地识别和阻止不安全输出，为LLM安全护栏提供了全新的概率化范式。

Abstract: Recent research on Large Language Model (LLM) safety has widely adopted guardrails to identify unsafe LLM outputs. Existing guardrails typically formulate safety assessment as a deterministic classification task, mapping a discrete token sequence to a discrete safety label. However, this paradigm has two limitations: First, safety assessment is inherently an uncertain problem, particularly during the early generation state. Second, relying solely on discrete token sequences discards the rich probabilistic information embedded in the LLM output distribution. To address these limitations, we propose the first completely probabilistic architecture-agnostic guardrail \textsc{ProbGuard} to leverage the LLM early output distributional signals for estimating and calibrating the safety probability, thereby enabling early stopping of unsafe ongoing outputs. Specifically, given an LLM's generated prefix distribution, we formulate the safety risk as the unsafe probability of its continued generation dynamics and estimate this risk by Monte-Carlo sampling. Through post-training on the distributional signals and calibrated safety risk, \textsc{ProbGuard} achieves the best calibration performance across all nine model--dataset combination settings, reducing the average Brier score and ECE by 79.6\% and 71.9\%, respectively, over the best baseline. \textsc{ProbGuard} further limits the attack success rate to at most 1\% across six representative jailbreak attacks after observing the LLM early output distributions from only the first ten decoding steps.

</details>


### [279] [IADD-TR: Intervention-Aware Dynamics Decoupling with Targeted Regularization for Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10634)
*Zefeng Liang,Jie Qiao,Ruichu Cai,Weilin Chen,Zhifeng Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IADD-TR统一框架，通过干预感知动力学解耦（IADD）和定向正则化（TR）解决基于模型的强化学习中动作与动态纠缠及动作覆盖不均导致的偏差问题，在五个MuJoCo任务上实现了更好的样本效率和竞争性回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MBRL方法将转移模型和critic视为整体预测器，忽视了策略诱导的数据偏差，导致动作与环境演化纠缠，且不均匀的动作覆盖会扭曲反事实价值估计，影响策略改进。

Method: 提出IADD-TR框架：IADD将转移分解为动作干预阶段和无动作自然演化阶段，利用零动作锚点解决两阶段分解的非唯一性；TR则从重放状态策略梯度的有效影响函数推导而来，用动作密度缩放残差修正critic，实现双稳健策略梯度估计。

Result: 在五个MuJoCo任务上的广泛实验表明，IADD-TR在实现竞争性回报的同时具有更优的样本效率。

Conclusion: 通过解耦动作干预与自然演化，并引入双稳健的定向正则化，有效缓解了策略诱导数据偏差对MBRL的影响，提升了样本效率。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL), which learns environment dynamics to generate synthetic experience, is a promising approach to sample-efficient decision making. Numerous methods have been developed to improve dynamics prediction and policy optimization for MBRL through uncertainty estimation, model regularization, and conservative value learning. However, these methods typically treat the transition model and critic as monolithic predictors, overlooking the policy-induced data bias. Consequently, action can become entangled with environmental evolution, while uneven action coverage may distort the counterfactual value estimates used for policy improvement. To address this, we propose IADD-TR, a unified framework combining Intervention-Aware Dynamics Decoupling (IADD) and Targeted Regularization (TR). IADD factorizes transitions into an action-intervention stage and an action-free natural evolution stage, using a zero-action anchor to resolve the non-uniqueness of this two-stage factorization for robust generalization. Its latent and state-aligned components are identifiable up to an invertible within-block transformation and pointwise, respectively. For policy learning, we derive TR from the efficient influence function of a replay-state policy-gradient functional. TR augments the critic with an action-density-scaled residual correction and optimizes a targeted loss, yielding doubly robust policy-gradient estimation when either the critic or the replay action density is consistently specified. Extensive experiments on five MuJoCo tasks show that IADD-TR achieves competitive returns with improved sample efficiency.

</details>


### [280] [Optimize Cheap, Deploy Strong: Cost-Aware Cross-Tier Transfer for Evolutionary Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10694)
*Tal Oved,Roi Pony,Oshri Naparstek,Udi barzelay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过分层模型分级（cheap-tier/target-tier）重构LLM提示词与智能体程序进化搜索的方法，将高流量的回答角色放在最便宜的模型层级运行，仅将稀少的反思/变异操作交给强模型，并利用跨层级迁移在目标层级部署进化得到的提示词。在四个任务和十一个模型上，该方法以5.6-14倍（推理场景25-54倍）的成本降低达到或超过同层级优化效果，且超过96%的搜索token落在最便宜层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM提示词与智能体程序的进化优化（如GEPA）主要受限于适应度评估成本：每次评估都需要在验证集上运行回答LLM，因此评估器的价格层级直接决定了总搜索成本。现有方法将所有角色绑定在同一模型上，导致成本高昂且难以扩展。

Method: 将LLM在进化搜索中扮演的三个角色解耦：将高流量的回答（evaluation）角色运行在最便宜的模型层级，将低频的反思/变异（reflection/variation）操作保留给强模型，并利用跨层级迁移（cross-tier transfer），将廉价层级进化得到的提示词部署到更强的目标模型上。

Result: 在HotpotQA、IFBench、LiveBench-Math、HoVer四个任务和四个模型家族的十一个模型上，该方法匹配或超过同层级优化效果，超过96%的搜索token落在最便宜层级，搜索成本降低5.6-14倍，在推理模型层级（长思维链场景）下成本降低可达25-54倍。

Conclusion: 作者提供了一种成本受控（cost-controlled）的刻画方法，说明了何时廉价层级的搜索可以替代目标层级的搜索，以及何种情况下这种替代会失效。这一框架为LLM智能体搜索提供了显著的成本效率提升。

Abstract: Evolutionary optimization of LLM prompts and agentic programs (e.g., GEPA) is dominated by fitness evaluation: scoring each candidate runs an answering LLM over a validation set, so the evaluator's price tier dictates total search cost. We restructure that search by decoupling the three roles an LLM plays, running the high-volume answering role on the cheapest tier, reserving a strong model for the rare reflection/variation operator, then exploiting upward cross-tier transfer to deploy the cheaply evolved prompt on a stronger target. We contribute a cost-controlled characterization of when cheap-tier search substitutes for target-tier search, and where it fails. Across four tasks (HotpotQA, IFBench, LiveBench-Math, HoVer) and eleven models in four model families, the resulting prompt matches or exceeds same-tier optimization while placing over 96% of search tokens on the cheapest tier, at 5.6-14x lower search cost, rising to 25-54x where reasoning tiers emit long chains of thought on every fitness call.

</details>


### [281] [ProTAGAD: A Foundation Model for TAG Anomaly Detection with Decoupled Topological and Textual Prototypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10699)
*Ziyan Wang,Liwen Wu,Cheng Xie,Song Gao,Zhenli He,Xin Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于文本属性图异常检测的解耦原型基础模型，通过独立建模拓扑原型和文本原型来缓解传统GNN中跨模态耦合导致的异常边界模糊问题，在14个基准数据集上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GNN异常检测器在传播过程中将结构邻接性和文本语义无差别融合，导致跨模态深度耦合，这种纠缠作为噪声放大器掩盖了细微的异常信号，引发"模糊异常边界"问题，使正常与异常的决策边界难以分离。这一问题在图基础模型的跨域泛化场景中尤为突出。

Method: 构建双原型库（decoupled dual prototype banks），分别独立建模结构正常性和语义一致性。拓扑原型负责捕捉结构层面的正常模式，文本原型负责捕捉语义层面的正常模式，通过解耦设计避免异常线索在耦合聚合中被稀释。

Result: 在14个不同基准数据集上的跨域实验表明该方法一致取得SOTA性能。消融研究验证了BAB问题在传统耦合TAG异常检测器中的普遍存在性，并证明解耦原型设计能有效缓解该挑战。

Conclusion: 解耦的拓扑与文本原型设计是解决TAG异常检测中跨模态耦合问题的有效范式，能够显著改善异常边界清晰度并提升跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Text-Attributed Graphs (TAGs), endowed with abundant textual content along with topological structures, have emerged as a versatile backbone for real-world anomaly detection spanning large language model security, social network moderation, and cyber threat identification. Unlike conventional Graph Anomaly Detection (GAD), which relies primarily on structural irregularities, TAG anomaly detection must jointly leverage both topological patterns and fine-grained textual semantics to capture nuanced anomalous behaviors. The current GNN-based anomaly detectors adopt holistic message-passing schemes that indiscriminately fuse structural proximity and textual semantics during propagation, leading to deep cross-modality coupling. This entanglement acts as a noise amplifier, obscuring subtle anomalous signals and directly giving rise to the Blurred-Anomaly-Boundary (BAB) issue by rendering normal-anomalous decision boundaries poorly separable. This challenge is further amplified for graph foundation models that require robust cross-domain generalization. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel foundation model for TAG anomaly detection featuring decoupled topological and textual prototypes. Our framework constructs dual prototype banks to independently model structural normality and semantic consistency, effectively isolating anomaly cues that are otherwise diluted during coupled aggregation. Extensive experiments across 14 diverse benchmark datasets demonstrate that our method consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance in cross-domain settings. Notably, the ablation studies further corroborate the prevalence of the BAB issue in conventional coupled TAG anomaly detectors, and show that our decoupled prototype design effectively mitigates this challenge.

</details>


### [282] [Your LLM, Your Style: Behavioral Mode Axes for LLM Behavioral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10703)
*Haoze Liu,Run Liu,Haiying Xu,Jiahui Han,Siyuan Fang,Siyu Yan,Huiqi Deng,Guanchu Wang,Na Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于情境行为数据（B-data）的框架来研究和控制大语言模型的"行为人格"，构建了涵盖20种行为模式和4种提示语境的3200个对比行为场景，并引入行为模式轴（BMAs）实现对模型行为风格的可控调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM人格研究依赖自我报告问卷，在一人称场景中施测，结果容易受表面措辞选择影响，且与具体模型行为缺乏充分关联，缺乏在真实交互情境中可测量、可控制的人格行为框架。

Method: 构建基于情境行为数据（B-data）的框架，创建3200个对比行为场景，涵盖20种行为模式和4种提示语域，基于BFI-2、DOSPERT和HEXACO等已验证的心理测量学要素；提出行为模式轴（BMAs），即从对比行为轨迹中导出的激活空间方向，分响应导出和思想导出两类进行控制。

Result: LLM表现出稳定且模型特异性的行为画像，同时呈现跨第一人称决策、建议给出和任务执行等语域的迁移变化；基于思想导出的BMAs比基于响应导出的BMAs更能忠实捕捉预期行为机制，提供更干净的情境行为风格控制，且不易发生特质漂移。

Conclusion: LLM类人格倾向不应被理解为抽象的自我报告特质，而应被视为可测量且可控制的行为模式，根植于具体的交互情境中。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly act in interactive settings where their behavioral styles affect user experience, safety, and downstream decision making. Existing LLM personality studies largely rely on self-report questionnaires administered in first-person settings, making the resulting profiles sensitive to surface elicitation choices and poorly grounded in concrete model behavior. In this work, we introduce a situated behavioral-data (B-data) framework for studying and controlling LLM behavioral personality. We construct 3,200 contrastive behavioral scenarios spanning 20 behavioral patterns and four prompt registers, grounded in validated psychometric facets such as BFI-2, DOSPERT, and HEXACO. Using this framework, we find that LLMs exhibit stable and model-specific behavioral profiles, while also revealing register-dependent shifts across first-person decisions, advice-giving, and task execution. We then show that these behavioral patterns can be controlled through Behavioral Mode Axes (BMAs), activation-space directions derived from contrastive behavioral traces. Compared with response-derived BMAs, which are more prone to trait drift, thought-derived BMAs more faithfully capture the intended behavioral mechanism and provide cleaner control over situated behavioral styles. Our results suggest that LLM personality-like tendencies are better understood not as abstract self-report traits, but as measurable and controllable behavioral modes grounded in concrete interaction contexts. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/lhz191/LLM-Behavioral-Personality.

</details>


### [283] [SQuaT: Self-Supervised Knowledge Distillation via Student-Aware Quantized Teacher Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10709)
*HyeonJun Lee,Hyeonsik Jo,Jinwoo Chung,Jangho Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SQuaT框架，通过将学生模型的量化参数应用于量化教师特征，从根本上消除量化感知训练与知识蒸馏结合时的不可约残差下界，实现无标签场景下的高效量化训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际场景中训练标签常因隐私、版权或成本原因不可用，知识蒸馏是常见解决方案。但先前工作将QAT与KD结合时存在根本性局限：教师与学生模型之间的范围不匹配会产生不可达残差，导致蒸馏损失存在不可消除的下界，限制了量化模型的精度。

Method: 提出SQuaT框架，核心思想是在蒸馏过程中使用学生模型的量化参数来量化教师的特征，从而在理论上消除由范围不匹配导致的蒸馏损失下界，实现无标签的量化感知训练。

Result: 在多种设置下的综合实验表明，SQuaT持续优于强基线方法，尤其在极端低位量化（1-bit和2-bit）场景下优势显著；跨多种模型设计选择的评估显示该方法不依赖特定架构假设，具有广泛适用性。

Conclusion: SQuaT作为一种无标签QAT框架，通过学生感知的量化教师特征策略有效解决了现有方法的固有局限，在多样化的架构和量化设置中均表现出色，代码已开源。

Abstract: Quantization-Aware Training (QAT) enables the deployment of quantized models with minimal accuracy degradation. However, in practical scenarios, training labels are often unavailable due to privacy, copyright, or cost constraints. Knowledge Distillation (KD) is a common approach to address this challenge, but we observe that prior work combining QAT with KD suffers from a fundamental limitation: during distillation, the range mismatch between the teacher and the quantized student model induces an unattainable residual, resulting in an irreducible lower bound on the distillation loss. Motivated by this observation, we propose SQuaT (Student-Aware Quantized Teacher Features), a label-free QAT framework with KD that theoretically eliminates this lower bound by applying the student's quantization parameters to quantize the teacher's features during distillation. Through comprehensive experiments across diverse settings, we demonstrate that SQuaT consistently outperforms strong baselines, with particularly pronounced gains in extreme low-bit (e.g., 1- and 2-bit) settings. Furthermore, extensive evaluations across various model design choices show that our approach does not rely on specific architectural assumptions, making it broadly applicable across diverse architectures and quantization settings. The source code is available at https://github.com/lcdbsa522/SQuaT.

</details>


### [284] [Long-Time Trajectory Approximation via SA-NODEs: Model Predictive and Floquet Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10738)
*Ziqian Li,Nikolaos M. Matzakos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究半自主神经常微分方程（SA-NODE）在长时间范围内逼近动力系统的问题。针对单一网络在整个时间域上训练时误差界随时间长度双重指数恶化的问题，提出了两种基于状态重置的训练策略：模型预测策略通过自适应划分窗口并从观测数据重启，实现误差均匀有界；Floquet策略针对具有稳定极限环的自主系统，通过学习返回映射的收缩性保证误差随时间线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经常微分方程在长时间范围内逼近动力系统时，传统单一网络的误差界会随时间长度呈双重指数恶化，这严重限制了SA-NODE在长期预测任务中的实用性。作者旨在开发能够克服这一根本性障碍的训练策略，使网络在长时间范围内保持可控误差。

Method: 提出两种训练策略：(1) 模型预测控制（MPC）式策略——自适应划分时间窗口，每个窗口从观测数据重启训练，当每个窗口达到指定容差时，复合模型误差随时间均匀有界，参数预算与地平线长度呈线性关系；(2) Floquet策略——针对具有稳定极限环的自主目标系统，无需部署时数据，利用学习到的返回映射的验证收缩性将误差限制为随周期数线性增长。此外，针对时间周期架构中标量证书退化的问题，证明了统一的轨道保证及其假设的可验证性，并发现精确周期学习场中存在一个障碍：小单周期误差与收缩的频闪映射不能同时成立。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上进行了数值实验，实验结果验证了所预测的误差规律，并对每个保证的假设进行了量化测量。

Conclusion: 本文通过两种基于状态重置的训练策略有效解决了SA-NODE在长时间逼近中的误差爆炸问题，为长时程动力系统学习的误差可控性提供了理论保证和实验验证。

Abstract: We study the approximation of dynamical systems by semi-autonomous neural ordinary differential equations (SA-NODEs) over long time horizons. For a single network trained on the whole horizon, the available error bound deteriorates double exponentially in the horizon length. We develop two training strategies that avoid this barrier, each built on a reset of the state. The model predictive strategy partitions the horizon adaptively and restarts every window from observed data: when training meets a prescribed tolerance on every window, the composite model meets it uniformly in time, with a parameter budget linear in the horizon for targets with a bounded, uniformly regular reachable tube. The Floquet strategy addresses autonomous targets with a stable limit cycle and uses no data at deployment: a certified contraction of the learned return map confines the error to linear growth in the number of elapsed periods. For the time-periodic architecture we deploy, the scalar certificate degenerates; we prove instead a uniform-in-time orbital guarantee whose hypotheses are measured on the trained model, and an obstruction showing that, for an exactly periodic learned field, small one-period error and a contracting stroboscopic map cannot hold at once. Numerical experiments on four benchmarks confirm the predicted error laws and measure the hypotheses of every guarantee.

</details>


### [285] [Path Integral Value Matching for Linear Quadratic Stochastic Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10777)
*Bangyan Liao,Chenglei Yu,Yuchen Yang,Chuanrui Wang,Zhisheng Song,Peidong Liu,Tailin Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于值函数的路径积分值匹配（PI-VM）算法，通过截断和边缘化标准路径积分公式推导出值函数的时序递归形式，利用时序差分学习和Girsanov定理结合经验重放实现离策略训练，在保持SOTA精度的情况下大幅提升计算效率并缓解模式崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于策略的LQ-SOC方法因依赖全轨迹仿真而面临高昂计算成本和训练不稳定性；标准PIC方法也存在高方差瓶颈，亟需一种更高效、更稳定的求解方案。

Method: 提出从基于策略的方法转向基于值函数的方法，通过截断并边缘化原始路径积分公式，推导出值函数的时序递归形式；基于此构建PI-VM算法，采用时序差分学习近似递归值动态，并整合Girsanov定理与经验重放实现离策略训练。

Result: 在多种SOC基准和采样任务中，PI-VM在低维场景下以数量级的效率提升达到与SOTA方法相当的精度，在高维场景下有效缓解模式崩溃问题。

Conclusion: PI-VM为求解复杂SOC问题提供了一种可扩展的解决方案，兼具高效性与稳定性，代表了LQ-SOC领域从策略优化到值函数学习的重要范式转变。

Abstract: Linear Quadratic Stochastic Optimal Control (LQ-SOC) establishes a fundamental framework for steering noisy dynamical systems and has recently gained renewed interest in the machine learning community. However, current state-of-the-art policy-based methods suffer from prohibitive computational costs and instability due to their heavy reliance on full-trajectory simulation. To overcome these limitations, we propose a paradigm shift toward a value-based approach by revisiting Path Integral Control (PIC). Although standard PIC suffers from the same high-variance bottleneck as policy-based methods, we discover that by truncating and marginalizing the original path integral formulation, we can derive a temporal recursive form of the value function. Building upon this theoretical foundation, we propose the Path Integral Value Matching (PI-VM) algorithm. Specifically, we employ temporal-difference learning to approximate the recursive value dynamics, and further integrate the Girsanov theorem with experience replay to enable off-policy training. We benchmark PI-VM against SOTA policy-based methods across various SOC benchmarks and sampling tasks. Empirical results demonstrate that PI-VM matches SOTA precision with an order-of-magnitude efficiency gain in low-dimensional settings, while effectively mitigating mode collapse in high-dimensional scenarios. Consequently, PI-VM offers a scalable solution for solving complex SOC problems.

</details>


### [286] [MoE Proxy Models for Low-Cost Failure Reproduction and Diagnosis in LLM RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10823)
*Yikai Wang,Chuansai Zhou,Yuhang Zhou,Weiqiang Wu,Cong Wu,Yue Deng,Ben Feng,Mingming Zhu,Beirong Zhou,Zhibin Wang,Sheng Zhong,Chen Tian,Wangze Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了华为昇腾平台上大规模RL训练中的故障类型，提出了一种基于结构保持的聚类专家剪枝的代理模型构建方法，用于低成本复现和辅助诊断RL后训练中的故障。实验表明该方法可将加速器需求降低50%-87.5%，单步NPU-小时成本最高降低33.3倍，同时保持主要训练动态和故障响应一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的RL后训练计算密集且系统管线复杂，调试开销巨大。训练中常因框架适配、数值精度、算子实现等因素导致梯度溢出和损失发散等故障。直接在大模型上复现故障需要大量时间和计算资源，因此需要一种低成本的方法来辅助故障排查与复现。

Method: 首先在华为昇腾平台上系统分析大规模RL训练中的故障，归纳代表性故障类型并识别三个与故障复现相关的模型侧因素。基于这些因素，提出代理模型构建方法：采用结构保持的基于聚类的专家剪枝策略，选择代表性专家，同时保留模型的骨干架构、路由机制和基本任务能力，从而以低成本代理原始模型进行故障复现。

Result: 实验结果显示，代理模型将加速器需求降低了50%-87.5%，每步NPU-小时成本最高减少33.3倍，同时保留了主要的训练动态，并能复现与原始模型一致的故障响应。

Conclusion: 代理模型可以作为RL后训练中故障复现、定向验证和辅助诊断的低成本替代方案，在显著降低计算资源开销的同时保持故障复现的准确性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of large language models (LLMs) is computationally intensive and involves complex system pipelines with substantial debugging overhead. In practice, factors such as framework adaptation, numerical precision, and operator implementation can cause failures, including gradient overflow and loss divergence. Reproducing such failures directly on large models requires considerable time and computational resources. This paper systematically analyzes failures encountered during large-scale RL training on the Huawei Ascend platform, summarizes representative failure types, and identifies three model-side factors relevant to fault reproduction. Based on these factors, we propose a proxy-model construction method for low-cost fault investigation and auxiliary diagnosis. It employs structure-preserving, clustering-based expert pruning to select representative experts while retaining the model's backbone architecture, routing mechanism, and basic task capabilities. Our experimental results show that the proxy models reduce accelerator requirements by 50%-87.5% and achieve up to a 33.3x reduction in per-step NPU-hour cost, while preserving major training dynamics and reproducing fault responses consistent with the original models. Overall, the proxy models can serve as low-cost surrogates for fault reproduction, targeted validation, and auxiliary diagnosis in RL post-training.

</details>


### [287] [TACTICL: Task-Aware Compression of Tabular ICL Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10837)
*Mykhailo Koshil,Matthias Feurer,Katharina Eggensperger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TACTICL框架，通过联合剪枝Transformer层并用轻量适配器替换，实现对表格型上下文学习模型的任务感知压缩，在47个基准数据集上可替换高达85%的层而无显著性能损失，同时保持对数据偏移的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在表格任务上表现优异，但推理成本高昂。现有的模型蒸馏方法虽能减小模型规模和计算需求，却牺牲了上下文适应能力，需要一种既能压缩模型又能保留上下文学习能力的方案。

Method: TACTICL是一种自动化的任务感知压缩框架，核心是联合执行两个操作：剪枝Transformer层并替换为在下游任务上训练的轻量适配器，从而将上下文学习与权重学习相结合，利用模型深度维度的冗余性实现结构压缩与任务适配的统一。

Result: 在47个基准数据集上，TACTICL能够替换高达85%的层而性能无明显下降，且保持对数据偏移的鲁棒性，上下文学习能力完好保留。

Conclusion: TACTICL提供了一个稳健的压缩框架，通过结合任务特定适配与结构化压缩，有效利用表格基础模型在深度维度上的冗余性，在显著降低推理成本的同时保留上下文适应能力。

Abstract: The strong performance of foundation models for tabular tasks comes at substantial inference costs. Distilling models into task-specific architectures reduces model size and computational demands but also sacrifices in-context adaptability. Here we introduce TACTICL, an automated task-aware compression framework for tabular in-context learning models that jointly prunes transformer layers and replaces them with lightweight adapters trained on downstream tasks, thus blending in-context with in-weight learning. We study TACTICL on 47 benchmark datasets and show that we can substitute up to 85% of layers without substantial performance drop on a given downstream task. We further show that TACTICL maintains robustness to data shifts, leaving its in-context ability intact. Overall, TACTICL provides a robust framework for exploiting the depth-wise redundancy of tabular foundation models by combining task-specific adaptation and structured compression. We provide the code at: https://github.com/Hebog/tfm_compression

</details>


### [288] [Diffract: Spectral View of LLM Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10850)
*Nikita Borodin,Maria Krylova,Artem Zabolotnyi,Dmitry Aspisov,Egor Shikov,Nikita Tyuplyaev,Oleg Travkin,Roman Alferov,Dmitry Vinichenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究持续预训练（CPT）作为将通用大语言模型适配到数学、指令、代码和自然文本等专业领域的手段。通过权重矩阵的奇异值分解发现，CPT主要改变奇异向量而非奇异值谱；注意力头投影矩阵分析揭示了强领域依赖的头异质性，据此定义头重要性标准，最多可移除60%的头更新而不损失质量。选择性回退低重要性头到预训练状态可提升基准准确率最高4%。CPT检查点之间线性插值可实现平滑的领域质量插值，并发布Diffract开源工具包用于十亿级参数模型的可扩展频谱分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型需要适配到特定专业领域，持续预训练（CPT）是一种重要机制，但其内部工作机制——尤其是参数空间中发生了什么变化、哪些组件对领域适配真正关键——尚不清晰。理解这些机制有助于提高CPT的效率和效果。

Method: 利用奇异值分解（SVD）分析CPT前后权重矩阵的变化；对注意力头投影矩阵进行异质性分析并定义头重要性标准；通过选择性回退低重要性头到预训练状态来提升性能；使用线性插值研究CPT检查点之间的领域连通性；开发Diffract开源工具包实现可扩展的频谱分析。

Result: CPT几乎不改变奇异值谱，适配主要由奇异向量变化驱动；注意力头呈强领域依赖的异质性，最多60%的头更新可被移除而不损失质量；选择性回退低重要性头可将基准准确率提升最高4%；CPT检查点间线性插值可实现平滑的领域质量插值；发布Diffract工具包支持十亿级参数模型分析。

Conclusion: 持续预训练的本质机制是奇异向量的重定向而非奇异值谱的调整，注意力头的异质性为高效领域适配提供了可操作的裁剪与回退策略，领域连通性表明CPT检查点之间存在平滑的插值路径，这些发现为更高效的领域适配提供了理论和工具支持。

Abstract: We study continual pre-training (CPT) as a mechanism for adapting general-purpose large language models to specialized domains: mathematics, instruction, code, and natural text. Using singular value decomposition of weight matrices, we find that CPT leaves singular value spectra largely invariant, with adaptation driven mainly by changes in singular vectors. An analysis of attention-head projection matrices reveals strong, domain-dependent head heterogeneity, which we exploit to define a head importance criterion: up to 60% of head updates can be removed without measurable quality loss. Selectively rewinding low-importance heads to their pre-trained state improves benchmark accuracy by up to 4% versus the fully trained baseline. Finally, we identify domain connectivity - linear interpolation between CPT checkpoints yields smooth domain-quality interpolation without notable degradation on either domain - and release Diffract, an open-source toolkit for scalable spectral analysis of billion-parameter models.

</details>


### [289] [FiGuRO: Intrinsic Dimension Estimation for Multi-Modal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10857)
*Viktoria Schuster,Sana Tonekaboni,Caroline Uhler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FiGuRO（保真度引导的秩优化）框架，用于在模型容量和超参数约束下估计单模态和多模态数据的本征维度（ID），通过截断奇异值分解和维度增减算法实现共享与私有信息的解耦，无需复杂的辅助损失函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定数据的本征维度（ID）是实现高效、可解释表征学习的基础，但在多模态场景下学习共享和私有信息的解耦表示尤为困难。现有方法存在关键缺陷：通常是静态的、单模态的，或如对比学习方法只能隐式适应共享ID，缺乏动态且多模态的解决方案。

Method: FiGuRO利用截断奇异值分解学习低秩投影的维度，并通过算法决定何时在哪个潜在空间中减少或增加维度。共享与私有信息的解耦作为该优化过程的涌现属性自然产生，无需复杂的辅助损失函数。

Result: 实验表明FiGuRO优于现有的ID估计技术，对超参数变化更稳健。在模拟和真实数据上，FiGuRO成功捕获不同的ID尺度和变化的子空间比例，有效分解共享和私有信息。此外，FiGuRO可应用于现代单模态预训练模型，实现高效的后验多模态表示解耦。

Conclusion: FiGuRO为多模态本征维度估计提供了一种新的动态优化框架，兼具鲁棒性和通用性，并可通过后验方式应用于预训练模型，为高效的多模态表征解耦提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Determining the complexity, or Intrinsic Dimension (ID), of data is fundamental to efficient and interpretable representation learning. This is particularly challenging in multi-modal settings when trying to learn disentangled representations for shared and private information. Existing techniques leave a critical gap: they are often static, uni-modal, or in the case of contrastive methods, adapt only to the shared ID implicitly. We introduce Fidelity-Guided Rank Optimization (FiGuRO), a framework for approximating the ID of uni- and multi-modal data under constraints of model capacity and hyperparameters. FiGuRO learns the dimensions of low-rank projections using truncated singular value decomposition and an algorithm that determines when to reduce or increase dimension and in which latent space. Disentanglement of shared and private information arises as an emergent property of this optimization, eliminating the need for complex auxiliary loss functions. We demonstrate that FiGuRO outperforms existing ID estimation techniques and is more robust to hyperparameter changes. Across simulations and real-world data, FiGuRO captures distinct ID scales and varying subspace ratios, and decomposes shared and private information successfully. Furthermore, we show that FiGuRO can be applied to modern uni-modal pretrained models, enabling efficient, post-hoc disentanglement of multi-modal representations.

</details>


### [290] [Can Bayesian Optimization Efficiently Find a Strong Single Expert in Neural Thickets?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10867)
*Nigel Bastian Cendra,Abdelhamid Ezzerg,Fernando Julio Cendra,Jeremias Knoblauch,Jakob Zeitler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于贝叶斯优化的无梯度后训练方法，在随机线性嵌入的权重空间中利用高斯过程代理模型引导候选评估，以显著减少评估预算，实现更强的单专家模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无梯度后训练方法成本高昂，作者希望探究在适度的评估预算下，结构化搜索能否找到强大的单一专家模型，以降低训练成本。

Method: 利用权重更新位于低维子空间的证据，在权重空间的随机线性嵌入中应用贝叶斯优化，使用高斯过程代理模型引导候选评估，无需反向传播。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Instruct（0.5B至3B参数）的多个推理基准上，贝叶斯优化使用五倍更少的候选评估即可匹配或超越基线方法RandOpt。

Conclusion: 代理引导的搜索能够大幅降低无梯度后训练的评估成本，同时生成更强的可部署单专家模型。

Abstract: Gradient-free post-training has emerged as a compelling alternative to gradient-based optimization for large language models (LLMs), but existing approaches remain costly. We ask whether structured search can identify a strong single expert under a modest evaluation budget. Motivated by evidence that useful weight updates lie in low-dimensional subspaces, we apply Bayesian optimization within a random linear embedding of weight space. Our method requires no backpropagation and uses a Gaussian process surrogate to guide candidate evaluations efficiently. Across several reasoning benchmarks with Qwen2.5-Instruct models from 0.5B to 3B parameters, Bayesian optimization using five times less candidate evaluations matches or exceeds RandOpt. These results show that surrogate-guided search can substantially reduce the evaluation cost of gradient-free post-training while producing stronger deployable single experts.

</details>


### [291] [Optimistic Rates for Multiclass PAC Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10869)
*Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文填补了多分类学习理论中乐观率（optimistic rate）的空白：对于任意固定的预言机风险L*，最优超额风险为Θ̃(√(L*·d_N/n)+d_DS/n)，在所有字母表大小上一致成立，并且该界由不知道L*和置信度的学习器达到。上界通过覆盖-菜单-压缩架构结合新的"面向比较器的相对压缩定理"实现；下界通过校准到L*的pair-Assouad方案和伪立方上的纤维论证实现。两定理均推广到列表学习（list learning）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最坏情况多分类边界在最佳分类器已接近正确时不会变小，缺少乐观率——即波动随预言机风险本身缩放的保证。Natarajan维度和Daniely-Shalev-Shwartz维度的两个端点（可实现情形和不可知情形）的最优超额风险已知，但中间情形开放。本文旨在填补这一空白并推广到列表学习。

Method: 上界：将[CEH+26]的覆盖-菜单-压缩架构与[Pab26]的可实现速率相结合，提出一个新的"比较器面向的相对压缩定理"——大小为k的压缩规则在经验上支配比较器h时，其总体风险至多为L(h)+O(√(L(h)Γ)+Γ)，其中Γ=(k log n+log(1/δ))/n，且无需稳定性假设，从而将二元理论的比较原理推广到多分类标签。下界：使用校准到L*的pair-Assouad方案强制两个项，并通过Natarajan与DS分离的伪立方上的纤维论证，在单个类别和单个分布上同时实现两个下界项。

Result: 在任意固定预言机风险L*下，最优超额风险为Θ̃(√(L*·d_N/n)+d_DS/n)，均匀于字母表大小，由同时不知道L*和置信度的学习器达成。该结果也推广到列表学习：针对最佳r元假设组，同一架构和两个机制产生乐观率和同形状的下界，强制了[Pab26]预期对列表比较器必要的波动项，并从已知可实现列表下界中移除因子r。

Conclusion: 本文闭合了多分类学习中间情形的优化率缺口，建立了完整的乐观率刻画，并推广到列表学习。关键理论贡献在于无需稳定性的比较器面向相对压缩定理，以及在固定L*下同时强制双下界项的证明技术。

Abstract: Worst-case multiclass bounds do not become smaller when the best classifier is already nearly correct: what is missing is an optimistic rate, a guarantee whose fluctuation scales with the oracle risk itself. For a class of Natarajan dimension $d_N$ and Daniely-Shalev-Shwartz dimension $d_{DS}$, the optimal excess risk is known at the two endpoints ($d_{DS}/n$ realizable, $\sqrt{d_N/n}+d_{DS}/n$ agnostic [HMZ24, CEH+26, Pab26]) and open in between. We close the gap: at every fixed oracle risk $L^\star$, the optimal excess risk is $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{L^\star d_N/n}+d_{DS}/n)$, uniformly in the alphabet size, attained by a learner that knows neither $L^\star$ nor the confidence level. The upper bound composes the cover-menu-compression architecture of [CEH+26], at the realizable rate of [Pab26], with a new comparator-facing relative compression theorem: a size-$k$ compression rule that empirically dominates a comparator $h$ has population risk at most $L(h)+O(\sqrt{L(h)Γ}+Γ)$ with $Γ=(k\log n+\log(1/δ))/n$, without stability; this transfers the comparison principle of the sharp binary theory [MQZ26] while discarding its Boolean-cube geometry, which does not lift to multiclass labels. The lower bound forces both terms using one class and one distribution at every fixed $L^\star$, by a pair-Assouad scheme calibrated to $L^\star$ and a fiber argument on the pseudo-cubes underlying the Natarajan-versus-DS separation of [BCD+22]. Both theorems extend to list learning: against the best $r$-tuple of hypotheses, the same architecture and the same two engines yield an optimistic rate and a lower bound of the same shape, forcing the fluctuation term that [Pab26] expected to be necessary against list comparators, and removing the factor $r$ from the known realizable list lower bound.

</details>


### [292] [Benchmarking Time Series Generation Methods for Privacy-Preserving Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10891)
*Luis Amorim,Vitor Cerqueira,Moises Santos,Paulo J. Azevedo,Carlos Soares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过TSTR协议下的基准测试，系统评估了合成时间序列生成方法和基于噪声的匿名化基线在隐私敏感场景中的表现，并提出了隐私增强的Grasynda-P方法。结果显示：现有的生成方法无法完全替代原始数据训练；噪声匿名化隐私最强但预测最差；简单变换方法优于深度生成模型；Grasynda-P位于Pareto前沿，兼顾了预测性能与隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测在隐私敏感领域通常需要在发布数据而非原始数据上训练模型，但合成时间序列生成方法主要面向数据增强场景开发，其在完全替代原始训练数据时的表现及所携带的隐私风险尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建基准测试框架，在"合成训练、真实测试"（TSTR）协议下评估多种合成生成方法及基于噪声的匿名化基线；在7个数据集上联合评估预测性能和基于距离的经验隐私风险，刻画两者间的权衡关系；并提出Grasynda-P，一种面向隐私的图式生成器扩展，融合矩阵集成和核密度估计技术。

Result: （1）没有一种生成方法能够完全替代原始训练数据；（2）基于噪声的匿名化提供最强隐私保护但预测性能最差；（3）简单基于变换的生成器在预测任务上优于深度生成模型；（4）Grasynda-P位于Pareto前沿，预测性能具有竞争力且隐私分离度强于其他生成器。

Conclusion: 本研究为隐私感知的合成时间序列生成方法的评估与发展建立了参考基准，揭示了预测性能与隐私保护之间的权衡关系，并验证了Grasynda-P在两者间取得良好平衡的有效性。

Abstract: Time series forecasting in privacy-sensitive domains often requires training models on released data rather than original observations. Synthetic time series generation has been developed primarily for data augmentation, where generated series supplement the original training set. How well these methods perform when fully replacing the original data - and how much privacy risk the released series carry - remains underexplored. We address this gap through a benchmark evaluating synthetic generation methods and noise-based anonymization baselines under a Train on Synthetic, Test on Real (TSTR) protocol. We jointly assess forecasting performance and distance-based empirical privacy risk across seven datasets, characterizing the trade-off between these objectives. We also introduce Grasynda-P, a privacy-motivated extension of the graph-based generator Grasynda, incorporating matrix ensembling and kernel density estimation. Our results show that: (1) no generation method fully substitutes for original training data; (2) noise-based anonymization yields the strongest privacy but the worst forecasting performance; (3) simple transformation-based generators outperform deep generative models for forecasting in this setting; and (4) Grasynda-P lies on the Pareto frontier, achieving competitive forecasting with stronger privacy separation than other generators. This benchmark establishes a reference point for evaluating and developing new privacy-aware synthetic time series generation methods.

</details>


### [293] [Partially Observable Learning for Multi-Platform Dispatch Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10897)
*Fengming Yao,Man Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出POLO框架，一个面向多平台即时配送系统的部分可观测多智能体强化学习框架，通过平台-网格独立智能体建模、基于注意力的策略表示和反事实奖励塑形机制，在仅依赖本地观测的条件下实现高效的配送调度优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的即时配送平台面临多平台共存、骑手非独占以及平台只能观测自身局部信息的部分可观测环境，而现有调度优化研究大多假设完全可观测性和强制接单，导致在真实多平台场景下性能严重下降。

Method: POLO框架包含三个核心组件：1) 将每个平台-网格对建模为独立智能体，仅基于平台本地观测学习调度策略；2) 引入基于注意力的策略表示，选择性聚合骑手间的不完整异构信息；3) 设计反事实奖励塑形机制，缓解跨网格联合行动导致的非平稳性问题，提升学习的稳定性和可扩展性。

Result: 在自建高保真模拟器上的广泛实验表明，POLO在不同平台数量和系统规模下均一致优于强基线，在平台收入和骑手出行效率方面均取得显著提升，展现出在多平台现实场景中的鲁棒性和有效性。

Conclusion: POLO通过部分可观测的多智能体强化学习设计，有效解决了多平台即时配送调度中的信息隐私约束和异构骑手可观测性问题，为现实多平台场景下的配送调度优化提供了可行且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Instant delivery platforms have become a critical component of urban logistics, increasingly relying on crowdsourced couriers to fulfill highly dynamic orders. In real-world systems, couriers are not exclusive to a single platform and may concurrently serve multiple platforms, while each platform can only observe its own orders and couriers' interactions due to privacy and operational constraints. This results in a multi-platform dispatch environment with inherent partial observability. However, most existing works on dispatch optimization assume full courier observability and mandatory assignment acceptance, causing substantial performance degradation when deployed in realistic multi-platform settings. In this paper, we propose POLO, a partially observable multi-agent reinforcement learning framework for dispatching optimization in multi-platform instant delivery systems. POLO firstly models each platform-grid pair as an independent agent that learns dispatch policies solely from platform-local observations, aligning the learning process with real-world privacy and operational constraints. To support effective decision-making under incomplete and heterogeneous courier information, POLO introduces a novel attention-based policy representation that selectively aggregates inter-courier information. Moreover, we design a counterfactual reward shaping mechanism to mitigate the non-stationarity induced by joint actions across grids, leading to more stable and scalable learning. We develop a high-fidelity simulator to evaluate dispatch performance under varying numbers of platforms and system scales. Extensive experiments demonstrate that POLO consistently outperforms strong baselines in terms of platform revenue and courier travel efficiency, highlighting its robustness and effectiveness in realistic multi-platform settings.

</details>


### [294] [ReOrder-OPD:Reliability-Aware Prompt Ordering for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10905)
*Ximo Zhu,Ruiqi Liu,Rong Wang,Ping Wu,Xiang Zheng,Wenzhuo Xu,Xubin Yao,Zhiyuan Yan,Bo Li,Jun Gao,Xiaolei Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReOrder-OPD方法，通过定义提示级别教师延续可靠性R并设计代理指标来排序提示，从而提升在线蒸馏（OPD）的训练效果。该方法在多个数学和代码生成数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线蒸馏（OPD）中教师对student生成轨迹的监督并非总是可靠，现有方法依赖局部置信度或师生一致性来加权、过滤或截断轨迹，但这些信号无法直接判断教师能否从学生前缀延续到正确答案。此外，轨迹级别的干预可能混淆单次rollout的不可靠性与提示的预期训练价值低下这两个不同问题。

Method: 本文定义了提示级别的教师延续可靠性R（教师从学生前缀到达正确答案的概率，跨前缀和当前学生诱导的轨迹求平均）。由于估计R需要大量教师延续，作者使用学生独立rollout与验证器正确的同提示教师轨迹之间的最大ROUGE-5 F1作为代理。ReOrder-OPD按代理分数对提示排序，然后为标准OPD抽取独立的在线训练轨迹。

Result: Oracle实验表明高R提示带来更大的OPD收益，且降序训练优于随机和升序训练。代理分数在十个等频区间上与平均R呈单调上升关系，说明能有效区分粗粒度可靠性水平。ReOrder-OPD在Qwen3和Gemma4数学设置及Qwen3代码设置中改进了所有匹配的总体比较，且在全部六个FiRe-OPD和ExOPD设置中均有增益。

Conclusion: 提示排序是提升在线蒸馏效果的有效方向，提示级别的教师可靠性排序与轨迹内的监督机制互补，可以共同带来一致的性能提升。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) applies token-level teacher supervision to student-generated trajectories, but this supervision is not always reliable. Existing methods use local confidence or teacher-student agreement to weight, filter, or truncate the sampled trajectory. These signals do not directly determine whether the teacher can continue a student prefix to a correct answer, and trajectory-level interventions can conflate one rollout's unreliability with low expected training value of its prompt. We define prompt-level teacher continuation reliability $R$ as the teacher's probability of reaching a correct answer from a student prefix, averaged over prefixes and trajectories induced by the current student. Oracle experiments show that high-$R$ prompts yield larger OPD gains and that descending-$R$ training outperforms random and ascending orders on a fixed prompt pool. Because estimating $R$ requires many teacher continuations, we use the maximum ROUGE-5 F1 between one independent student rollout and verifier-correct same-prompt teacher trajectories. Across ten equal-frequency bins of this actual score, mean $R$ rises monotonically, showing that the proxy separates coarse reliability levels. ReOrder-OPD sorts prompts by the proxy, then draws independent on-policy training trajectories for vanilla OPD. It improves every matched aggregate comparison across Qwen3 and Gemma4 mathematics settings and Qwen3 code settings. Gains in all six FiRe-OPD and ExOPD settings show that prompt ordering complements within-trajectory supervision.

</details>


### [295] [Physics-informed Diffusion Generative Model for Time-Series Data Synthesis in Dynamic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10941)
*Haiteng Wang,Yunfei Zhu,Tao Wang,Yikang Li,Jiabao Dong,Xiaoge Zhang,Lei Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysDGM，一种逐步物理嵌入的扩散生成模型，用于合成与物理定律一致的时间序列数据。该模型在扩散生成过程的每个反向步骤中嵌入物理定律，保证轨迹级别的物理一致性。通过4.4百万样本（20倍扩展）的大规模AI合成数据集，在34个数据集（涡扇发动机、航空发动机、电池、化工过程）上验证了高保真度，下游任务性能较仅用真实数据提升48%（剩余寿命预测）、15%（健康指标估计）、22%（健康状态评估）、20%（故障诊断），同时训练数据需求量降低10-20倍，并展示了在航空发动机早期故障识别方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业时间序列数据（如航空发动机涡轮温度和转速）对复杂动力系统的健康监测至关重要，但在高温高压等恶劣环境下采集数据成本高、难度大，导致数据稀缺，制约了基于数据的下游任务性能。

Method: PhysDGM将物理定律直接嵌入扩散生成模型的每个反向扩散步骤中，而非仅在最终输出处施加约束，从而保证生成轨迹在每一步都满足物理一致性。

Result: 构建了440万样本（20倍扩展）的AI合成数据集，在34个数据集上验证高保真度；下游任务性能较真实数据提升48%（RUL预测）、15%（健康指标）、22%（SOH评估）、20%（故障诊断）；训练数据需求降低10-20倍；在航空发动机早期故障识别中展现出潜力。

Conclusion: PhysDGM为生成物理一致的工业时间序列提供了坚实基础，推动了物理引导AI在数据稀缺环境（工业机械和复杂化学反应动力学）中的广泛应用。

Abstract: Industrial time-series signals, such as turbine temperature and rotational speed in aero-engines, are essential for monitoring the health and operational status of complex dynamical systems. However, collecting such data is often limited by harsh environments (e.g., high temperature and high pressure) and the high cost of experimental testing. To address this challenge, we introduce PhysDGM, a stepwise physics-embedded diffusion generative model for synthesizing time-series data that are consistent with the underlying physical laws of dynamical systems. PhysDGM embeds physical laws directly into each reverse diffusion step of the generative process, ensuring trajectory-level physical consistency, rather than enforcing constraints only at the final output. A large-scale AI-synthetic dataset (4.4 million samples, 20x scale-up) constructed by PhysDGM demonstrates strong fidelity across 34 datasets spanning turbofan engines, aero-engines, batteries, and chemical processes. After incorporating the synthetic data, the downstream task performance substantially surpassed that using real data alone by 48% for remaining useful life prediction, 15% for health indicator estimation, 22% for state-of-health assessment, and 20% for fault diagnosis. Moreover, it requires 10-20x less training data than existing approaches, substantially reducing the high cost of data collection in dynamical systems. We further demonstrate PhysDGM's potential in identifying early-stage faults in aero-engines by incorporating AI-synthesized data. In summary, PhysDGM provides a solid foundation for generating physically consistent industrial time-series, paving the way for expanding physics-guided AI into diverse data-scarce environments, including both industrial machinery and complex chemical reaction dynamics.

</details>


### [296] [GARLIC: Graph Attention-based Relational Learning of Multivariate Time Series in Intensive Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10969)
*Ruirui Wang,Yanke Li,Manuel Günther,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GARLIC模型，一种用于重症监护室（ICU）不规则采样多变量时序数据的图注意力神经网络架构，通过可学习的指数衰减编码器进行数据插补，利用时滞汇总图捕捉传感器间依赖关系，并通过跨维度序列注意力融合全局模式。模型在三个ICU基准数据集上达到最先进水平，同时提供内置的观测级、信号级和边级可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICU医疗记录是不规则采样的异构多变量时序数据，存在普遍缺失，而临床应用要求预测模型既准确又可解释。现有方法在预测性能和解释能力之间难以兼顾，需要一种既能有效处理缺失数据又能提供透明解释的深度学习模型。

Method: GARLIC模型包含三个核心模块：(1) 可学习指数衰减编码器用于缺失数据插补；(2) 时滞汇总图用于捕捉传感器间的时序依赖关系；(3) 跨维度序列注意力用于融合全局模式与各维度信息。所有注意力权重和图边均端到端学习，提供观测级、信号级和边级的内置解释。训练采用交替解耦优化方案以协调辅助重构与主分类目标。

Result: 在PhysioNet 2012、PhysioNet 2019和MIMIC-III三个ICU基准数据集上，GARLIC在AUROC和AUPRC指标上显著优于最佳基线，计算成本相当。消融实验验证了各模块的贡献；特征移除实验通过单调的性能下降证实了重要性归因的保真度；实时案例研究展示了可操作的预警与透明解释。此外，模型在ICU领域之外的多种时序数据集上也表现出优越性。

Conclusion: GARLIC在不规则采样ICU时序数据的准确预测与可解释诊断方面取得了显著进展，兼顾了预测性能和解释透明度，并验证了其在更广泛时序任务上的泛化能力，为可解释深度学习在临床医疗中的应用提供了新方向。

Abstract: Healthcare data, such as Intensive Care Unit (ICU) records, comprise heterogeneous multivariate time series sampled at irregular intervals with pervasive missingness. However, clinical applications demand predictive models that are both accurate and interpretable. We present our Graph Attention-based Relational Learning for Intensive Care (GARLIC) model, a novel neural network architecture that imputes missing data through a learnable exponential-decay encoder, captures inter-sensor dependencies via time-lagged summary graphs, and fuses global patterns with cross-dimensional sequential attention. All attention weights and graph edges are learned end-to-end to serve as built-in observation-, signal-, and edge-level explanations. To reconcile auxiliary reconstruction and primary classification objectives, we developed an alternating decoupled optimization scheme that stabilizes training. On three ICU benchmarks (PhysioNet 2012 & 2019, MIMIC-III), GARLIC sets the new state of the art in outcome prediction, significantly improving AUROC and AUPRC over best-performing baselines at comparable computational cost. Ablation studies confirm the contribution of each module, and feature-removal trials validate the fidelity of importance attribution through a monotonic performance drop (full > top 50% > random 50% > bottom 50%). Real-time case studies demonstrate actionable risk warnings with transparent explanations, marking a significant advance toward accurate, explainable deep learning for irregularly sampled ICU time series data. Moreover, we demonstrated \proposed{}'s superiority in data imputation and classification on various time-series datasets beyond the ICU domain, showing its generalizability and applicability to broader tasks.

</details>


### [297] [DEFT: Data-Efficient Frequency-domain Top-k Sampling via Inverse Discrete Fourier Transform for Spatiotemporal Dynamical Systems Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11019)
*Hengbo Xiao,Jiale Liu,Jiahao Song,Guannan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DEFT方法，一种基于频域的数据采样方法，通过识别物理系统的主导傅里叶模式并系统地变化其振幅和相位，利用逆离散傅里叶变换生成物理一致的训练数据，用于高效学习PDE控制的时空动力系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDE控制的时空动力系统建模面临两难困境：基于物理的仿真器计算成本高昂，而纯数据驱动的模型往往泛化能力差，难以适应下游动态运行条件。需要一种既高效又物理一致的数据生成方法。

Method: DEFT方法包含以下核心步骤：1）在频域中识别物理系统的主导傅里叶模式；2）系统地变化这些模式的振幅和相位以生成多样化但物理一致的样本；3）通过逆离散傅里叶变换将频域扰动映射回时域生成训练数据。此外，作者推导了该方法的泛化界，并提供了理论上有原则的K（主导模式数量）选择准则。

Result: 三类实验验证了DEFT的有效性：1）在经典PDE求解任务上优于传统方法（系统由少数主导频率成分支配时）；2）在PDEBench的扩散-吸附和Burgers方程上作为数据价值过滤器，数据需求减少40%，预测精度损失小于2%；3）在电池退化PDE系统中，各测试数据集上的R²值超过0.99，且频域特征可迁移至其他电池化学体系（仅需20%微调数据）。

Conclusion: DEFT是一种高效且物理一致的数据采样方法，能够显著降低算子学习的数据需求，同时保持较高的预测精度，并展现出良好的跨体系迁移能力，为PDE驱动的动力系统建模提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Modeling spatiotemporal dynamical systems governed by partial differential equations (PDEs) poses two major challenges: it either requires expensive physics-based simulators that entail iterative numerical solving at high computational cost, or it depends on abundant training data, yet purely data-driven models often generalize poorly to downstream dynamic operating conditions. We propose DEFT, a frequency-domain data sampling method that identifies the dominant Fourier modes of a physical system and systematically varies the corresponding amplitudes and phases to generate physically consistent training data via the inverse discrete Fourier transform. In addition, we derive a generalization bound of this method. We note that it also provides a theoretically principled criterion for selecting $K$. We evaluate the proposed method through three sets of experiments, each targeting a distinct aspect of its utility. First, we validate the framework on canonical PDEs solving demonstrating that it outperforms traditional methods when the system is dominated by a few prominent frequency components. Second, we employ DEFT as a data-value filter on the diffusion--sorption and Burgers equations of PDEBench, showing that it reduces data requirements by $40\%$ while sacrificing less than $2\%$ in predictive accuracy. Third, to evaluate DEFT for more challenging and practically relevant problems, we validate it in the battery degradation PDE system, achieving consistently high predictive accuracy across various test datasets with $R^2$ values exceeding $0.99$. Moreover, the learned frequency-domain features transfer to other battery chemistries with only $20\%$ of the fine-tuning data. These results demonstrate that DEFT is an effective data-sampling method for efficient operator learning.

</details>


### [298] [Derivative Computation in PINNs: Automatic Differentiation, Finite Differences and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11020)
*Maciej J. Mikulski,Tadeusz Uhl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在物理信息神经网络(PINN)中使用有限差分(FD)替代自动微分(AD)进行导数计算的方法。研究表明，在三个基准偏微分方程上，经过适当校准步长的FD方法在精度上与AD相当，但速度更快且GPU内存占用更低；作者还提出了一种随机变体，在平稳问题上优于AD。此外，论文发现对于具有样本间依赖的神经网络架构（如BatchNorm、自注意力），标准的PyTorch自动微分是静默错误的，而FD提供了更接近真实逐样本导数的前向近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动微分是PINN训练中计算导数的主要方式，但它在内存和计算效率上存在瓶颈，尤其是在批处理规模和网络架构复杂度增加时。此外，某些具有样本间依赖的架构在使用PyTorch自动微分时会产生静默的错误结果。因此，探索更高效且正确的替代方案（如有限差分）具有重要的实际价值。

Method: 作者系统地比较了有限差分(FD)与自动微分(AD)在PINN中的导数计算性能。他们在三个基准PDE上进行了实验，通过校准FD的步长来匹配AD的精度，并测试了不同批处理大小下的速度和内存表现。此外，他们还提出了一个随机变体FD方法用于处理平稳问题，并分析了在具有样本间依赖的架构（BatchNorm、自注意力）中FD相对于标准自动微分的优越性。

Result: 实验结果表明，校准好步长的FD在精度上与AD相当，但在所有测试的批处理大小范围内运行速度更快，且GPU内存占用显著更低。随机FD变体在平稳问题上优于AD。对于具有样本间依赖的架构，标准PyTorch自动微分存在静默错误，而FD的近似结果在经验上比标准AD更接近真实逐样本导数（误差小一个数量级）。

Conclusion: 有限差分可以作为PINN中自动微分的高效替代方案，在保持精度的同时提升计算速度和内存效率。对于存在样本间依赖的神经网络架构，FD避免了自动微分在逐样本导数计算上的不可行性，提供了更准确且可行的近似方案。

Abstract: We systematically investigate finite-difference (FD) derivative computation in Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) as an alternative to automatic differentiation (AD). On three benchmark PDEs we show that, with a properly calibrated step size, FD matches AD in accuracy on every problem while running faster across the full tested batch-size range and using substantially less GPU memory, and that a stochastic variant we propose outperforms AD on a stationary problem. We further show that for neural architectures with inter-sample dependencies (e.g. BatchNorm, self-attention) the standard PyTorch autograd idiom is silently incorrect; the correct per-sample alternative is computationally infeasible at PINN-relevant batch sizes, while FD provides a forward-only approximation that is empirically an order of magnitude closer to the true per-sample derivative.

</details>


### [299] [Mapping and Measuring the Behavioral Evolution of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11027)
*Dong Qiao,Chris Ding,Jicong Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三种句子级不相似度度量（对齐均值距离、PCA压缩差异、Gromov-Wasserstein距离），分析32个模型在10,000个提示上的响应行为，揭示模型家族聚类结构、跨代漂移与时间趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准排行榜只反映模型性能高低，无法揭示模型输出行为的相互关系或跨代变化，作者希望从行为层面刻画模型之间的组织与演进模式。

Method: 对32个模型的10,000个提示响应进行嵌入，构建三种互补的句子级不相似度：对齐平均每提示距离（伪度量）、PCA压缩的提示不一致摘要、无对齐的Gromov-Wasserstein几何差异。通过行为图谱、家族漂移、层次聚类、跨家族收敛和响应云离散度分析静态组织与时间变化，并用token级MMD交叉验证。

Result: 模型家族形成连贯聚类，gpt-2为全局离群点；跨家族距离随时间递减；近期推理导向模型响应云更紧凑；句子级与token级度量高度一致（Spearman ρ=0.98）；三种额外编码器（最小为73倍缩小）保持秩几何、离群点与时间趋势符号。

Conclusion: 基于度量的行为分析可揭示超越了性能排名的模型组织规律与演进趋势，且提出了架构无关的充分条件，将行为相似性与提示覆盖、超额对数损失及有效目标分布联系起来，为训练侧差异提供理论视角。

Abstract: Benchmark leaderboards summarize how well a language model performs, but not how its behavior relates to that of other models or changes across generations. We characterize the output behavior of 32 models from six families using their responses to a shared bank of 10{,}000 prompts. After embedding each response, we construct three complementary sentence-level dissimilarities: an aligned mean per-prompt distance, which is a pseudometric on observed model responses; a PCA-compressed summary of prompt-wise disagreement; and an alignment-free Gromov--Wasserstein discrepancy between models' internal response geometries. We use these constructions to study static organization and temporal change on a release-date axis through behavioral maps, family-wise drift, hierarchical clustering, cross-family convergence, and response-cloud dispersion. Across the three constructions, model families form coherent clusters, with \texttt{gpt-2} as a global outlier; cross-family distances decrease over time; and several recent reasoning-oriented models have comparatively compact response clouds. A token-level cross-check based on per-prompt Maximum Mean Discrepancy closely agrees with the sentence-level mean distance (Spearman $ρ=0.98$) and recovers the same qualitative findings. We organize these comparisons through a measure-theoretic lens making their alignment and invariance assumptions explicit. We also establish an architecture-agnostic sufficient condition linking behavioral similarity to inference-prompt coverage, small excess population log-loss, and similar effective target distributions---a possible training-side account rather than an empirical explanation of the observed trends. Our pipeline is label-free, and re-encoding every response with three further encoders---down to one $73\times$ smaller---preserves the rank geometry, the outliers, and the sign of the time trend.

</details>


### [300] [ReRound: Reconstructive Rounding to Resolve Midpoint Ambiguity in Calibration-Free LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11045)
*He-Yen Hsieh,H. T. Kung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReRound是一种后训练量化方法，通过训练条件扩散模型生成低比特权重的连续重建信号，来解决标准RTN量化中接近区间中点权重的舍入方向歧义问题，并在小规模LLM上取得优于RTN和多种免标定方法的量化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RTN量化在权重接近量化区间中点时存在舍入方向的不确定性（midpoint ambiguity），这会影响低比特量化的精度。现有方法要么需要标定数据，要么精度不足，需要一种无需标定且能解决中点歧义问题的量化方法。

Method: ReRound包含三个关键设计：1）训练条件扩散模型生成低比特权重的连续重建，作为舍入方向选择的引导信号；2）引入容忍度（tolerance）指标，根据量化权重与中点的距离决定使用扩散重建还是RTN进行量化；3）通过扫描容忍度参数生成多个候选量化矩阵，选择奇异值分布最接近原始全精度权重的候选，从而确定最优容忍度参数。

Result: ReRound在3比特和4比特权重量化上一致优于标准RTN，超越了广泛的免标定方法，与依赖标定的方法竞争力相当。该方法全离线运行，推理阶段无额外开销，特别适合小规模LLM。

Conclusion: ReRound提出了一种新的低比特量化范式，通过扩散模型重建引导解决中点舍入歧义问题，为低比特量化提供了一条新路径，且方法可推广到LLM之外的AI模型。

Abstract: ReRound (Reconstructive Rounding) is a post-training quantization method that addresses the midpoint ambiguity inherent in standard round-to-nearest (RTN) schemes when quantizing weights near the centers of quantization intervals.
  Starting from a pretrained LLM, ReRound trains a conditional diffusion model to produce continuous reconstructions of low-bit weights for the LLM. These reconstructed weights act as a guidance signal to disambiguate the rounding direction of weights located close to interval midpoints.
  To integrate this reconstruction-guided rounding with conventional RTN, ReRound introduces a tolerance metric measuring how far the quantized weight (not the final quantized integer) is away from the midpoint: quantized weights within a tolerance region around midpoints are quantized using diffusion-based reconstructions, whereas weights closer to quantization boundaries are quantized with RTN. By sweeping the tolerance parameter, ReRound generates multiple candidate quantized integer weight matrices and selects the de-quantized weight matrix candidate whose leading singular values most closely match those of the original full-precision weights. This selected candidate determines the tolerance parameter ReRound uses.
  ReRound is particularly effective for smaller LLMs. Across a range of such models, it consistently outperforms standard RTN for 3-bit and 4-bit weight quantization. ReRound achieves superior accuracy compared to an extensive set of calibration-free methods, remains competitive with calibration-dependent approaches, and operates entirely offline, introducing no additional overhead during low-bit inference.
  The ReRound strategy represents a new approach for low-bit quantization. The method applies to AI models beyond LLMs. This paper focuses on its applications to small LLMs.

</details>


### [301] [Efficient Hypergradient Descent for Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11052)
*Nikita Sevriukov,Anna Barabanova,Uliana Gagarina,Karina Ivanova,Sofiia Kasaeva,Ilya Levin,Marina Sheshukova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Fisher信息矩阵结构的高效逆强化学习双层级优化方法，通过流式频谱草图近似逆Fisher-向量乘积，避免了显式构造大型Fisher矩阵的开销，在离散和连续控制环境中取得了竞争性的策略性能和奖励排序质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将逆强化学习(IRL)建模为双层优化问题时，外层更新需要计算涉及逆Hessian-向量积的超梯度，这在计算上极具挑战性，成为实际应用中的主要瓶颈。

Method: 首先证明在内部最优解处，内层目标的Hessian矩阵与策略的Fisher信息矩阵成比例，从而得到基于Fisher结构化的超梯度（与自然超梯度下降密切相关）。为应对大规模Fisher矩阵的存储和计算瓶颈，采用流式频谱草图（streaming spectral sketch）近似逆Fisher-向量积，避免显式构造Fisher矩阵。

Result: 在离散和连续控制环境中，与一阶随机双层基线相比，该方法展现出竞争性的策略性能和强奖励排序质量；Fisher草图方法降低了曲率存储复杂度，并相比显式Fisher求解器提升了计算效率。

Conclusion: 该研究为IRL双层优化中的超梯度计算提供了理论基础和实用近似方案，通过Fisher信息矩阵的结构性洞察和频谱草图技术，在不牺牲性能的前提下显著缓解了计算瓶颈。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) aims to recover a reward function under which the resulting policy reproduces the behavior observed in expert demonstrations. A natural approach is to formulate IRL as a bilevel optimization problem, in which the inner level corresponds to policy optimization under the learned reward and the outer level measures the discrepancy between the induced policy and expert data. However, this formulation is computationally challenging in practice because the outer update requires a hypergradient involving an inverse-Hessian-vector product for the inner objective. We address this challenge by showing that, at the inner optimum, the Hessian of the inner objective is proportional to the Fisher information matrix of the policy, yielding a structured Fisher-based hypergradient closely related to Natural Hypergradient Descent. To address the resulting scalability bottleneck associated with large Fisher matrices, we approximate the required inverse-Fisher-vector product using a streaming spectral sketch, avoiding explicit construction of the Fisher matrix. We evaluate our approach against a first-order stochastic bilevel baseline across discrete- and continuous-control environments. The results demonstrate competitive policy performance and strong reward-ranking quality, while Fisher sketching reduces curvature-storage complexity and can improve computational efficiency relative to an explicit Fisher solver.

</details>


### [302] [Uncertainty-Aware Deep Learning for Genomics Applications: Insights from an Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11054)
*Sepideh Saran,Mahsa Ghanbari,Uwe Ohler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对深度学习模型在基因组学应用中的不确定性量化（UQ）方法进行了系统性实证分析，对比了深度集成、贝叶斯神经网络和MC-dropout三种方法在序列-活性模型和单细胞表达分析两个应用场景中的表现，并提供了UQ方法在基因组学中的适用性指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度学习模型已成为基因组学应用的标准计算工具，但该领域中不同不确定性估计方法的可靠性缺乏系统性研究。作者希望通过实证分析填补这一空白，为基因组学中的UQ方法选择提供指导。

Method: 在两类基因组学应用（序列-活性模型和单细胞表达分析）上进行系列实验，系统对比深度集成、贝叶斯神经网络和MC-dropout三种UQ方法，评估它们在不同场景下（包括类别不平衡和分布外数据等情况）量化不确定性的能力，并开发了一个系统比较框架。

Result: 贝叶斯神经网络在捕捉由强类别不平衡和分布外数据引起的不确定性方面表现更优，尽管其计算成本较高。此外，不确定性分数可用于筛选蛋白质-RNA相互作用中的高质量预测结果。

Conclusion: 本文提供了基因组学中UQ方法的适用性和可靠性指南，明确了各方法在不同场景下的优势和局限，并展示了不确定性分数在预测质量筛选中的实用价值。

Abstract: Deep learning models have emerged as the standard computational tool for a wide range of applications in genomics. Yet, uncertainty quantification (UQ) -- and more specifically, the reliability of different uncertainty estimates in this domain -- has received little systematic attention. This work presents an empirical analysis of UQ in deep learning models, focusing on genomics applications. In a series of experiments, we contrast Deep Ensembles, Bayesian Neural Networks, and Monte Carlo-dropout methods. We assess their ability to quantify uncertainty in different scenarios, accounting for common dataset characteristics in two genomic application areas and modalities: sequence-to-activity models, and single-cell expression analysis. Our systematic comparison framework provides guidelines for the applicability and reliability of UQ methods in genomics, highlighting their strengths and limitations in different scenarios. We show that Bayesian Neural Networks are better at capturing uncertainty caused by strong class imbalance and out-of-distribution data in genomics, despite their computational disadvantages. Moreover, we show how uncertainty scores can be used to select high-quality predictions in protein-RNA interactions.

</details>


### [303] [Batch Size or Negatives? A Selection Rule for Memory-Constrained Recommender Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11061)
*Artyom Sabitov,Daniil Volkov,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在固定内存预算下，采样softmax训练推荐系统时批量大小n与负样本数k的最优分配问题，理论上证明n最大、k最小的配置收敛最快，并通过合成数据和四个真实推荐基准验证了这一结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模神经推荐系统使用softmax交叉熵训练时，最终分类层占主导内存。采样softmax限制负样本数以减少内存，但在固定预算B=nk下，应优先增加批量大小还是增加负样本数量尚不明确，作者旨在回答这一配置问题。

Method: 作者从理论上分析了固定内存约束下的采样softmax训练收敛性，基于标准光滑性和方差假设，推导出收敛速度与n、k分配的关系，并提出"尽可能多包含样本"（n最大、k最小）的配置规则，同时在合成数据和真实数据集（如MovieLens-20M）上进行实验验证。

Result: 理论分析表明最快收敛来自n~B、k~1的分配（即优先增加批量大小）。实验结果表明该配置比不平衡配置收敛更快、最终推荐质量更好，涵盖四个真实序列推荐基准。

Conclusion: 在固定内存预算下训练推荐系统时，应优先将预算分配给批量大小而非负样本数量。该研究为推荐系统训练中的内存配置提供了理论和实证基础。

Abstract: Large-scale neural recommender systems are typically trained with a softmax cross-entropy objective over the full item vocabulary. For a typical large number of possible items $K$, the final classification layer dominates memory, requiring $O(nK)$ logits and gradients to materialize for a batch of $n$ examples. Sampled softmax reduces this cost by restricting the objective to only $k \ll K$ candidate negative items, resulting in an $O(nk)$ memory. However, for a fixed budget $B = n k$, it remains unclear whether one should prioritize larger batches or the inclusion of more negative items.
  We address this question by analyzing sampled-softmax training under a fixed memory constraint. Under standard smoothness and variance assumptions, our theoretical evidence suggests that the fastest convergence arises from an $ n \sim B, k \sim 1$ allocation. So, an actionable rule is to include as many objects as possible given computational constraints.
  Our theory is supported by controlled synthetic and synthetic and four real sequential recommendation benchmarks, including MovieLens-20M. The suggested configuration achieve faster convergence and better final recommendation quality than imbalanced alternatives within the same memory constraint. These findings provide a theoretical and empirical foundation for configuring memory during the training of recommender systems. Code, reproducibility materials, and all scripts for generating figures are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/LimitedMemoryRule-BBFB

</details>


### [304] [Cross-View Feature Matching: Survey, Benchmarking, and Foundation-Model Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11093)
*Songlin Du,Xiaoyong Lu,Zeyu Wu,Xiaobo Lu,Guobao Xiao,Bin Fan,Jiayi Ma,Takeshi Ikenaga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对跨视角特征匹配领域进行了系统综述，提出统一分类体系涵盖特征提取、单类型/多类型特征匹配器、视觉基础模型方法、训练策略与鲁棒估计等维度，并在统一协议下对代表性方法进行基准测试，最后讨论效率、极端条件鲁棒性及跨域泛化等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨视角特征匹配领域在过去十年经历了从任务特定模型到统一可泛化模型的重要演变，但由于现有研究在问题定义、模型架构、训练范式和评估协议上高度多样化，缺乏统一理解，阻碍了领域整体发展的清晰认知与比较。

Method: 提出结构化分类体系，涵盖特征提取、单类型特征匹配器、多类型特征匹配器、基于视觉基础模型的方法、训练策略和鲁棒估计六大方面；随后梳理近期进展并提炼关键设计原则；最后在一致协议下对代表性先进方法进行统一实验基准测试。

Result: 构建了统一的分析与比较框架，完成了跨视角特征匹配方法的系统分类，在统一评估协议下实现了代表性方法的公平对比，并识别出领域向统一可泛化对应模型发展的关键趋势。

Conclusion: 该综述为理解视觉基础模型时代下跨视角特征匹配的演进历程、当前格局和未来发展方向提供了全面且结构化的参考，并指出效率、极端条件鲁棒性和跨域泛化是未来研究的关键方向。

Abstract: Cross-view feature matching aims to establish reliable correspondences across images with large viewpoint variations. Over the past decade, the field has evolved from task-specific models toward increasingly unified and generalizable correspondence models, with recent progress further driven by the emergence of vision foundation models (VFMs). Despite these advances, existing studies remain highly diverse in their problem formulations, model architectures, training paradigms, and evaluation protocols, making it difficult to obtain a unified understanding of the field. In this survey, we present a unified review of cross-view feature matching. We first introduce a structured taxonomy covering feature extraction, single-type feature matcher, multi-type feature matcher, VFMs based methods, training strategy and robust estimation, providing a coherent framework for analysis and comparison. We further examine recent advances, distilling key design principles and highlighting the shift toward unified and generalizable correspondence models. We also provide a unified experimental benchmarking of representative state-of-the-art methods under consistent protocols, enabling fair and comprehensive performance comparisons. In addition, we discuss open challenges and future directions, including efficiency, robustness under extreme conditions, and cross-domain generalization. This survey aims to provide a comprehensive and structured reference for understanding the evolution, current landscape, and future development of cross-view feature matching in the era of vision foundation models.

</details>


### [305] [Two-stage Odd Residual Flows for Mean-Preserving Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11114)
*Kiran Madhusudhanan,Christian Klötergens,Lars Schmidt-Thieme,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TORF框架，通过两阶段方法将均值预测与不确定性估计解耦，第一阶段用预训练确定性模型产生准确均值，第二阶段用严格奇函数的受限归一化流学习残差分布，在保持均值准确性的同时提供灵活的概率密度估计，在长短时域预测中均取得了SOTA的确定性精度（NMAE）和密度估计性能（CRPS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率预测方法在分布灵活性与均值预测准确性之间存在根本性权衡：传统参数化方法（如MVE）在联合NLL目标下训练会导致点预测精度下降，而现代灵活生成模型（如归一化流、扩散模型）依赖昂贵的蒙特卡洛采样且均值估计欠优。

Method: 提出两阶段奇残差流（TORF）框架：第一阶段使用预训练的确定性模型生成准确的均值预测；第二阶段采用严格奇函数的受限归一化流来学习围绕点预测的灵活残差分布，从而在无需采样的条件下保证第一阶段的均值保持不变。

Result: 实验表明TORF在短时域和长时域预测中均实现了最先进的确定性精度（NMAE），同时提供了强健的密度估计性能（CRPS）。

Conclusion: TORF通过解耦均值预测与不确定性建模，成功解决了概率预测中分布灵活性与点预测精度难以兼得的矛盾，为长时域风险敏感决策提供了更可靠的预测框架。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting plays an essential role in risk-sensitive decision-making, particularly in long-horizon settings. However, existing approaches often face a fundamental trade-off between distributional flexibility and accurate mean prediction. Traditional parametric methods, such as Mean Variance Estimation (MVE), can suffer from degraded point accuracy when trained under joint Negative Log-Likelihood (NLL) objectives, while modern-flexible generative models, including Normalizing Flows and Diffusion Models, typically rely on costly Monte Carlo sampling and may yield suboptimal mean estimates. To address this limitation, we propose Two-stage Odd Residual Flows (TORF), a framework that decouples mean forecasting from uncertainty estimation. In the first stage, a pre-trained deterministic model is used to produce an accurate mean prediction. In the second stage, a Restricted Normalizing Flow, with strictly odd functions learns flexible residual distributions around the point forecast, guaranteeing mean preservation from the first stage without sampling. Experiments show that TORF achieves state-of-the-art deterministic accuracy (NMAE) while providing strong density estimation performance (CRPS) on short and long-horizon forecasting.

</details>


### [306] [A Recommendation System Approach for Interference-Robust Sensor Subset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11143)
*Kaan Buyukkalayci,Kyle Pak,Merve Karakas,Christina Fragouli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于推荐系统思想的传感器子集选择方法，利用频带声学特征和双塔多层感知机（MLP）架构来替代传统的RSSI方法，在室外车辆跟踪部署中相比RSSI基线提升了约20%的准确性，同时保持实时选择性感知所需的低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本声学RSSI测量虽可用于推荐传感器子集，但易受声学干扰影响，导致跟踪精度下降，需要更鲁棒且高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出一种受推荐系统启发的框架，利用频带声学特征替代RSSI信号，并采用双塔多层感知机（Two-Tower MLP）架构对各候选传感器子集进行高效评分。

Result: 在室外车辆跟踪部署实验中，该方法相比RSSI基线将跟踪精度提升约20%，且保持了实时选择性感知所需的低计算开销。

Conclusion: 基于频带特征和双塔MLP的推荐框架能有效克服RSSI方法对声学干扰敏感的缺陷，实现更准确且计算高效的传感器子集选择。

Abstract: This paper develops a method for sensor-subset selection for tracking. Prior work showed that low-cost acoustic Received Signal Strength Indicator (RSSI) measurements can be used to recommend subsets of sensor nodes whose expensive sensing modalities, such as cameras, can achieve high tracking accuracy. While efficient, RSSI-based approaches are challenged by acoustic interference. We propose a recommendation-system-inspired framework that instead leverages frequency-band acoustic features and a Two-Tower Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) architecture to efficiently score candidate sensor subsets. Experimental results on outdoor vehicle-tracking deployments show that the proposed method can improve accuracy by around 20\% over the RSSI baseline while maintaining the low computational overhead required for real-time selective sensing.

</details>


### [307] [DACRI: Decision-Aware Causal Intervention Ranking for Critical Supply Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11154)
*Shiqi Huang,Jiani He,Dingyan Shang,Yihua Xu,Jize Li,Yan Lyu,Lashimi Muraleedharan Nair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CriticalSCM-Bench v1，一个具有因果真值、事实/反事实回放和显式净价值目标的受控合成基准，用于评估供应链中断干预选择。实验发现LambdaMART在部分场景下优于静态基准（提升5.7-16.2%），但数字基础设施场景中领域知识的常量缓冲策略仍更强，显示模型复杂度并非总是合理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的供应链中断研究多聚焦于检测或归因，但检测到中断与选择能最大化可恢复净价值的干预措施是两个不同的任务。作者旨在弥补评估干预选择方法时缺乏具备因果真值、可反事实验证和明确经济目标的受控基准这一空白。

Method: 构建CriticalSCM-Bench v1合成基准，包含因果结构真值、成对的事实/反事实回放轨迹和显式净价值目标函数。在此基准上对比全信息训练选择的静态基准与LambdaMART自适应排序方法，并针对半导体、关键材料、数字基础设施等不同领域原型进行测试，同时涵盖部分/延迟信息设置、压力测试和解释稳定性研究。

Result: LambdaMART在半导体和关键材料原型中显著提升归一化净价值（5.7-16.2%），但在数字基础设施中输给领域信息的常量缓冲策略。LambdaMART在部分/延迟设置下保留全量信息的33-75%价值。压力测试显示干预保真度、时机、成本和未见中断会改变策略排序，关键材料在外分布场景中保持最弱。540次生成的解释研究中，确定性验证和模板回退可保留所有固定干预决策，但措辞不稳定。

Conclusion: 受控实验识别出两类场景：自适应排序能增加价值的场景，以及更简单的结构性策略仍然更优的场景。这提示供应链干预选择应对模型复杂度与实际价值收益进行权衡，而非盲目追求更复杂的模型。

Abstract: Detecting or attributing a supply-chain disruption is not the same as selecting the intervention that maximizes recoverable net value. We present CriticalSCM-Bench v1, a controlled synthetic benchmark with causal ground truth, paired factual/counterfactual rollouts, and an explicit net-value objective. Relative to a full-information train-selected static benchmark, LambdaMART improves median normalized net value by 5.7--16.2\%, with paired statistical support on the semiconductor and critical-material archetypes but not on digital infrastructure. On digital infrastructure, a domain-informed constant-buffer policy remains stronger, showing that greater model complexity is not uniformly justified. Across partial and delayed settings, LambdaMART retains 33--75\% of full-clamp value. Stress tests further show that intervention fidelity, timing, cost, and held-out disruptions can alter policy ordering. Critical materials show the weakest out-of-distribution retention. Separately, a guarded explanation study over 540 generations preserves every fixed intervention decision after deterministic validation and template fallback, although exact wording remains unstable. Within this controlled setting, the results identify regimes in which adaptive ranking adds value and those in which simpler structural policies remain preferable.

</details>


### [308] [The Gaussian-Multinoulli Restricted Boltzmann Machine: A Potts Model Extension of the GRBM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11635)
*Nikhil Kapasi,Mohamed Elfouly,William Whitehead,Luke Theogarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出高斯-穆利诺伊受限玻尔兹曼机（GM-RBM），将GB-RBM的二元隐藏单元扩展为q状态类别（Potts）单元，以支持多值离散概念的表征。在容量匹配和参数匹配两种设置下，GM-RBM在类比回忆和结构化记忆基准上达到与GB-RBM相当甚至更优的性能，且训练成本相当，仅需吉布斯更新即可实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多现实任务（如联想记忆、符号推理）受益于离散化、结构化的表征，而标准连续潜变量模型难以表达这种结构。现有的高斯-伯努利RBM使用二元隐藏单元，其潜状态空间有限，难以表达多值概念，因此需要一种支持多类别离散潜在单元的生成模型。

Method: 将GB-RBM中的二元隐藏单元替换为q状态类别（Potts）单元，构建GM-RBM。提供能量函数、条件分布和学习规则的完整推导。采用对比散度（CD）配合温度退火和槽内多样性约束来避免状态坍缩。在容量匹配和参数匹配两种实验设置下与GB-RBM进行公平对比。

Result: 在类比回忆和结构化记忆基准上，GM-RBM在等容量条件下达到与GB-RBM相当甚至更优的召回性能，训练成本相当，且仅使用吉布斯更新即可。q值离散化公式还便于高效实现。

Conclusion: 研究结果阐明了在可处理的RBM框架内，类别隐藏单元相比二元潜变量为离散推理提供了一种简单、可扩展的替代方案，并明确了其在何种场景下具有优势。

Abstract: Many real-world tasks, from associative memory to symbolic reasoning, benefit from discrete, structured representations that standard continuous latent models can struggle to express. We introduce the Gaussian-Multinoulli Restricted Boltzmann Machine (GM-RBM), a generative energy-based model that extends the Gaussian-Bernoulli RBM (GB-RBM) by replacing binary hidden units with q-state categorical (Potts) units, yielding a richer latent state space for multivalued concepts. We provide a self-contained derivation of the energy, conditional distributions, and learning rules, and detail practical training choices (contrastive divergence with temperature annealing and intra-slot diversity constraints) that avoid state collapse. To separate architectural effects from sheer latent capacity, we evaluate under both capacity-matched and parameter-matched setups, comparing GM-RBM with GB-RBM configured to have the same number of possible latent assignments. On analogical recall and structured memory benchmarks, GM-RBM achieves competitive, and in several regimes improved, recall at equal capacity with comparable training cost, despite using only Gibbs updates. The discrete q-ary formulation is also amenable to efficient implementation. These results clarify when categorical hidden units provide a simple, scalable alternative to binary latents for discrete inference within tractable RBMs.

</details>


### [309] [Hierarchical Empirical-Bayes Naive Bayes: Minimax Smoothing and Calibration with AODE Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11162)
*Nguyen Thai Anh,Truong Viet Vu,Tran Thien Thanh,Vo Nguyen Quoc Bao,Ngo Hoang Tu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出层级经验贝叶斯朴素贝叶斯（HEB-NB）方法，通过Type-II最大似然自适应学习Dirichlet先验的集中度参数，替代传统固定强度平滑规则（如Laplace、Lidstone等），解决了高基数表格数据上固定平滑导致的不消失偏差问题。理论证明了ℓ1误差界与Laplace的严格分离，实证在31个基准数据集上取得最佳平均Friedman排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统朴素贝叶斯分类器的平滑规则（Laplace、Lidstone、K-T和m估计）都使用固定平滑强度，忽略了特征基数、样本量和类别不平衡等因素，导致在高基数表格数据上产生不消失的偏差，分类性能受限。

Method: 提出HEB-NB：为每个类-特征条件概率赋予Dirichlet先验，其集中度参数通过Type-II最大似然从数据中自适应学习，实现跨类别的信息共享且保持闭式推断。进一步扩展为HEB-AODE（平均单依赖估计器），证明自适应平滑可迁移至结构松弛模型。理论分析包括非渐近ℓ1误差界、Laplace紧下界、超额贝叶斯风险界和top-1 ECE推论。

Result: 在31个UCI和OpenML基准上，HEB-NB在概率指标上取得最佳平均Friedman排名，高基数数据集上log-loss最多降低22.1%；HEB-AODE一致优于标准AODE。结合互信息加权后，top-1 ECE降低41%-70%。

Conclusion: 数据自适应的经验贝叶斯平滑显著优于固定平滑规则，在概率准确性和校准两方面均带来实质性提升，且方法具有良好的理论保证和可扩展性。

Abstract: The Naive Bayes (NB) classifier remains a standard choice for categorical data, yet its widely used smoothing rules, such as Laplace, Lidstone, Krichevsky-Trofimov, and the $m$-estimate, all prescribe a fixed smoothing strength that ignores feature cardinality, sample size, and class imbalance, inducing a non-vanishing bias on modern high-cardinality tabular data. We propose hierarchical empirical-Bayes Naive Bayes (HEB-NB), in which each class-feature conditional probability is smoothed by a Dirichlet prior whose concentration is learned data-adaptively via Type-II maximum likelihood, enabling principled information sharing across classes while retaining closed-form inference. We further introduce HEB average one-dependence estimators (HEB-AODE), showing that the adaptive smoothing transfers cleanly to structural relaxations of NB. Theoretically, we establish a non-asymptotic $\ell_1$ error bound for HEB-NB matching the empirical-distribution minimax rate plus a vanishing data-adaptive bias, together with a matching Laplace-tight lower bound that yields a finite-sample, risk-level strict separation from Laplace. We further derive a plug-in excess Bayes-risk bound via total-variation tensorization and a population top-1 expected calibration error (ECE) corollary. Empirically, across 31 UCI and OpenML benchmarks, HEB-NB attains the best average Friedman rank on probabilistic metrics, with up to 22.1% log-loss reductions on high-cardinality datasets and consistent improvements of HEB-AODE over vanilla AODE. Combining HEB-NB with mutual-information weighting reduces top-1 ECE by 41%-70%, demonstrating substantial gains in probabilistic accuracy and calibration.

</details>


### [310] [Beyond a Bag of Features: Set-Level Instability in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11197)
*Nikolai Bolik,Lennart Stöpler,Artur Andrzejak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过稀疏自动编码器（SAE）激活潜在变量集合的重叠度作为相似度度量，重新审视了LLM表征与人类概念范畴之间的关系，发现SAE激活集并未比密集嵌入更好地恢复人类范畴边界或典型性结构，且概念变化与SAE激活集变化之间存在显著不匹配，表明SAE特征在非理想化场景下不遵循简单的词袋语义组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究（Shani et al., 2026）使用余弦相似度表明LLM表征能恢复人类概念范畴边界但无法反映细粒度典型性结构。本文旨在使用更具可解释性的SAE激活集合重叠度量重新审视这一结论，探究SAE语义特征是否能更好地对齐人类概念认知。

Method: 使用SAE激活潜在变量集合的重叠作为相似度度量。首先在受控玩具模型中验证该集合级度量的有效性（能否恢复并集式组合结构、在自然文本中诱导语义连贯邻域），然后将其应用于人类概念分析，并与密集嵌入和残差流状态进行对比，最后在受控语义修改下研究激活集的变化与人类概念变化判断之间的关系。

Result: SAE激活集并未比密集嵌入或残差流状态更忠实地恢复人类范畴边界或范畴内典型性，而是更倾向于反映模型内部的相似性结构。在受控语义修改实验中，SAE激活集的变化与人类对概念变化的判断存在显著不匹配。

Conclusion: 该发现表明，在理想化设置之外，SAE特征并不通过简单的词袋语义进行组合，这质疑了SAE稀疏特征作为人类概念语义可解释表示的可靠性，提示需要对SAE特征的组合语义进行更深入的理解和建模。

Abstract: Shani et al. (2026) show that LLM representations broadly recover human category boundaries, while failing to reflect fine-grained typicality structure. Their analysis uses cosine similarity over dense model representations. We revisit their approach using overlap over active sparse autoencoder (SAE) latent sets as a more interpretable similarity measure. We first verify that this set-level measure is meaningful: SAE latent sets can recover union-like compositional structure in controlled toy models and induce semantically coherent neighborhoods in natural text. Extending the human-concepts analysis to SAE set similarities, we find that SAE activation sets do not recover human category boundaries or within-category typicality more faithfully than dense embeddings or residual-stream states, but instead track model-internal similarity structure. To probe this gap further, we study active latent sets under well-controlled semantic modifications, revealing a substantial mismatch between human judgements of conceptual change and change in the SAE active set. We interpret this as evidence that, outside idealised settings, SAE features do not compose via simple bag-of-features semantics.

</details>
